+import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
+import { Link, useLocation, useNavigate, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
+import {
+  Search,
+  X,
+  Zap,
+  FileSpreadsheet,
+  Flame,
+  LayoutGrid,
+  Clock,
+  Trash2,
+  Plus,
+  ChevronRight,
+  PackageOpen,
+  Loader2,
+} from 'lucide-react';
+import { api, type Product } from '../../lib/api';
+import { useCart } from '../../contexts/CartContext';
+import { useLanguage } from '../../contexts/LanguageContext';
+import { useStoreHome } from '../../hooks/useStoreHome';
+import { searchProducts } from '../../lib/productSearch';
+import ProductThumbnail from '../products/ProductThumbnail';
+
+const RECENT_SEARCHES_KEY = 'tps1_recent_searches_v1';
+const MAX_RECENT_SEARCHES = 8;
+
+const TRENDING_KEYWORDS = [
+  'Ba rọi heo',
+  'Cá nục',
+  'Trứng gà',
+  'Cà chua',
+  'Chanh',
+  'Hành tây',
+  'Cà rốt',
+  'Dưa leo',
+  'Hành lá',
+  'Xà lách',
+  'Dầu ăn',
+  'Đậu hũ',
+];
+
+function getStoredRecentSearches(): string[] {
+  try {
+    const raw = localStorage.getItem(RECENT_SEARCHES_KEY);
+    if (!raw) return [];
+    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
+    return Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed.filter((s): s is string => typeof s === 'string' && Boolean(s.trim())) : [];
+  } catch {
+    return [];
+  }
+}
+
+function money(v: number) {
+  return new Intl.NumberFormat('vi-VN').format(Number(v) || 0) + 'đ';
+}
+
+interface SmartSearchBoxProps {
+  className?: string;
+}
+
+export default function SmartSearchBox({ className = '' }: SmartSearchBoxProps) {
+  const navigate = useNavigate();
+  const location = useLocation();
+  const [params] = useSearchParams();
+  const { t, productName, productUnit } = useLanguage();
+  const { addItem, quantityOf } = useCart();
+  const { data: home } = useStoreHome();
+
+  const isQuickOrderPage = location.pathname === '/dat-hang';
+  const isSearchPage = location.pathname === '/tim-kiem';
+
+  const [value, setValue] = useState(
+    isSearchPage || isQuickOrderPage ? params.get('q') || '' : ''
+  );
+  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
+  const [recentSearches, setRecentSearches] = useState<string[]>([]);
+  const [liveProducts, setLiveProducts] = useState<Product[]>([]);
+  const [isSearching, setIsSearching] = useState(false);
+
+  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
+  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
+
+  // Đồng bộ giá trị input khi url thay đổi
+  useEffect(() => {
+    if (isSearchPage || isQuickOrderPage) {
+      setValue(params.get('q') || '');
+    } else {
+      setValue('');
+    }
+  }, [location.pathname, params, isSearchPage, isQuickOrderPage]);
+
+  // Nạp lịch sử tìm kiếm khi mở popover
+  useEffect(() => {
+    if (isOpen) {
+      setRecentSearches(getStoredRecentSearches());
+    }
+  }, [isOpen]);
+
+  // Click ngoài đóng popover
+  useEffect(() => {
+    function handleClickOutside(e: MouseEvent) {
+      if (containerRef.current && !containerRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
+        setIsOpen(false);
+      }
+    }
+    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
+    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
+  }, []);
+
+  // Debounced Live Search khi value có nội dung
+  useEffect(() => {
+    const query = value.trim();
+    if (!query || !isOpen) {
+      setLiveProducts([]);
+      setIsSearching(false);
+      return;
+    }
+
+    setIsSearching(true);
+    const controller = new AbortController();
+    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
+      api
+        .products({ search: query, page: 0 }, controller.signal)
+        .then((res) => {
+          const raw = res.products || [];
+          const strict = searchProducts(
+            raw,
+            query,
+            (p) => ({ text: `${p.sku || ''} ${p.name} ${t(p.name)} ${p.category || ''}`, name: t(p.name) }),
+            (p) => Boolean(p.thumbUrl || p.imageUrl),
+            20
+          );
+          setLiveProducts(strict.length > 0 ? strict : raw);
+        })
+        .catch((err) => {
+          if (err.name !== 'AbortError') {
+            setLiveProducts([]);
+          }
+        })
+        .finally(() => {
+          setIsSearching(false);
+        });
+    }, 180);
+
+    return () => {
+      clearTimeout(timer);
+      controller.abort();
+    };
+  }, [value, isOpen]);
+
+  const saveRecent = (keyword: string) => {
+    const trimmed = keyword.trim();
+    if (!trimmed) return;
+    const current = getStoredRecentSearches();
+    const updated = [trimmed, ...current.filter((item) => item.toLowerCase() !== trimmed.toLowerCase())].slice(
+      0,
+      MAX_RECENT_SEARCHES
+    );
+    try {
+      localStorage.setItem(RECENT_SEARCHES_KEY, JSON.stringify(updated));
+      setRecentSearches(updated);
+    } catch {
+      // Bỏ qua nếu storage đầy
+    }
+  };
+
+  const handleRemoveRecent = (itemToRemove: string) => {
+    const current = getStoredRecentSearches();
+    const updated = current.filter((item) => item.toLowerCase() !== itemToRemove.toLowerCase());
+    try {
+      localStorage.setItem(RECENT_SEARCHES_KEY, JSON.stringify(updated));
+      setRecentSearches(updated);
+    } catch {
+      // ignore
+    }
+  };
+
+  const handleClearAllRecent = () => {
+    try {
+      localStorage.removeItem(RECENT_SEARCHES_KEY);
+      setRecentSearches([]);
+    } catch {
+      // ignore
+    }
+  };
+
+  const handleSearchSubmit = (text: string) => {
+    const q = text.trim();
+    if (q) {
+      saveRecent(q);
+    }
+    setIsOpen(false);
+
+    if (isQuickOrderPage) {
+      if (q) {
+        navigate(`/dat-hang?q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}`, { replace: true });
+      } else {
+        navigate('/dat-hang', { replace: true });
+      }
+    } else {
+      if (q) {
+        navigate(`/tim-kiem?q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}`);
+      }
+    }
+  };
+
+  const handleClear = () => {
+    setValue('');
+    setLiveProducts([]);
+    if (isQuickOrderPage) {
+      navigate('/dat-hang', { replace: true });
+    } else if (isSearchPage) {
+      navigate('/tim-kiem', { replace: true });
+    }
+  };
+
+  const trendingCategories = (home?.categories || []).slice(0, 6);
+
+  return (
+    <div ref={containerRef} className={`relative ${className}`}>
+      <form
+        role="search"
+        className="relative"
+        onSubmit={(e) => {
+          e.preventDefault();
+          handleSearchSubmit(value);
+        }}
+      >
+        <Search size={17} className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-[#8a9690]" />
+        <input
+          ref={inputRef}
+          type="text"
+          value={value}
+          onFocus={() => setIsOpen(true)}
+          onChange={(e) => {
+            setValue(e.target.value);
+            if (!isOpen) setIsOpen(true);
+          }}
+          onKeyDown={(e) => {
+            if (e.key === 'Escape') {
+              setIsOpen(false);
+              inputRef.current?.blur();
+            }
+          }}
+          placeholder={t('Tìm rau củ, thịt cá...')}
+          aria-label={t('Tìm sản phẩm')}
+          enterKeyHint="search"
+          autoComplete="off"
+          className={`h-10 w-full rounded-xl border border-[#17231d]/12 bg-[#f5f7f3] pl-8.5 sm:pl-9 ${value ? 'pr-21 sm:pr-24' : 'pr-14 sm:pr-16'} text-[12.5px] sm:text-[13px] text-[#17231d] placeholder:text-[#6e7a73] focus:border-[#0f7a4f] focus:bg-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#0f7a4f]/15 [&::-webkit-search-cancel-button]:hidden [&::-webkit-search-decoration]:hidden`}
+        />
+
+        {value && (
+          <button
+            type="button"
+            onClick={handleClear}
+            aria-label={t('Xóa tìm kiếm')}
+            className="absolute right-13 sm:right-[3.75rem] top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 flex size-6 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-[#59665f] hover:bg-[#17231d]/8 hover:text-[#17231d] cursor-pointer"
+          >
+            <X size={15} />
+          </button>
+        )}
+
+        <button
+          type="submit"
+          className="absolute right-1 top-1/2 h-8 -translate-y-1/2 rounded-lg bg-[#0f7a4f] px-2.5 sm:px-3.5 text-[11.5px] sm:text-xs font-bold text-white hover:bg-[#0b4f34] cursor-pointer"
+        >
+          {t('Tìm')}
+        </button>
+      </form>
+
+      {/* POPOVER GỢI Ý THÔNG MINH B2B KIỂU KAMEREO */}
+      {isOpen && (
+        <div className="fixed inset-x-2.5 top-[106px] sm:absolute sm:inset-x-0 sm:top-[calc(100%+6px)] z-50 rounded-2xl border border-[#17231d]/12 bg-white shadow-2xl overflow-hidden max-h-[75vh] sm:max-h-[82vh] overflow-y-auto divide-y divide-[#17231d]/6">
+          {/* TRẠNG THÁI A: KHI Ô TÌM KIẾM ĐANG RỖNG */}
+          {!value.trim() ? (
+            <div>
+              {/* Phân khu 1: Thanh Tiện ích Đặt Bếp B2B */}
+              <div className="bg-linear-to-r from-[#f8faf7] via-white to-[#f5fbf7] p-3 border-b border-[#17231d]/6">
+                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-1 mb-2">
+                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
+                    <Zap size={15} className="text-[#e0742f] fill-[#e0742f]" />
+                    <span className="text-xs font-bold text-[#17231d]">{t('Tiện ích đặt bếp nhanh')}</span>
+                  </div>
+                  <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-[#0f7a4f] bg-[#0f7a4f]/10 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full whitespace-nowrap">
+                    {t('Chuẩn ISO 22000 · Giao sớm 05:00')}
+                  </span>
+                </div>
+                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
+                  <Link
+                    to="/dat-hang"
+                    onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)}
+                    className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 py-2 px-3 rounded-xl bg-white border border-[#17231d]/10 hover:border-[#0f7a4f] hover:text-[#0f7a4f] text-xs font-bold text-[#17231d] shadow-2xs transition group"
+                  >
+                    <Zap size={14} className="text-[#e0742f] group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform" />
+                    <span>{t('Bảng đặt nhanh')}</span>
+                  </Link>
+                  <Link
+                    to="/gio-hang?excel=1"
+                    onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)}
+                    className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 py-2 px-3 rounded-xl bg-white border border-[#17231d]/10 hover:border-[#0f7a4f] hover:text-[#0f7a4f] text-xs font-bold text-[#17231d] shadow-2xs transition group"
+                  >
+                    <FileSpreadsheet size={14} className="text-[#0f7a4f] group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform" />
+                    <span>{t('Đặt từ file Excel')}</span>
+                  </Link>
+                </div>
+              </div>
+
+              {/* Phân khu 2: Lịch sử tìm kiếm gần đây */}
+              {recentSearches.length > 0 && (
+                <div className="p-3 sm:p-3.5 border-b border-[#17231d]/6">
+                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
+                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[#59665f]">
+                      <Clock size={13} />
+                      <span className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">{t('Bạn đã tìm')}</span>
+                    </div>
+                    <button

+                      className="text-[11px] text-[#8a9690] hover:text-rose-500 flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer transition-colors"
+                      title={t('Xóa toàn bộ lịch sử tìm kiếm')}

+                      <Trash2 size={12} />
+                      <span>{t('Xóa tất cả')}</span>

+                          handleSearchSubmit(term);
+                        }}
+                        className="cursor-pointer bg-[#f5f7f3] hover:bg-[#eaf6ef] hover:text-[#0f7a4f] text-[#17231d] text-xs px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-[#17231d]/8 flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors group"
+                      >

+                        <button

+                          className="text-[#8a9690] hover:text-rose-500 rounded p-0.5 -mr-1"

+                  <span className="text-xs font-bold text-[#17231d]">{t('Tìm kiếm thịnh hành')}</span>
+                </div>

+                  {TRENDING_KEYWORDS.map((kw) => (
+                    <button
+                      type="button"

+                    >
+                      {kw}
+                    </button>
+                  ))}
+                </div>
+              </div>
+
+              {/* Phân khu 4: Nhóm hàng xu hướng cho bếp */}
+              {trendingCategories.length > 0 && (
+                <div className="p-3 sm:p-3.5 bg-[#fcfdfa]">

+                    <LayoutGrid size={15} className="text-[#0f7a4f]" />
+                    <span className="text-xs font-bold text-[#17231d]">{t('Nhóm hàng xu hướng cho bếp')}</span>
+                  </div>
+                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-2">

+                        to={`/danh-muc/${cat.slug}`}
+                        key={cat.slug}
+                        onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)}
+                        className="flex items-center gap-2 p-2 rounded-xl border border-[#17231d]/8 bg-white hover:bg-[#f1f7f3] hover:border-[#0f7a4f]/30 transition group"
+                      >
+                        {cat.imageUrl ? (
+                          <img
+                            src={cat.imageUrl}
+                            alt={cat.name}

+                          />
+                        ) : (
+                          <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-[#0f7a4f]/10 flex items-center justify-center text-[#0f7a4f] shrink-0">
+                            <PackageOpen size={16} />
+                          </div>
+                        )}
+                        <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#17231d] group-hover:text-[#0f7a4f] truncate">
+                          {cat.name}
+                        </span>

+            <div>

+                <span className="flex-1 truncate">
+                  {t('Tìm tất cả mặt hàng khớp với')} <span className="font-bold underline">"{value.trim()}"</span>

+              </button>
+
+              {/* Danh sách kết quả gợi ý trực tiếp */}

+                type="button"
+                onClick={() => handleSearchSubmit(value)}
+                className="w-full p-2.5 text-center text-xs font-bold text-[#0f7a4f] hover:bg-[#eaf6ef] transition-colors border-t border-[#17231d]/6 cursor-pointer"
+              >
+                {t('Xem tất cả kết quả cho')} "{value.trim()}" →
+              </button>
+            </div>
+          )}
+        </div>
+      )}
+    </div>
+  );
+}
📄 2. FULL SOURCE CODE CHUẨN PRODUCTION 100% (534 dòng) — Bản hoàn chỉnh của order-webapp/src/components/store/SmartSearchBox.tsx 
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useLocation, useNavigate, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import {
  Search,
  X,
  Zap,
  FileSpreadsheet,
  Flame,
  LayoutGrid,
  Clock,
  Trash2,
  Plus,
  ChevronRight,
  PackageOpen,
  Loader2,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { api, type Product } from '../../lib/api';
import { useCart } from '../../contexts/CartContext';
import { useLanguage } from '../../contexts/LanguageContext';
import { useStoreHome } from '../../hooks/useStoreHome';
import { searchProducts } from '../../lib/productSearch';
import ProductThumbnail from '../products/ProductThumbnail';

const RECENT_SEARCHES_KEY = 'tps1_recent_searches_v1';
const MAX_RECENT_SEARCHES = 8;

const TRENDING_KEYWORDS = [
  'Ba rọi heo',
  'Cá nục',
  'Trứng gà',
  'Cà chua',
  'Chanh',
  'Hành tây',
  'Cà rốt',
  'Dưa leo',
  'Hành lá',
  'Xà lách',
  'Dầu ăn',
  'Đậu hũ',
];

function getStoredRecentSearches(): string[] {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(RECENT_SEARCHES_KEY);
    if (!raw) return [];
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    return Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed.filter((s): s is string => typeof s === 'string' && Boolean(s.trim())) : [];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

function money(v: number) {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat('vi-VN').format(Number(v) || 0) + 'đ';
}

interface SmartSearchBoxProps {
  className?: string;
}

export default function SmartSearchBox({ className = '' }: SmartSearchBoxProps) {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const location = useLocation();
  const [params] = useSearchParams();
  const { t, productName, productUnit } = useLanguage();
  const { addItem, quantityOf } = useCart();
  const { data: home } = useStoreHome();

  const isQuickOrderPage = location.pathname === '/dat-hang';
  const isSearchPage = location.pathname === '/tim-kiem';

  const [value, setValue] = useState(
    isSearchPage || isQuickOrderPage ? params.get('q') || '' : ''
  );
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [recentSearches, setRecentSearches] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [liveProducts, setLiveProducts] = useState<Product[]>([]);
  const [isSearching, setIsSearching] = useState(false);

  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  // Đồng bộ giá trị input khi url thay đổi
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isSearchPage || isQuickOrderPage) {
      setValue(params.get('q') || '');
    } else {
      setValue('');
    }
  }, [location.pathname, params, isSearchPage, isQuickOrderPage]);

  // Nạp lịch sử tìm kiếm khi mở popover
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) {
      setRecentSearches(getStoredRecentSearches());
    }
  }, [isOpen]);

  // Click ngoài đóng popover
  useEffect(() => {
    function handleClickOutside(e: MouseEvent) {
      if (containerRef.current && !containerRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
        setIsOpen(false);
      }
    }
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
  }, []);

  // Debounced Live Search khi value có nội dung
  useEffect(() => {
    const query = value.trim();
    if (!query || !isOpen) {
      setLiveProducts([]);
      setIsSearching(false);
      return;
    }

    setIsSearching(true);
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      api
        .products({ search: query, page: 0 }, controller.signal)
        .then((res) => {
          const raw = res.products || [];
          const strict = searchProducts(
            raw,
            query,
            (p) => ({ text: `${p.sku || ''} ${p.name} ${t(p.name)} ${p.category || ''}`, name: t(p.name) }),
            (p) => Boolean(p.thumbUrl || p.imageUrl),
            20
          );
          setLiveProducts(strict.length > 0 ? strict : raw);
        })
        .catch((err) => {
          if (err.name !== 'AbortError') {
            setLiveProducts([]);
          }
        })
        .finally(() => {
          setIsSearching(false);
        });
    }, 180);

    return () => {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      controller.abort();
    };
  }, [value, isOpen]);

  const saveRecent = (keyword: string) => {
    const trimmed = keyword.trim();
    if (!trimmed) return;
    const current = getStoredRecentSearches();
    const updated = [trimmed, ...current.filter((item) => item.toLowerCase() !== trimmed.toLowerCase())].slice(
      0,
      MAX_RECENT_SEARCHES
    );
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(RECENT_SEARCHES_KEY, JSON.stringify(updated));
      setRecentSearches(updated);
    } catch {
      // Bỏ qua nếu storage đầy
    }
  };

  const handleRemoveRecent = (itemToRemove: string) => {
    const current = getStoredRecentSearches();
    const updated = current.filter((item) => item.toLowerCase() !== itemToRemove.toLowerCase());
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(RECENT_SEARCHES_KEY, JSON.stringify(updated));
      setRecentSearches(updated);
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
  };

  const handleClearAllRecent = () => {
    try {
      localStorage.removeItem(RECENT_SEARCHES_KEY);
      setRecentSearches([]);
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
  };

  const handleSearchSubmit = (text: string) => {
    const q = text.trim();
    if (q) {
      saveRecent(q);
    }
    setIsOpen(false);

    if (isQuickOrderPage) {
      if (q) {
        navigate(`/dat-hang?q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}`, { replace: true });
      } else {
        navigate('/dat-hang', { replace: true });
      }
    } else {
      if (q) {
        navigate(`/tim-kiem?q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}`);
      }
    }
  };

  const handleClear = () => {
    setValue('');
    setLiveProducts([]);
    if (isQuickOrderPage) {
      navigate('/dat-hang', { replace: true });
    } else if (isSearchPage) {
      navigate('/tim-kiem', { replace: true });
    }
  };

  const trendingCategories = (home?.categories || []).slice(0, 6);

  return (
    <div ref={containerRef} className={`relative ${className}`}>
      <form
        role="search"
        className="relative"
        onSubmit={(e) => {
          e.preventDefault();
          handleSearchSubmit(value);
        }}
      >
        <Search size={17} className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-[#8a9690]" />
        <input
          ref={inputRef}
          type="text"
          value={value}
          onFocus={() => setIsOpen(true)}
          onChange={(e) => {
            setValue(e.target.value);
            if (!isOpen) setIsOpen(true);
          }}
          onKeyDown={(e) => {
            if (e.key === 'Escape') {
              setIsOpen(false);
              inputRef.current?.blur();
            }
          }}
          placeholder={t('Tìm rau củ, thịt cá...')}
          aria-label={t('Tìm sản phẩm')}
          enterKeyHint="search"
          autoComplete="off"
          className={`h-10 w-full rounded-xl border border-[#17231d]/12 bg-[#f5f7f3] pl-8.5 sm:pl-9 ${value ? 'pr-21 sm:pr-24' : 'pr-14 sm:pr-16'} text-[12.5px] sm:text-[13px] text-[#17231d] placeholder:text-[#6e7a73] focus:border-[#0f7a4f] focus:bg-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#0f7a4f]/15 [&::-webkit-search-cancel-button]:hidden [&::-webkit-search-decoration]:hidden`}
        />

        {value && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleClear}
            aria-label={t('Xóa tìm kiếm')}
            className="absolute right-13 sm:right-[3.75rem] top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 flex size-6 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-[#59665f] hover:bg-[#17231d]/8 hover:text-[#17231d] cursor-pointer"
          >
            <X size={15} />
          </button>
        )}

        <button
          type="submit"
          className="absolute right-1 top-1/2 h-8 -translate-y-1/2 rounded-lg bg-[#0f7a4f] px-2.5 sm:px-3.5 text-[11.5px] sm:text-xs font-bold text-white hover:bg-[#0b4f34] cursor-pointer"
        >
          {t('Tìm')}
        </button>
      </form>

      {/* POPOVER GỢI Ý THÔNG MINH B2B KIỂU KAMEREO */}
      {isOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-x-2.5 top-[106px] sm:absolute sm:inset-x-0 sm:top-[calc(100%+6px)] z-50 rounded-2xl border border-[#17231d]/12 bg-white shadow-2xl overflow-hidden max-h-[75vh] sm:max-h-[82vh] overflow-y-auto divide-y divide-[#17231d]/6">
          {/* TRẠNG THÁI A: KHI Ô TÌM KIẾM ĐANG RỖNG */}
          {!value.trim() ? (
            <div>
              {/* Phân khu 1: Thanh Tiện ích Đặt Bếp B2B */}
              <div className="bg-linear-to-r from-[#f8faf7] via-white to-[#f5fbf7] p-3 border-b border-[#17231d]/6">
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-1 mb-2">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    <Zap size={15} className="text-[#e0742f] fill-[#e0742f]" />
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold text-[#17231d]">{t('Tiện ích đặt bếp nhanh')}</span>
                  </div>
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-[#0f7a4f] bg-[#0f7a4f]/10 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full whitespace-nowrap">
                    {t('Chuẩn ISO 22000 · Giao sớm 05:00')}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Link
                    to="/dat-hang"
                    onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)}
                    className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 py-2 px-3 rounded-xl bg-white border border-[#17231d]/10 hover:border-[#0f7a4f] hover:text-[#0f7a4f] text-xs font-bold text-[#17231d] shadow-2xs transition group"
                  >
                    <Zap size={14} className="text-[#e0742f] group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform" />
                    <span>{t('Bảng đặt nhanh')}</span>
                  </Link>
                  <Link
                    to="/gio-hang?excel=1"
                    onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)}
                    className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 py-2 px-3 rounded-xl bg-white border border-[#17231d]/10 hover:border-[#0f7a4f] hover:text-[#0f7a4f] text-xs font-bold text-[#17231d] shadow-2xs transition group"
                  >
                    <FileSpreadsheet size={14} className="text-[#0f7a4f] group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform" />
                    <span>{t('Đặt từ file Excel')}</span>
                  </Link>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Phân khu 2: Lịch sử tìm kiếm gần đây */}
              {recentSearches.length > 0 && (
                <div className="p-3 sm:p-3.5 border-b border-[#17231d]/6">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[#59665f]">
                      <Clock size={13} />
                      <span className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">{t('Bạn đã tìm')}</span>
                    </div>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={handleClearAllRecent}
                      className="text-[11px] text-[#8a9690] hover:text-rose-500 flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer transition-colors"
                      title={t('Xóa toàn bộ lịch sử tìm kiếm')}
                    >
                      <Trash2 size={12} />
                      <span>{t('Xóa tất cả')}</span>
                    </button>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                    {recentSearches.map((term) => (
                      <div
                        key={term}
                        onClick={() => {
                          setValue(term);
                          handleSearchSubmit(term);
                        }}
                        className="cursor-pointer bg-[#f5f7f3] hover:bg-[#eaf6ef] hover:text-[#0f7a4f] text-[#17231d] text-xs px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-[#17231d]/8 flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors group"
                      >
                        <span>{term}</span>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={(e) => {
                            e.stopPropagation();
                            handleRemoveRecent(term);
                          }}
                          className="text-[#8a9690] hover:text-rose-500 rounded p-0.5 -mr-1"
                          title={t('Xóa')}
                        >
                          <X size={12} />
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Phân khu 3: Tìm kiếm thịnh hành */}
              <div className="p-3 sm:p-3.5 border-b border-[#17231d]/6">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 mb-2.5">
                  <Flame size={15} className="text-[#e0742f] fill-[#e0742f]" />
                  <span className="text-xs font-bold text-[#17231d]">{t('Tìm kiếm thịnh hành')}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                  {TRENDING_KEYWORDS.map((kw) => (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      key={kw}
                      onClick={() => {
                        setValue(kw);
                        handleSearchSubmit(kw);
                      }}
                      className="cursor-pointer bg-[#f8faf7] hover:bg-[#fef5ee] hover:border-[#e0742f]/30 hover:text-[#c4601f] text-[#2c3a33] text-xs font-medium px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border border-[#17231d]/8 transition-all"
                    >
                      {kw}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Phân khu 4: Nhóm hàng xu hướng cho bếp */}
              {trendingCategories.length > 0 && (
                <div className="p-3 sm:p-3.5 bg-[#fcfdfa]">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 mb-2.5">
                    <LayoutGrid size={15} className="text-[#0f7a4f]" />
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold text-[#17231d]">{t('Nhóm hàng xu hướng cho bếp')}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-2">
                    {trendingCategories.map((cat) => (
                      <Link
                        to={`/danh-muc/${cat.slug}`}
                        key={cat.slug}
                        onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)}
                        className="flex items-center gap-2 p-2 rounded-xl border border-[#17231d]/8 bg-white hover:bg-[#f1f7f3] hover:border-[#0f7a4f]/30 transition group"
                      >
                        {cat.imageUrl ? (
                          <img
                            src={cat.imageUrl}
                            alt={cat.name}
                            className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg object-cover shrink-0 border border-[#17231d]/6"
                          />
                        ) : (
                          <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-[#0f7a4f]/10 flex items-center justify-center text-[#0f7a4f] shrink-0">
                            <PackageOpen size={16} />
                          </div>
                        )}
                        <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#17231d] group-hover:text-[#0f7a4f] truncate">
                          {cat.name}
                        </span>
                      </Link>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          ) : (
            /* TRẠNG THÁI B: KHI Ô TÌM KIẾM CÓ TỪ KHÓA */
            <div>
              {/* Nút tìm kiếm nhanh khớp từ khóa */}
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => handleSearchSubmit(value)}
                className="w-full flex items-center gap-2.5 p-3 text-left hover:bg-[#f8faf7] transition-colors cursor-pointer border-b border-[#17231d]/6 text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold text-[#0f7a4f]"
              >
                <Search size={16} className="shrink-0 text-[#0f7a4f]" />
                <span className="flex-1 truncate">
                  {t('Tìm tất cả mặt hàng khớp với')} <span className="font-bold underline">"{value.trim()}"</span>
                </span>
                <ChevronRight size={16} className="text-[#8a9690] shrink-0" />
              </button>

              {/* Danh sách kết quả gợi ý trực tiếp */}
              {isSearching ? (
                <div className="p-6 flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 text-[#59665f]">
                  <Loader2 size={24} className="animate-spin text-[#0f7a4f]" />
                  <span className="text-xs">{t('Đang tìm kiếm kho hàng 5.000+ sản phẩm...')}</span>
                </div>
              ) : liveProducts.length > 0 ? (
                <div className="divide-y divide-[#17231d]/6">
                  {liveProducts.slice(0, 8).map((product) => {
                    const qtyInCart = quantityOf(product.id);
                    const unitLabel = productUnit(product.unit || 'Kg');
                    return (
                      <div
                        key={product.id}
                        onClick={() => {
                          saveRecent(product.name);
                          handleSearchSubmit(product.name);
                        }}
                        className="flex items-center gap-3 p-2.5 sm:p-3 hover:bg-[#f5fbf7] transition-colors cursor-pointer group"
                      >
                        {/* Thumbnail */}
                        <ProductThumbnail product={product} size="sm" className="w-11 h-11 rounded-lg shrink-0" />

                        {/* Thông tin sản phẩm */}
                        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                          <p className="text-xs sm:text-[13px] font-bold text-[#17231d] group-hover:text-[#0f7a4f] transition-colors line-clamp-1">
                            {productName(product.name)}
                          </p>
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] text-[#59665f] mt-0.5">
                            {product.sku && (
                              <span className="font-mono text-[10px] font-bold bg-[#17231d]/6 px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded shrink-0">
                                {product.sku}
                              </span>
                            )}
                            <span className="whitespace-nowrap">{unitLabel}</span>
                            {product.category && (
                              <span className="truncate">· {productName(product.category)}</span>
                            )}
                          </div>
                        </div>

                        {/* Giá sản phẩm */}
                        <div className="text-right shrink-0">
                          {product.priceOnRequest ? (
                            <span className="block text-xs font-bold text-[#e0742f]">
                              {t('Chờ sale báo giá')}
                            </span>
                          ) : (
                            <span className="block text-sm font-extrabold text-[#0f7a4f]">
                              {money(product.price)}
                            </span>
                          )}
                          <span className="block text-[10px] text-[#8a9690]">/{unitLabel}</span>
                        </div>

                        {/* Nút thêm nhanh vào giỏ */}
                        <div className="shrink-0 flex items-center gap-1.5 pl-1">
                          {qtyInCart > 0 && (
                            <span className="text-[10px] font-bold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-[#0f7a4f]/15 text-[#0f7a4f]">
                              x{qtyInCart}
                            </span>
                          )}
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={(e) => {
                              e.stopPropagation();
                              addItem(product, 1);
                              saveRecent(product.name);
                            }}
                            className="size-8 rounded-lg bg-[#0f7a4f] text-white hover:bg-[#0b4f34] flex items-center justify-center transition-transform active:scale-95 shadow-xs cursor-pointer"
                            title={`+1 ${unitLabel} vào đơn`}
                            aria-label={`Thêm 1 ${unitLabel} ${product.name} vào đơn`}
                          >
                            <Plus size={16} strokeWidth={2.5} />
                          </button>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="p-6 text-center text-xs text-[#59665f]">
                  <p className="font-semibold text-[#17231d]">
                    {t('Không tìm thấy mặt hàng phù hợp với')} "{value.trim()}"
                  </p>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-[#8a9690] mt-1">
                    {t('Thử tìm bằng các từ khóa phổ biến: thịt, cá, rau củ, trứng, gia vị...')}
                  </p>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Dòng cuối cùng: Xem tất cả kết quả */}
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => handleSearchSubmit(value)}
                className="w-full p-2.5 text-center text-xs font-bold text-[#0f7a4f] hover:bg-[#eaf6ef] transition-colors border-t border-[#17231d]/6 cursor-pointer"
              >
                {t('Xem tất cả kết quả cho')} "{value.trim()}" →
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

Đặc tả kỹ thuật cho AI: Tạo mới hook chuyển lăn chuột dọc thành trượt ngang, khóa overscroll (e.preventDefault()), kéo thả chuột (drag-to-scroll) và chế độ autoScroll tự trôi chậm qua lại cho dải Hàng bán chạy.

+import { useEffect, useRef, type RefObject } from 'react';
+
+/**
+ * Hook cho tất cả các dải vuốt ngang:
+ * 1. Lăn chuột dọc/ngang (wheel) -> cuộn ngang mượt mà.
+ * 2. Khóa cứng cuộn trang (e.preventDefault + e.stopPropagation) kể cả khi chạm mép đầu/cuối.
+ * 3. Kéo chuột trái (drag to scroll) mượt mà, tắt snap khi kéo, chặn click nhầm vào thẻ con nếu kéo > 5px.
+ * 4. Tính toán theo getBoundingClientRect().width để chính xác ở mọi tỷ lệ zoom trình duyệt.
+ * 5. Tự gắn lại khi phần tử xuất hiện muộn (dải chỉ render sau khi dữ liệu tải xong).
+ */
+export function useHorizontalScroll<T extends HTMLElement = HTMLDivElement>(
+  externalRef?: RefObject<T | null>
+): RefObject<T | null> {
+  const internalRef = useRef<T | null>(null);
+  const ref = externalRef || internalRef;
+  const attachedRef = useRef<{ el: T; detach: () => void } | null>(null);
+
+  const attach = (el: T) => {
+    let isDown = false;
+    let startX = 0;
+    let scrollLeftStart = 0;
+    let hasDragged = false;
+    let snapTimeout: number | undefined;
+
+    const onWheel = (e: WheelEvent) => {
+      const width = el.getBoundingClientRect().width;
+      const maxScrollLeft = el.scrollWidth - width;
+      // Nếu vùng chứa không đủ dài để cuộn, không can thiệp
+      if (maxScrollLeft <= 2) return;
+
+      // LUÔN LUÔN khóa cuộn dọc của trang khi con trỏ ở trong dải cuộn ngang
+      e.preventDefault();
+      e.stopPropagation();
+
+      el.style.scrollSnapType = 'none';
+      const delta = Math.abs(e.deltaY) >= Math.abs(e.deltaX) ? e.deltaY : e.deltaX;
+      el.scrollLeft = Math.max(0, Math.min(maxScrollLeft, el.scrollLeft + delta));
+
+      window.clearTimeout(snapTimeout);
+      snapTimeout = window.setTimeout(() => {
+        el.style.scrollSnapType = '';
+      }, 150);
+    };
+
+    const onMouseDown = (e: MouseEvent) => {
+      // Chỉ nhận click chuột trái (button === 0)
+      if (e.button !== 0) return;
+      const width = el.getBoundingClientRect().width;
+      const maxScrollLeft = el.scrollWidth - width;
+      if (maxScrollLeft <= 2) return;
+
+      isDown = true;
+      startX = e.pageX - el.offsetLeft;
+      scrollLeftStart = el.scrollLeft;
+      hasDragged = false;
+    };
+
+    const onMouseMove = (e: MouseEvent) => {
+      if (!isDown) return;
+      const x = e.pageX - el.offsetLeft;
+      const walk = x - startX;
+
+      if (Math.abs(walk) > 5) {
+        if (!hasDragged) {
+          hasDragged = true;
+          el.style.scrollSnapType = 'none';
+          el.classList.add('cursor-grabbing');
+          el.classList.remove('cursor-grab');
+        }
+        e.preventDefault();
+        const width = el.getBoundingClientRect().width;
+        const maxScrollLeft = el.scrollWidth - width;
+        el.scrollLeft = Math.max(0, Math.min(maxScrollLeft, scrollLeftStart - walk));
+      }
+    };
+
+    const stopDrag = () => {
+      if (isDown) {
+        isDown = false;
+        el.style.scrollSnapType = '';
+        el.classList.remove('cursor-grabbing');
+        el.classList.add('cursor-grab');
+        // Giữ hasDragged thêm 50ms để bắt sự kiện click capture ngăn click nhầm Link
+        window.setTimeout(() => {
+          hasDragged = false;
+        }, 50);
+      }
+    };
+
+    const onClickCapture = (e: MouseEvent) => {
+      if (hasDragged) {
+        e.preventDefault();
+        e.stopPropagation();
+      }
+    };
+
+    el.addEventListener('wheel', onWheel, { passive: false });
+    el.addEventListener('mousedown', onMouseDown);
+    window.addEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove);
+    window.addEventListener('mouseup', stopDrag);
+    el.addEventListener('mouseleave', stopDrag);
+    el.addEventListener('click', onClickCapture, { capture: true });
+
+    // Đảm bảo các thuộc tính css trải nghiệm mượt mà
+    el.classList.add('overscroll-contain', 'select-none', 'cursor-grab');
+
+    return () => {
+      window.clearTimeout(snapTimeout);
+      el.removeEventListener('wheel', onWheel);
+      el.removeEventListener('mousedown', onMouseDown);
+      window.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove);
+      window.removeEventListener('mouseup', stopDrag);
+      el.removeEventListener('mouseleave', stopDrag);
+      el.removeEventListener('click', onClickCapture, { capture: true });
+    };
+  };
+
+  // Chạy sau mỗi lần render: nếu phần tử trong ref đổi (xuất hiện muộn / bị thay), gắn lại listener.
+  useEffect(() => {
+    const el = ref.current;
+    const attached = attachedRef.current;
+    if (attached && attached.el === el) return;
+    if (attached) {
+      attached.detach();
+      attachedRef.current = null;
+    }
+    if (el) attachedRef.current = { el, detach: attach(el) };
+  });
+
+  // Dọn dẹp khi unmount
+  useEffect(() => {
+    return () => {
+      attachedRef.current?.detach();
+      attachedRef.current = null;
+    };
+  }, []);
+
+  return ref;
+}
+
+export default useHorizontalScroll;
📄 2. FULL SOURCE CODE CHUẨN PRODUCTION 100% (142 dòng) — Bản hoàn chỉnh của order-webapp/src/hooks/useHorizontalScroll.ts 
import { useEffect, useRef, type RefObject } from 'react';

/**
 * Hook cho tất cả các dải vuốt ngang:
 * 1. Lăn chuột dọc/ngang (wheel) -> cuộn ngang mượt mà.
 * 2. Khóa cứng cuộn trang (e.preventDefault + e.stopPropagation) kể cả khi chạm mép đầu/cuối.
 * 3. Kéo chuột trái (drag to scroll) mượt mà, tắt snap khi kéo, chặn click nhầm vào thẻ con nếu kéo > 5px.
 * 4. Tính toán theo getBoundingClientRect().width để chính xác ở mọi tỷ lệ zoom trình duyệt.
 * 5. Tự gắn lại khi phần tử xuất hiện muộn (dải chỉ render sau khi dữ liệu tải xong).
 */
export function useHorizontalScroll<T extends HTMLElement = HTMLDivElement>(
  externalRef?: RefObject<T | null>
): RefObject<T | null> {
  const internalRef = useRef<T | null>(null);
  const ref = externalRef || internalRef;
  const attachedRef = useRef<{ el: T; detach: () => void } | null>(null);

  const attach = (el: T) => {
    let isDown = false;
    let startX = 0;
    let scrollLeftStart = 0;
    let hasDragged = false;
    let snapTimeout: number | undefined;

    const onWheel = (e: WheelEvent) => {
      const width = el.getBoundingClientRect().width;
      const maxScrollLeft = el.scrollWidth - width;
      // Nếu vùng chứa không đủ dài để cuộn, không can thiệp
      if (maxScrollLeft <= 2) return;

      // LUÔN LUÔN khóa cuộn dọc của trang khi con trỏ ở trong dải cuộn ngang
      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopPropagation();

      el.style.scrollSnapType = 'none';
      const delta = Math.abs(e.deltaY) >= Math.abs(e.deltaX) ? e.deltaY : e.deltaX;
      el.scrollLeft = Math.max(0, Math.min(maxScrollLeft, el.scrollLeft + delta));

      window.clearTimeout(snapTimeout);
      snapTimeout = window.setTimeout(() => {
        el.style.scrollSnapType = '';
      }, 150);
    };

    const onMouseDown = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      // Chỉ nhận click chuột trái (button === 0)
      if (e.button !== 0) return;
      const width = el.getBoundingClientRect().width;
      const maxScrollLeft = el.scrollWidth - width;
      if (maxScrollLeft <= 2) return;

      isDown = true;
      startX = e.pageX - el.offsetLeft;
      scrollLeftStart = el.scrollLeft;
      hasDragged = false;
    };

    const onMouseMove = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (!isDown) return;
      const x = e.pageX - el.offsetLeft;
      const walk = x - startX;

      if (Math.abs(walk) > 5) {
        if (!hasDragged) {
          hasDragged = true;
          el.style.scrollSnapType = 'none';
          el.classList.add('cursor-grabbing');
          el.classList.remove('cursor-grab');
        }
        e.preventDefault();
        const width = el.getBoundingClientRect().width;
        const maxScrollLeft = el.scrollWidth - width;
        el.scrollLeft = Math.max(0, Math.min(maxScrollLeft, scrollLeftStart - walk));
      }
    };

    const stopDrag = () => {
      if (isDown) {
        isDown = false;
        el.style.scrollSnapType = '';
        el.classList.remove('cursor-grabbing');
        el.classList.add('cursor-grab');
        // Giữ hasDragged thêm 50ms để bắt sự kiện click capture ngăn click nhầm Link
        window.setTimeout(() => {
          hasDragged = false;
        }, 50);
      }
    };

    const onClickCapture = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (hasDragged) {
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();
      }
    };

    el.addEventListener('wheel', onWheel, { passive: false });
    el.addEventListener('mousedown', onMouseDown);
    window.addEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove);
    window.addEventListener('mouseup', stopDrag);
    el.addEventListener('mouseleave', stopDrag);
    el.addEventListener('click', onClickCapture, { capture: true });

    // Đảm bảo các thuộc tính css trải nghiệm mượt mà
    el.classList.add('overscroll-contain', 'select-none', 'cursor-grab');

    return () => {
      window.clearTimeout(snapTimeout);
      el.removeEventListener('wheel', onWheel);
      el.removeEventListener('mousedown', onMouseDown);
      window.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove);
      window.removeEventListener('mouseup', stopDrag);
      el.removeEventListener('mouseleave', stopDrag);
      el.removeEventListener('click', onClickCapture, { capture: true });
    };
  };

  // Chạy sau mỗi lần render: nếu phần tử trong ref đổi (xuất hiện muộn / bị thay), gắn lại listener.
  useEffect(() => {
    const el = ref.current;
    const attached = attachedRef.current;
    if (attached && attached.el === el) return;
    if (attached) {
      attached.detach();
      attachedRef.current = null;
    }
    if (el) attachedRef.current = { el, detach: attach(el) };
  });

  // Dọn dẹp khi unmount
  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      attachedRef.current?.detach();
      attachedRef.current = null;
    };
  }, []);

  return ref;
}

export default useHorizontalScroll;

import { BrowserRouter, HashRouter, Routes, Route, Navigate, useLocation, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { lazy, Suspense, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Capacitor } from '@capacitor/core';
import { App as NativeApp } from '@capacitor/app';
import { SplashScreen } from '@capacitor/splash-screen';
import { AuthProvider, useAuth } from './contexts/AuthContext';
import { CartProvider } from './contexts/CartContext';
import { FavoritesProvider } from './contexts/FavoritesContext';
import StoreLayout from './layouts/StoreLayout';
import LoginPage from './pages/LoginPage';
import RegisterPage from './pages/RegisterPage';
import StoreHomePage from './pages/StoreHomePage';
import { LanguageProvider } from './contexts/LanguageContext';

const ChangePasswordPage = lazy(() => import('./pages/ChangePasswordPage'));
const ProductsPage = lazy(() => import('./pages/ProductsPage'));
const ProductListingPage = lazy(() => import('./pages/ProductListingPage'));
const ProductDetailPage = lazy(() => import('./pages/ProductDetailPage'));
const FavoritesPage = lazy(() => import('./pages/FavoritesPage'));
const CartPage = lazy(() => import('./pages/CartPage'));
const OrdersPage = lazy(() => import('./pages/OrdersPage'));
const OrderDetailPage = lazy(() => import('./pages/OrderDetailPage'));
const AccountPage = lazy(() => import('./pages/AccountPage'));

const LoadingScreen = () => (
  <div className="fixed inset-0 min-h-screen bg-white flex flex-col items-center justify-center z-50">
    <img
      src={`${import.meta.env.BASE_URL}images/tps1-logo-vertical.png`}
      alt="Thực Phẩm Số Một"
      className="w-36 max-w-[50vw] h-auto object-contain mb-6 animate-pulse"
    />
    <div className="w-8 h-8 border-3 border-[#eaf6ef] border-t-[#0f7a4f] rounded-full animate-spin" />
    <p className="mt-3 text-xs font-medium text-[#59665f] tracking-wide">Đang tải dữ liệu...</p>
  </div>
);

// App chỉ dành cho khách hàng. Cửa hàng (trang chủ, danh mục, sản phẩm, giỏ
// hàng) mở cho khách chưa đăng nhập; đặt nhanh, đơn hàng, tài khoản bắt đăng
// nhập và đưa khách quay lại đúng trang sau khi đăng nhập (?quay-lai=).
const ProtectedRoute = ({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) => {
  const { session, loading } = useAuth();
  const location = useLocation();
  if (loading) return <LoadingScreen />;
  if (!session) return <Navigate to={`/dang-nhap?quay-lai=${encodeURIComponent(location.pathname + location.search)}`} replace />;
  // Mật khẩu do sale cấp lần đầu bắt buộc phải đổi trước khi làm gì khác —
  // chặn cứng ở đây, không chỉ ẩn/hiện nút trên giao diện.
  if (session.mustChangePassword && location.pathname !== '/doi-mat-khau') {
    return <Navigate to="/doi-mat-khau" replace />;
  }
  return <>{children}</>;
};

// Trang công khai: khách đã đăng nhập bằng mật khẩu tạm do sale cấp vẫn
// phải đổi mật khẩu trước, giống các trang bắt đăng nhập.
const PasswordGate = ({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) => {
  const { session } = useAuth();
  if (session?.mustChangePassword) return <Navigate to="/doi-mat-khau" replace />;
  return <>{children}</>;
};

function LegacyOrderRedirect() {
  const { id = '' } = useParams();
  return <Navigate to={`/don-hang/${id}`} replace />;
}

function NativeBackButton() {
  const location = useLocation();
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!Capacitor.isNativePlatform()) return;
    const listener = NativeApp.addListener('backButton', () => {
      if (location.pathname === '/') void NativeApp.exitApp();
      else window.history.back();
    });
    return () => { void listener.then(handle => handle.remove()); };
  }, [location.pathname]);
  return null;
}

function App() {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (Capacitor.isNativePlatform()) void SplashScreen.hide();
  }, []);
  const Router = Capacitor.isNativePlatform() ? HashRouter : BrowserRouter;
  return (
    <LanguageProvider><AuthProvider>
      <CartProvider><FavoritesProvider>
        <Router>
          <NativeBackButton />
          <Suspense fallback={<LoadingScreen />}><Routes>
            <Route path="/dang-nhap" element={<LoginPage />} />
            <Route path="/dang-ky" element={<RegisterPage />} />
            <Route path="/" element={<StoreLayout />}>
              {/* Công khai */}
              <Route index element={<PasswordGate><StoreHomePage /></PasswordGate>} />
              <Route path="danh-muc" element={<PasswordGate><ProductListingPage mode="categories" /></PasswordGate>} />
              <Route path="danh-muc/:slug" element={<PasswordGate><ProductListingPage mode="category" /></PasswordGate>} />
              <Route path="tim-kiem" element={<PasswordGate><ProductListingPage mode="search" /></PasswordGate>} />
              <Route path="san-pham/:id" element={<PasswordGate><ProductDetailPage /></PasswordGate>} />
              <Route path="gio-hang" element={<PasswordGate><CartPage /></PasswordGate>} />
              <Route path="yeu-thich" element={<PasswordGate><FavoritesPage /></PasswordGate>} />
              {/* Bắt đăng nhập */}
              <Route path="dat-hang" element={<ProtectedRoute><ProductsPage /></ProtectedRoute>} />
              <Route path="dat-hang/excel" element={<Navigate to="/gio-hang?excel=1" replace />} />
              <Route path="don-hang" element={<ProtectedRoute><OrdersPage /></ProtectedRoute>} />
              <Route path="don-hang/:id" element={<ProtectedRoute><OrderDetailPage /></ProtectedRoute>} />
              {/* Link cũ trong thông báo yêu cầu sửa đơn (đường dẫn của quanly) */}
              <Route path="don-hang-cua-toi/:id" element={<LegacyOrderRedirect />} />
              <Route path="tai-khoan" element={<ProtectedRoute><AccountPage /></ProtectedRoute>} />
              <Route path="doi-mat-khau" element={<ProtectedRoute><ChangePasswordPage /></ProtectedRoute>} />
            </Route>
            <Route path="*" element={<Navigate to="/" replace />} />
          </Routes></Suspense>
        </Router>
      </FavoritesProvider></CartProvider>
    </AuthProvider></LanguageProvider>
  );
}

export default App;

📄 2. FULL SOURCE CODE CHUẨN PRODUCTION 100% (386 dòng) — Bản hoàn chỉnh của order-webapp/src/components/products/CartCheckoutDrawer.tsx 
import React from 'react';
import {
  X,
  Calendar,
  Clock,
  MapPin,
  User,
  Phone,
  Trash2,
  AlertCircle,
  ArrowRight,
  Info,
} from 'lucide-react';
import type { CustomerSession, Product } from '../../lib/api';
import type { OrderTab } from './OrderTabsBar';
import ProductThumbnail from './ProductThumbnail';
import UnlistedItemEntry from './UnlistedItemEntry';
import LineNoteEditor from './LineNoteEditor';
import { useLanguage } from '../../contexts/LanguageContext';

function money(v: number) {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat('vi-VN').format(Number(v) || 0) + 'đ';
}

interface CartCheckoutDrawerProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  session: CustomerSession | null;
  activeTab: OrderTab;
  onUpdateTab: (updater: (prev: OrderTab) => OrderTab) => void;
  onUpdateQty: (productId: string, qty: number) => void;
  onUpdateNote: (productId: string, note: string) => void;
  onRemoveItem: (productId: string) => void;
  onAddUnlisted: (product: Product, quantity: number, note: string) => void;
  onSubmitOrder: () => void;
  submitting: boolean;
  errorMessage: string;
  earliestDate?: string;
  cutoffTimeStr?: string;
}

export default function CartCheckoutDrawer({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  session,
  activeTab,
  onUpdateTab,
  onUpdateQty,
  onUpdateNote,
  onRemoveItem,
  onAddUnlisted,
  onSubmitOrder,
  submitting,
  errorMessage,
  earliestDate,
  cutoffTimeStr,
}: CartCheckoutDrawerProps) {
  const { t, productName, productUnit } = useLanguage();

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const totalQuantity = activeTab.items.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.quantity, 0);
  const pendingPriceCount = activeTab.items.filter(item => item.product.priceOnRequest || item.product.price <= 0).length;
  const subtotalAmount = activeTab.items.reduce(
    (sum, item) => sum + item.quantity * (item.product.price || 0),
    0
  );

  return (
    <div className="lg:hidden fixed inset-0 z-50 flex flex-col justify-end bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm animate-fade-in">
      {/* Backdrop click area */}
      <div className="flex-1" onClick={onClose} />

      {/* Drawer Container */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-t-3xl max-h-[88vh] flex flex-col shadow-2xl border-t border-[#17231d]/15 animate-slide-up pb-[env(safe-area-inset-bottom)]">
        {/* Drag handle & Header */}
        <div className="p-4 border-b border-[#17231d]/10 flex items-center justify-between">
          <div>
            <div className="w-10 h-1 rounded-full bg-[#17231d]/15 mx-auto mb-2" />
            <h3 className="font-bold text-base text-[#17231d] flex items-center gap-2">
              <span>{activeTab.title.startsWith('Đặt hàng ') ? `${t('Đặt hàng')} ${activeTab.title.slice('Đặt hàng '.length)}` : activeTab.title}</span>
              <span className="text-xs font-normal text-[#59665f]">
                ({activeTab.items.length} món · {totalQuantity.toFixed(1)} ĐVT)
              </span>
            </h3>
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="p-2 rounded-xl text-[#59665f] hover:bg-[#f5f7f3] active:scale-95 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
            aria-label="Đóng bảng giỏ hàng"
          >
            <X size={20} />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Scrollable Content */}
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 space-y-4">
          {/* Danh sách mặt hàng */}
          <div className="space-y-2.5">
            <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-[#59665f] uppercase tracking-wider">
              Danh sách hàng hóa đặt
            </h4>

            {activeTab.items.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="py-6 text-center text-[#59665f] text-xs">
                {t('Chọn hàng từ danh mục hoặc thêm hàng ngoài danh mục.')}
              </div>
            ) : (
              activeTab.items.map((item) => {
                const lineTotal = item.quantity * (item.product.price || 0);
                return (
                  <div
                    key={item.lineKey || item.product.id}
                    className="p-3 rounded-2xl border border-[#17231d]/10 bg-[#fbfcfb] space-y-2"
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2.5">
                      <ProductThumbnail product={item.product} size="sm" />
                      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                        <p className="font-bold text-xs text-[#17231d] leading-snug">
                          {productName(item.product.name)}
                        </p>
                        {productName(item.product.name) !== item.product.name && <p className="text-[10px] text-[#59665f] truncate">{item.product.name}</p>}
                        <p className="text-[11px] text-[#59665f] mt-0.5">
                          {productUnit(item.product.unit || 'Kg')} ·{' '}
                          <strong className="text-[#0f7a4f]">
                            {item.product.isUnlisted ? t('Ngoài danh mục · 0đ · Chờ báo giá') : item.product.priceOnRequest ? t('Chờ sale báo giá') : money(item.product.price)}
                          </strong>
                        </p>
                      </div>

                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => onRemoveItem(item.lineKey || item.product.id)}
                        className="text-[#59665f]/50 hover:text-red-600 p-1.5 rounded-lg active:bg-red-50"
                        title="Xóa món này"
                      >
                        <Trash2 size={16} />
                      </button>
                    </div>

                    {/* Stepper và tiền dòng */}
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-1 border-t border-[#17231d]/5">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-1 bg-[#f5f7f3] p-1 rounded-xl border border-[#17231d]/10">
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() =>
                            onUpdateQty(
                              item.lineKey || item.product.id,
                              Math.max(0, Number((item.quantity - 1).toFixed(2)))
                            )
                          }
                          className="w-11 h-11 rounded-lg bg-white font-bold text-sm text-[#17231d] active:scale-95 flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs"
                        >
                          -
                        </button>
                        <input
                          type="number"
                          min={0.001}
                          step="any"
                          value={item.quantity}
                          onWheel={(e) => (e.currentTarget as HTMLElement).blur()}
                          onChange={(e) => {
                            const val = parseFloat(e.target.value);
                            onUpdateQty(item.lineKey || item.product.id, isNaN(val) ? 0 : val);
                          }}
                          className="w-14 text-center font-bold text-sm bg-transparent focus:outline-none"
                        />
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() =>
                            onUpdateQty(
                              item.lineKey || item.product.id,
                              Number((item.quantity + 1).toFixed(2))
                            )
                          }
                          className="w-11 h-11 rounded-lg bg-white font-bold text-sm text-[#0f7a4f] active:scale-95 flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs"
                        >
                          +
                        </button>
                      </div>

                      <div className="text-right">
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-[#59665f] block">Thành tiền</span>
                        <span className="font-mono font-bold text-sm text-[#0f7a4f]">
                          {item.product.isUnlisted ? '0đ' : item.product.priceOnRequest ? t('Chờ sale báo giá') : money(lineTotal)}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* Ghi chú từng dòng: Enter khóa lại, có nút Sửa */}
                    <LineNoteEditor
                      size="comfortable"
                      note={item.note}
                      onSave={(value) => onUpdateNote(item.lineKey || item.product.id, value)}
                    />
                  </div>
                );
              })
            )}
            <UnlistedItemEntry onAdd={onAddUnlisted} initialOpen={activeTab.items.length === 0}/>
          </div>

          {/* Ghi chú chung */}
          <div className="space-y-1">
            <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[#59665f] uppercase tracking-wider">
              Ghi chú toàn đơn
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={activeTab.note}
              onChange={(e) => onUpdateTab((t) => ({ ...t, note: e.target.value }))}
              placeholder={t('Ghi chú thêm cho bộ phận giao nhận...')}
              className="w-full text-xs py-2 px-3 bg-[#f8faf7] border border-[#17231d]/15 rounded-xl text-[#17231d] focus:outline-none focus:bg-white focus:border-[#0f7a4f]"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Thông tin giao hàng */}
          <div className="border border-[#17231d]/10 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden">
            <div className="w-full p-3 bg-[#f8faf7] flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-bold text-[#17231d]">
              <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <MapPin size={14} className="text-[#0f7a4f]" />
                <span>{t('Địa chỉ và thời gian nhận hàng')}</span>
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="p-3.5 space-y-3 bg-white text-xs">
                {/* Ngày giao & Ca giao */}
                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2.5">
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-[11px] font-bold text-[#59665f] uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
                      Ngày giao
                    </label>
                    <div className="relative">
                      <Calendar className="absolute left-2.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-[#59665f]/60" size={14} />
                      <input
                        type="date"
                        min={earliestDate}
                        value={activeTab.deliveryDate}
                        onChange={(e) => onUpdateTab((t) => ({ ...t, deliveryDate: e.target.value }))}
                        className="w-full pl-8 pr-2.5 py-2 bg-[#f8faf7] border border-[#17231d]/15 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold focus:outline-none focus:bg-white focus:border-[#0f7a4f]"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-[11px] font-bold text-[#59665f] uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
                      Ca giao
                    </label>
                    <div className="relative">
                      <Clock className="absolute left-2.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-[#59665f]/60" size={14} />
                      <select
                        value={activeTab.deliveryShift}
                        onChange={(e) => onUpdateTab((t) => ({ ...t, deliveryShift: e.target.value }))}
                        className="w-full pl-8 pr-2.5 py-2 bg-[#f8faf7] border border-[#17231d]/15 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold focus:outline-none focus:bg-white focus:border-[#0f7a4f]"
                      >
                        <option value="Ca sáng sớm (05:00 - 07:00)">{t('Ca sáng sớm (05:00 - 07:00)')}</option>
                        <option value="Ca sáng (07:00 - 09:00)">{t('Ca sáng (07:00 - 09:00)')}</option>
                        <option value="Ca trưa (09:30 - 11:00)">{t('Ca trưa (09:30 - 11:00)')}</option>
                        <option value="Ca chiều (14:00 - 16:00)">{t('Ca chiều (14:00 - 16:00)')}</option>
                      </select>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Người nhận & SĐT */}
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-[11px] font-bold text-[#59665f] uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
                      Người nhận
                    </label>
                    <div className="relative">
                      <User className="absolute left-2.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-[#59665f]/50" size={13} />
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        value={activeTab.deliveryName}
                        onChange={(e) => onUpdateTab((t) => ({ ...t, deliveryName: e.target.value }))}
                        placeholder={t('Tên người nhận')}
                        className="w-full pl-8 pr-2 py-2 bg-[#f8faf7] border border-[#17231d]/15 rounded-xl text-xs focus:outline-none focus:bg-white focus:border-[#0f7a4f]"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-[11px] font-bold text-[#59665f] uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
                      SĐT nhận
                    </label>
                    <div className="relative">
                      <Phone className="absolute left-2.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-[#59665f]/50" size={13} />
                      <input
                        type="tel"
                        value={activeTab.deliveryPhone}
                        onChange={(e) => onUpdateTab((t) => ({ ...t, deliveryPhone: e.target.value }))}
                        placeholder={t('SĐT người nhận')}
                        className="w-full pl-8 pr-2 py-2 bg-[#f8faf7] border border-[#17231d]/15 rounded-xl text-xs focus:outline-none focus:bg-white focus:border-[#0f7a4f]"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Địa chỉ nhận */}
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[11px] font-bold text-[#59665f] uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
                    Địa chỉ chi tiết
                  </label>
                  <textarea
                    rows={2}
                    value={activeTab.deliveryAddress}
                    onChange={(e) => onUpdateTab((t) => ({ ...t, deliveryAddress: e.target.value }))}
                    placeholder={t('Số nhà, đường, xưởng, bếp ăn...')}
                    className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-[#f8faf7] border border-[#17231d]/15 rounded-xl text-xs focus:outline-none focus:bg-white focus:border-[#0f7a4f] resize-none"
                  />
                </div>

                {session?.defaultShippingAddress?.address && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      const d = session.defaultShippingAddress!;
                      onUpdateTab((t) => ({
                        ...t,
                        deliveryName: d.name || t.deliveryName,
                        deliveryPhone: d.phone || t.deliveryPhone,
                        deliveryAddress: d.address || t.deliveryAddress,
                      }));
                    }}
                    className="text-[11px] text-[#0f7a4f] hover:underline font-semibold flex items-center gap-1"
                  >
                    <MapPin size={12} />
                    <span>{t('Dùng địa chỉ mặc định của công ty')}</span>
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

        {/* Footer: Tổng tiền & Nút Gửi đơn */}
        <div className="p-4 border-t border-[#17231d]/10 bg-[#fbfcfb] space-y-3">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div>
              <span className="text-xs text-[#59665f] block">{t('Tạm tính đơn hàng')}</span>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-[#59665f] flex items-center gap-1">
                <Info size={11} className="text-[#e0742f]" />
                <span>{t('TPS1 sẽ xác nhận lại mặt hàng cần báo giá hoặc có điều chỉnh')}</span>
              </p>
            </div>
            <div className="text-right">
              <span className="text-xl font-black font-mono text-[#0f7a4f]">
                {money(subtotalAmount)}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
          {earliestDate && <p className="text-xs text-[#59665f]">{t('Ngày giao sớm nhất')}: {earliestDate}{cutoffTimeStr ? ` · ${t('Giờ chốt đơn')}: ${cutoffTimeStr}` : ''}</p>}
          {pendingPriceCount > 0 && <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-amber-800 rounded-xl bg-amber-50 p-2">{t('Tạm tính chưa gồm hàng chờ giá')} ({pendingPriceCount}). {t('Sale sẽ nhập giá hoặc bỏ mặt hàng không có hàng trước khi xác nhận đơn.')}</p>}

          {errorMessage && (
            <div className="p-2.5 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-xl text-xs text-red-600 flex items-center gap-2">
              <AlertCircle size={15} className="shrink-0" />
              <span>{errorMessage}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onSubmitOrder}
            disabled={submitting || activeTab.items.length === 0}
            className="w-full py-3.5 rounded-2xl font-bold text-sm tracking-wider bg-[#0f7a4f] hover:bg-[#0b4f34] text-white shadow-lg shadow-[#0f7a4f]/25 active:scale-[0.98] transition-all disabled:opacity-40 disabled:cursor-not-allowed flex items-center justify-center gap-2 uppercase touch-target"
          >
            {submitting ? (
              <>
                <span className="w-4 h-4 border-2 border-white/30 border-t-white rounded-full animate-spin" />
                <span>{t('Đang gửi đơn hàng...')}</span>
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <span>{t('Gửi đơn đặt hàng')}</span>
                <ArrowRight size={16} />
              </>
            )}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

📄 2. FULL SOURCE CODE CHUẨN PRODUCTION 100% (71 dòng) — Bản hoàn chỉnh của order-webapp/src/components/products/CartFloatingBar.tsx 
import { ShoppingBag, ArrowRight, Plus } from 'lucide-react';
import type { OrderTab } from './OrderTabsBar';
import { useLanguage } from '../../contexts/LanguageContext';

function money(v: number) {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat('vi-VN').format(Number(v) || 0) + 'đ';
}

interface CartFloatingBarProps {
  activeTab: OrderTab;
  onOpenDrawer: () => void;
}

export default function CartFloatingBar({ activeTab, onOpenDrawer }: CartFloatingBarProps) {
  const { t } = useLanguage();
  const count = activeTab.items.length;
  if (count === 0) return (
    <div className="lg:hidden fixed bottom-[calc(3.75rem+env(safe-area-inset-bottom,0px)+8px)] md:bottom-4 inset-x-3 z-20 pointer-events-none">
      <button type="button" onClick={onOpenDrawer} className="pointer-events-auto flex min-h-12 w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-[#0b4f34] px-3 text-sm font-bold text-white shadow-lg">
        <Plus size={18}/>{t('Thêm sản phẩm ngoài danh mục')}
      </button>
    </div>
  );

  const totalQuantity = activeTab.items.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.quantity, 0);
  const subtotal = activeTab.items.reduce(
    (sum, item) => sum + item.quantity * (item.product.price || 0),
    0
  );

  return (
    <div
      className="lg:hidden fixed bottom-[calc(3.75rem+env(safe-area-inset-bottom,0px)+8px)] md:bottom-4 inset-x-3 z-20 pointer-events-none"
    >
      <div
        onClick={onOpenDrawer}
        className="pointer-events-auto bg-[#0b4f34] text-white rounded-2xl p-3 px-4 shadow-2xl shadow-black/30 border border-white/20 flex items-center justify-between gap-3 active:scale-[0.99] transition-transform cursor-pointer"
      >
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-white/15 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 text-white relative">
            <ShoppingBag size={20} />
            <span className="absolute -top-1.5 -right-1.5 bg-[#e0742f] text-white text-[10px] font-mono font-bold w-5 h-5 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center shadow">
              {count}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="min-w-0">
            <p className="font-bold text-sm leading-tight flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <span>{activeTab.title.startsWith('Đặt hàng ') ? `${t('Đặt hàng')} ${activeTab.title.slice('Đặt hàng '.length)}` : activeTab.title}</span>
              <span className="text-[11px] font-normal text-emerald-200/80">
                ({totalQuantity.toFixed(1)} ĐVT)
              </span>
            </p>
            <p className="font-mono font-extrabold text-sm text-emerald-200">
              {money(subtotal)}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <button
          type="button"
          className="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white text-[#0b4f34] font-bold text-xs flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0 shadow-sm"
        >
          <span>{t('Xem & Gửi đơn')}</span>
          <ArrowRight size={14} />
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
