import { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
  Calendar,
  Clock,
  MapPin,
  User,
  Phone,
  Trash2,
  AlertCircle,
  ArrowRight,
  ShoppingBag,
  Info,
} from 'lucide-react';
import type { CustomerSession, Product } from '../../lib/api';
import type { OrderTab } from './OrderTabsBar';
import ProductThumbnail from './ProductThumbnail';
import UnlistedItemEntry from './UnlistedItemEntry';
import LineNoteEditor from './LineNoteEditor';
import { useLanguage } from '../../contexts/LanguageContext';

function money(v: number) {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat('vi-VN').format(Number(v) || 0) + 'đ';
}

interface CartSummarySidebarProps {
  session: CustomerSession | null;
  activeTab: OrderTab;
  onUpdateTab: (updater: (prev: OrderTab) => OrderTab) => void;
  onUpdateQty: (productId: string, qty: number) => void;
  /** Có truyền => hiện ô ghi chú từng dòng (Enter khóa + nút Sửa). */
  onUpdateNote?: (productId: string, note: string) => void;
  onRemoveItem: (productId: string) => void;
  onAddUnlisted: (product: Product, quantity: number, note: string) => void;
  onSubmitOrder: () => void;
  submitting: boolean;
  errorMessage: string;
  earliestDate?: string;
  cutoffTimeStr?: string;
  /** Khách chưa đăng nhập: ẩn thông tin nhận hàng, nút chính chuyển sang đăng nhập. */
  guest?: boolean;
  /** false khi đặt trong trang giỏ hàng (không cần dính khi cuộn). */
  sticky?: boolean;
  /** false khi trang đã hiện danh sách hàng riêng (trang Giỏ hàng). */
  showItems?: boolean;
  /** Chiều cao đồng bộ với danh sách sản phẩm bên trái trên Desktop để chống lệch OCD */
  matchedHeight?: number;
}

export default function CartSummarySidebar({
  session,
  activeTab,
  onUpdateTab,
  onUpdateQty,
  onUpdateNote,
  onRemoveItem,
  onAddUnlisted,
  onSubmitOrder,
  submitting,
  errorMessage,
  earliestDate,
  cutoffTimeStr,
  guest = false,
  sticky = true,
  showItems = true,
  matchedHeight,
}: CartSummarySidebarProps) {
  const { t, productName, productUnit } = useLanguage();
  const sidebarRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  const itemsListRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  const [isFillingInfo, setIsFillingInfo] = useState(false);

  // Lắng nghe sự kiện lăn chuột trên sidebar: Chỉ khóa cuộn trang khi đang thực sự điền thông tin
  useEffect(() => {
    const sidebarEl = sidebarRef.current;
    if (!sidebarEl) return;

    const handleWheel = (e: WheelEvent) => {
      const itemsEl = itemsListRef.current;

      // Khi người dùng ĐANG ĐIỀN THÔNG TIN trong input/textarea/select: Khóa cuộn trang
      if (isFillingInfo) {
        if (itemsEl && itemsEl.contains(e.target as Node)) {
          const { scrollTop, scrollHeight, clientHeight } = itemsEl;
          const canScroll = scrollHeight > clientHeight;
          if (!canScroll) {
            e.preventDefault();
            return;
          }
          if (
            (scrollTop <= 0 && e.deltaY < 0) ||
            (scrollTop + clientHeight >= scrollHeight - 1 && e.deltaY > 0)
          ) {
            e.preventDefault();
          }
        } else {
          e.preventDefault();
        }
        return;
      }

      // Khi KHÔNG điền thông tin: Rê chuột vào khung vẫn cuộn trang web mượt mà tự nhiên
    };

    sidebarEl.addEventListener('wheel', handleWheel, { passive: false });
    return () => sidebarEl.removeEventListener('wheel', handleWheel);
  }, [isFillingInfo]);

  const totalItemsCount = activeTab.items.length;
  const totalQuantity = activeTab.items.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.quantity, 0);
  const pendingPriceCount = activeTab.items.filter(item => item.product.priceOnRequest || item.product.price <= 0).length;
  const subtotalAmount = activeTab.items.reduce(
    (sum, item) => sum + item.quantity * (item.product.price || 0),
    0
  );

  return (
    <aside className={`space-y-3 ${sticky ? 'sticky top-34 z-20' : ''}`}>
      <div
        ref={sidebarRef}
        className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-[#17231d]/12 shadow-sm overflow-hidden flex flex-col"
        style={matchedHeight ? { minHeight: matchedHeight } : undefined}
        onFocusCapture={(e) => {
          const tag = (e.target as HTMLElement)?.tagName;
          if (tag === 'INPUT' || tag === 'TEXTAREA' || tag === 'SELECT') {
            setIsFillingInfo(true);
          }
        }}
        onBlurCapture={(e) => {
          const next = e.relatedTarget as HTMLElement | null;
          const nextTag = next?.tagName;
          if (
            !next ||
            !e.currentTarget.contains(next) ||
            (nextTag !== 'INPUT' && nextTag !== 'TEXTAREA' && nextTag !== 'SELECT')
          ) {
            setIsFillingInfo(false);
          }
        }}
      >
        {/* 1. HEADER (shrink-0): Tab title & Item Count */}
        <div className="p-3.5 sm:p-4 border-b border-[#17231d]/10 bg-linear-to-r from-[#0b4f34]/5 to-transparent flex items-center justify-between shrink-0">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-[#0f7a4f] text-white flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
              <ShoppingBag size={16} />
            </span>
            <div>
              <h3 className="font-bold text-sm text-[#17231d] leading-tight">
                {activeTab.title.startsWith('Đặt hàng ') ? `${t('Đặt hàng')} ${activeTab.title.slice('Đặt hàng '.length)}` : activeTab.title}
              </h3>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-[#2c3a33] font-semibold">
                {totalItemsCount} {t('mặt hàng')} ({totalQuantity.toFixed(1)} {t('ĐVT')})
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <span className="font-mono font-bold text-sm text-[#0f7a4f]">
            {money(subtotalAmount)}
          </span>
        </div>

        {/* 2. BODY: Danh sách món, thêm ngoài danh mục, ghi chú, giao nhận */}
        <div className="flex-1 divide-y divide-[#17231d]/8">
          {/* Danh sách các món trong đơn (scrollable khi >= 2 món) */}
          {showItems && (
            <div
              ref={itemsListRef}
              className={`divide-y divide-[#17231d]/8 max-h-[165px] overflow-y-auto px-2.5 py-1.5 space-y-1.5 scroll-smooth ${
                isFillingInfo ? 'overscroll-contain' : ''
              }`}
            >
              {activeTab.items.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="py-6 text-center text-[#2c3a33] font-semibold space-y-1">
                  <p className="text-xs font-bold text-[#17231d]">{t('Đơn hàng này chưa có món')}</p>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-[#2c3a33] font-medium">{t('Chọn hàng từ danh mục hoặc thêm hàng ngoài danh mục.')}</p>
                </div>
              ) : (
                activeTab.items.map((item) => {
                  const lineTotal = item.quantity * (item.product.price || 0);
                  return (
                    <div
                      key={item.lineKey || item.product.id}
                      className="p-2 rounded-xl border border-[#17231d]/8 bg-[#fbfcfb] hover:bg-[#f4f8f5] transition-colors space-y-1.5"
                    >
                      {/* Hàng 1: Ảnh, Tên + ĐVT/Đơn giá, Stepper nằm cạnh, Nút Xóa */}
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2.5">
                        <ProductThumbnail product={item.product} size="sm" className="w-10 h-10 rounded-lg shrink-0" />
                        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1 pr-1">
                          <p className="font-bold text-xs text-[#17231d] leading-snug line-clamp-1" title={item.product.name}>
                            {productName(item.product.name)}
                          </p>
                          <p className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#2c3a33] mt-0.5">
                            {productUnit(item.product.unit || 'Kg')} ·{' '}
                            <span className={item.product.priceOnRequest ? 'text-[#e0742f]' : 'text-[#0f7a4f]'}>
                              {item.product.isUnlisted ? t('Ngoài danh mục') : item.product.priceOnRequest ? t('Chờ báo giá') : money(item.product.price)}
                            </span>
                          </p>
                        </div>

                        {/* Bộ tăng giảm số lượng nằm ngay kế bên tên sản phẩm */}
                        <div className="shrink-0 flex items-center gap-0.5 bg-white p-0.5 rounded-lg border border-[#17231d]/15 shadow-2xs">
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() =>
                              onUpdateQty(
                                item.lineKey || item.product.id,
                                Math.max(0, Number((item.quantity - 1).toFixed(2)))
                              )
                            }
                            aria-label="Giảm"
                            className="w-6 h-6 rounded-md bg-[#f5f7f3] font-bold text-xs text-[#17231d] hover:bg-rose-50 hover:text-rose-600 flex items-center justify-center cursor-pointer select-none"
                          >
                            -
                          </button>
                          <input
                            type="number"
                            min={0.001}
                            step="any"
                            value={item.quantity}
                            onWheel={(e) => (e.currentTarget as HTMLElement).blur()}
                            onChange={(e) => {
                              const v = parseFloat(e.target.value);
                              onUpdateQty(item.lineKey || item.product.id, isNaN(v) ? 0 : v);
                            }}
                            className="w-9 text-center font-extrabold text-xs text-[#0b4f34] bg-transparent focus:outline-none [appearance:textfield] [&::-webkit-outer-spin-button]:appearance-none [&::-webkit-inner-spin-button]:appearance-none"
                          />
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() =>
                              onUpdateQty(
                                item.lineKey || item.product.id,
                                Number((item.quantity + 1).toFixed(2))
                              )
                            }
                            aria-label="Tăng"
                            className="w-6 h-6 rounded-md bg-[#0f7a4f] font-bold text-xs text-white hover:bg-[#0b4f34] flex items-center justify-center cursor-pointer select-none"
                          >
                            +
                          </button>
                        </div>

                        {/* Nút Xóa */}
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => onRemoveItem(item.lineKey || item.product.id)}
                          className="text-[#59665f] hover:text-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-50 p-1 rounded-lg transition-colors cursor-pointer shrink-0"
                          title={t('Xóa món')}
                        >
                          <Trash2 size={14} />
                        </button>
                      </div>

                      {/* Hàng 2: Ghi chú sơ chế & Thành tiền dòng */}
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 pt-1 border-t border-dashed border-[#17231d]/10">
                        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                          {item.note || onUpdateNote ? (
                            onUpdateNote ? (
                              <LineNoteEditor note={item.note} onSave={(value) => onUpdateNote(item.lineKey || item.product.id, value)} />
                            ) : (
                              <p className="text-[10px] text-[#0f7a4f] bg-[#0f7a4f]/5 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-[#0f7a4f]/15 italic">
                                📝 {item.note}
                              </p>
                            )
                          ) : null}
                        </div>
                        <div className="shrink-0 font-mono font-extrabold text-xs text-[#0f7a4f]">
                          {item.product.isUnlisted ? '0đ' : item.product.priceOnRequest ? t('Chờ báo giá') : money(lineTotal)}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  );
                })
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {showItems && <div className="p-2.5"><UnlistedItemEntry onAdd={onAddUnlisted}/></div>}

          {/* Ghi chú chung cho toàn bộ đơn hàng */}
          <div className="p-3 bg-[#fbfcfb]">
            <input
              type="text"
              value={activeTab.note}
              onChange={(e) => onUpdateTab((t) => ({ ...t, note: e.target.value }))}
              onKeyDown={(e) => {
                if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                  e.preventDefault();
                  e.currentTarget.blur();
                }
              }}
              placeholder={t('Ghi chú đơn (sơ chế, giao sớm, chia bao...)')}
              className="w-full bg-white border border-[#17231d]/15 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs text-[#17231d] placeholder:text-[#59665f]/60 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0f7a4f]"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Thông tin giao nhận */}
          {!guest && (
            <div>
              <div className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-[#f8faf7] flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-[#17231d] border-b border-[#17231d]/8">
                <MapPin size={14} className="text-[#0f7a4f]" />
                <span>{t('Thông tin nhận hàng')}</span>
              </div>

              <div className="p-3.5 space-y-3 bg-white text-xs">
                  {/* Ngày & Ca giao hàng */}
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-[11px] font-bold text-[#2c3a33] uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
                      {t('Ngày giao mong muốn')}
                    </label>
                    <div className="relative">
                      <Calendar
                        className="absolute left-2.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-[#59665f]/60"
                        size={14}
                      />
                      <input
                        type="date"
                        min={earliestDate}
                        value={activeTab.deliveryDate}
                        onChange={(e) => onUpdateTab((t) => ({ ...t, deliveryDate: e.target.value }))}
                        className="w-full pl-8 pr-2.5 py-1.5 bg-[#f8faf7] border border-[#17231d]/15 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold focus:outline-none focus:bg-white focus:border-[#0f7a4f]"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-[11px] font-bold text-[#2c3a33] uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
                      {t('Khung giờ / Ca giao')}
                    </label>
                    <div className="relative">
                      <Clock
                        className="absolute left-2.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-[#59665f]/60"
                        size={14}
                      />
                      <select
                        value={activeTab.deliveryShift}
                        onChange={(e) => onUpdateTab((t) => ({ ...t, deliveryShift: e.target.value }))}
                        className="w-full pl-8 pr-2.5 py-1.5 bg-[#f8faf7] border border-[#17231d]/15 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold focus:outline-none focus:bg-white focus:border-[#0f7a4f] cursor-pointer"
                      >
                        <option value="Ca sáng sớm (05:00 - 07:00)">{t('Ca sáng sớm (05:00 - 07:00)')}</option>
                        <option value="Ca sáng (07:00 - 09:00)">{t('Ca sáng (07:00 - 09:00)')}</option>
                        <option value="Ca trưa (09:30 - 11:00)">{t('Ca trưa (09:30 - 11:00)')}</option>
                        <option value="Ca chiều (14:00 - 16:00)">{t('Ca chiều (14:00 - 16:00)')}</option>
                      </select>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Tên & SĐT người nhận */}
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-[11px] font-bold text-[#2c3a33] uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
                        {t('Người nhận')}
                      </label>
                      <div className="relative">
                        <User className="absolute left-2 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-[#59665f]/50" size={13} />
                        <input
                          type="text"
                          value={activeTab.deliveryName}
                          onChange={(e) => onUpdateTab((t) => ({ ...t, deliveryName: e.target.value }))}
                          placeholder={t('Tên người nhận')}
                          className="w-full pl-7 pr-2 py-1.5 bg-[#f8faf7] border border-[#17231d]/15 rounded-xl text-xs focus:outline-none focus:bg-white focus:border-[#0f7a4f]"
                        />
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-[11px] font-bold text-[#2c3a33] uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
                        {t('SĐT nhận')}
                      </label>
                      <div className="relative">
                        <Phone className="absolute left-2 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-[#59665f]/50" size={13} />
                        <input
                          type="tel"
                          value={activeTab.deliveryPhone}
                          onChange={(e) => onUpdateTab((t) => ({ ...t, deliveryPhone: e.target.value }))}
                          placeholder={t('SĐT người nhận')}
                          className="w-full pl-7 pr-2 py-1.5 bg-[#f8faf7] border border-[#17231d]/15 rounded-xl text-xs focus:outline-none focus:bg-white focus:border-[#0f7a4f]"
                        />
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Địa chỉ giao hàng */}
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-[11px] font-bold text-[#2c3a33] uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
                      {t('Địa chỉ giao hàng')}
                    </label>
                    <textarea
                      rows={2}
                      value={activeTab.deliveryAddress}
                      onChange={(e) => onUpdateTab((t) => ({ ...t, deliveryAddress: e.target.value }))}
                      placeholder={t('Số nhà, đường, xưởng, bếp ăn...')}
                      className="w-full px-2.5 py-1.5 bg-[#f8faf7] border border-[#17231d]/15 rounded-xl text-xs focus:outline-none focus:bg-white focus:border-[#0f7a4f] resize-none"
                    />
                  </div>

                  {/* Nút dùng địa chỉ mặc định */}
                  {session?.defaultShippingAddress?.address && (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => {
                        const d = session.defaultShippingAddress!;
                        onUpdateTab((t) => ({
                          ...t,
                          deliveryName: d.name || t.deliveryName,
                          deliveryPhone: d.phone || t.deliveryPhone,
                          deliveryAddress: d.address || t.deliveryAddress,
                        }));
                      }}
                      className="text-[11px] text-[#0f7a4f] hover:underline font-semibold flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer"
                    >
                      <MapPin size={12} />
                      <span>{t('Dùng địa chỉ giao mặc định')}</span>
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
        </div>

        {/* 3. FOOTER CỐ ĐỊNH Ở ĐÁY (shrink-0 mt-auto): Tổng tiền, Tạm tính, Lưu ý, Nút Đặt hàng */}
        <div className="p-3.5 bg-[#fbfcfb] border-t border-[#17231d]/10 space-y-2.5 shrink-0 mt-auto">
          <div className="space-y-1">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-[#2c3a33] font-semibold">
              <span>{t('Tổng tiền hàng:')}</span>
              <span className="font-mono font-bold text-[#17231d]">{money(subtotalAmount)}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-1 border-t border-[#17231d]/10">
              <span className="text-sm font-bold text-[#17231d]">{t('Tạm tính:')}</span>
              <span className="text-xl font-black font-mono text-[#0f7a4f]">
                {money(subtotalAmount)}
              </span>
            </div>
            <p className="text-[10px] text-[#59665f] flex items-center gap-1 leading-tight pt-0.5">
              <Info size={12} className="text-[#e0742f] shrink-0" />
              <span>{guest
                ? t('Đây là giá tham khảo. Sau khi đăng nhập, giỏ hàng tự cập nhật theo giá hợp đồng của quý khách.')
                : t('Giá đang theo bảng giá hiện hành. Mặt hàng nào cần báo giá, TPS1 sẽ xác nhận lại với quý khách.')}</span>
            </p>
            {!guest && earliestDate && <p className="text-xs text-[#2c3a33] font-semibold">{t('Ngày giao sớm nhất')}: {earliestDate}{cutoffTimeStr ? ` · ${t('Giờ chốt')}: ${cutoffTimeStr}` : ''}</p>}
            {pendingPriceCount > 0 && (
              <p className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-amber-900 rounded-lg bg-amber-50 p-2 leading-snug border border-amber-200">
                {t('Tạm tính chưa gồm hàng chờ giá')} ({pendingPriceCount}). {t('Kinh doanh sẽ báo giá trước khi xác nhận đơn.')}
              </p>
            )}
          </div>

          {errorMessage && (
            <div className="p-2 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-xl text-xs text-red-600 flex items-center gap-2">
              <AlertCircle size={14} className="shrink-0" />
              <span>{errorMessage}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onSubmitOrder}
            disabled={submitting || totalItemsCount === 0}
            className="w-full py-3 rounded-xl font-bold text-sm tracking-wider bg-[#0f7a4f] hover:bg-[#0b4f34] text-white shadow-lg shadow-[#0f7a4f]/25 active:scale-[0.98] transition-all disabled:opacity-40 disabled:cursor-not-allowed flex items-center justify-center gap-2 cursor-pointer touch-target uppercase"
          >
            {submitting ? (
              <>
                <span className="w-4 h-4 border-2 border-white/30 border-t-white rounded-full animate-spin" />
                <span>{t('Đang gửi đơn...')}</span>
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <span>{guest ? t('Đăng nhập để đặt hàng') : t('Xác nhận đặt hàng')}</span>
                <ArrowRight size={16} />
              </>
            )}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </aside>
  );
}

📄 2. FULL SOURCE CODE CHUẨN PRODUCTION 100% (290 dòng) — Bản hoàn chỉnh của order-webapp/src/components/products/ProductCard.tsx 
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Plus, Minus, Heart, MessageSquarePlus, Check } from 'lucide-react';
import type { Product } from '../../lib/api';
import ProductThumbnail from './ProductThumbnail';
import { useLanguage } from '../../contexts/LanguageContext';

function money(v: number) {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat('vi-VN').format(Number(v) || 0) + 'đ';
}

const NO_SPINNER =
  '[appearance:textfield] [&::-webkit-outer-spin-button]:appearance-none [&::-webkit-inner-spin-button]:appearance-none';

interface ProductCardProps {
  product: Product;
  quantityInCart: number;
  itemNote?: string;
  isFavorite: boolean;
  onToggleFavorite: (productId: string) => void;
  onAdd: (product: Product, qty: number) => void;
  onUpdateQty: (productId: string, qty: number) => void;
  onUpdateNote: (productId: string, note: string) => void;
}

export default function ProductCard({
  product,
  quantityInCart,
  itemNote = '',
  isFavorite,
  onToggleFavorite,
  onAdd,
  onUpdateQty,
  onUpdateNote,
}: ProductCardProps) {
  const { t, productName, productUnit } = useLanguage();
  const [isEditingNote, setIsEditingNote] = useState(false);
  const [tempNote, setTempNote] = useState(itemNote);
  const [draftQuantity, setDraftQuantity] = useState('1');

  useEffect(() => {
    setTempNote(itemNote);
  }, [itemNote]);

  const isInCart = quantityInCart > 0;

  // Rời khỏi giỏ => đóng chế độ sửa ghi chú
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isInCart) setIsEditingNote(false);
  }, [isInCart]);

  const handleNoteSave = () => {
    onUpdateNote(product.id, tempNote.trim());
    setIsEditingNote(false);
  };

  const unitLabel = productUnit(product.unit || 'Kg');

  return (
    <div
      className={`relative bg-white rounded-2xl border transition-all duration-200 flex flex-col justify-between h-full overflow-hidden group ${
        isInCart
          ? 'border-[#0f7a4f]/40 shadow-sm shadow-[#0f7a4f]/5 ring-1 ring-[#0f7a4f]/20 bg-linear-to-b from-white to-[#f5fbf7]'
          : 'border-[#17231d]/10 hover:border-[#0f7a4f]/30 hover:shadow-md'
      }`}
    >
      {/* PHẦN TRÊN */}
      <div className="p-2 sm:p-3.5 flex-1 flex flex-col min-w-0">
        {/* TẦNG 1: Khung thông tin đầu thẻ */}
        <div className="min-w-0">
          {/* Hàng trên Tầng 1: Ảnh + Mã SKU + ĐVT + Nút tim */}
          <div className="relative flex items-start gap-1.5 sm:gap-2 min-h-10.5 sm:min-h-13">
            {/* Nút yêu thích góc trên phải */}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onToggleFavorite(product.id)}
              className="absolute -right-1 -top-1 z-10 p-0.5 sm:p-1 text-[#59665f]/60 hover:text-rose-500 rounded-lg hover:bg-rose-50 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              title={isFavorite ? t('Bỏ yêu thích') : t('Thêm vào món yêu thích của bếp')}
              aria-label={`${t(isFavorite ? 'Bỏ yêu thích' : 'Thêm vào món yêu thích của bếp')} ${productName(product.name)}`}
            >
              <Heart size={14} className={isFavorite ? 'fill-rose-500 text-rose-500' : ''} />
            </button>

            {/* Ảnh đại diện sản phẩm */}
            <ProductThumbnail product={product} size="md" className="w-10 h-10 sm:w-13 sm:h-13 rounded-xl shrink-0" />

            {/* Cụm bên phải ảnh: SKU & ĐVT */}
            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1 pr-4 sm:pr-5 flex flex-col justify-center gap-1">
              {/* Dòng 1: Mã SKU (không dùng truncate để không bị ... theo yêu cầu) */}
              <div className="min-w-0">
                {product.sku ? (
                  <span className="inline-block text-[8px] sm:text-[9.5px] font-sans font-bold tracking-tighter sm:tracking-tight text-[#17231d] bg-[#eaf0ec] px-1 sm:px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded leading-none whitespace-nowrap">
                    {product.sku}
                  </span>
                ) : (
                  <span className="text-[10px] text-[#8a9690] leading-none">—</span>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* Dòng 2: Đơn vị tính */}
              <div className="text-[10px] sm:text-[10.5px] text-[#2c3a33] font-semibold leading-none whitespace-nowrap">
                {t('ĐVT:')} <strong className="text-[#17231d] font-bold">{unitLabel}</strong>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Hàng dưới Tầng 1: Nhóm hàng + Trạng thái chọn hàng full chiều ngang thẻ */}
          <div className="mt-1.5 space-y-1 min-w-0">
            {/* Dòng 3: Phân nhóm hàng */}
            <div className="h-4 flex items-center text-[9.5px] sm:text-[10.5px] tracking-tight text-[#2c3a33] font-semibold leading-none whitespace-nowrap">
              {t('Nhóm:')} <span className="ml-1 font-bold text-[#17231d]">{product.category ? productName(product.category) : t('Khác')}</span>
            </div>

            {/* Dòng 4: Trạng thái chọn hàng */}
            <div className="h-4.5 flex items-center">
              {isInCart ? (
                <span className="inline-flex items-center text-[9.5px] sm:text-[10px] font-bold text-[#0f7a4f] bg-[#0f7a4f]/12 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full leading-none whitespace-nowrap">
                  {t('Đã chọn:')} {quantityInCart} {unitLabel}
                </span>
              ) : (
                <span className="text-[9.5px] sm:text-[10px] text-[#0f7a4f] font-semibold leading-none whitespace-nowrap">
                  {t('Sẵn hàng giao bếp')}
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* TẦNG 2: Tên sản phẩm khóa chuẩn 2 dòng h-9 sm:h-10 */}
        <div className="mt-2">
          <h3
            className="font-bold text-[12.5px] sm:text-sm text-[#17231d] leading-4.5 sm:leading-5 line-clamp-2 h-9 sm:h-10 overflow-hidden"
            title={product.name}
          >
            {productName(product.name)}
          </h3>
        </div>

        {/* TẦNG 3: Giá / Chờ sale báo giá khóa chuẩn h-7.5 sm:h-8.5 */}
        <div className="mt-1.5 pt-1.5 border-t border-dashed border-[#17231d]/10 h-7.5 sm:h-8.5 flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-baseline min-w-0">
            {product.priceOnRequest ? (
              <span className="text-[#e0742f] text-[11.5px] sm:text-[13px] font-extrabold whitespace-nowrap">
                {t('Chờ sale báo giá')}
              </span>
            ) : (
              <span className="text-[#0f7a4f] text-[13.5px] sm:text-base font-extrabold whitespace-nowrap">
                {money(product.price)}
              </span>
            )}
            <span className="text-[10px] sm:text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#17231d]/80 ml-0.5 sm:ml-1 whitespace-nowrap">/{unitLabel}</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Hàng 4 (TẦNG 4): Ghi chú quy cách sơ chế */}
        {isInCart && (
          <div className="mt-2 min-h-8 flex items-center">
            {isEditingNote ? (
              <div className="w-full flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <input
                  type="text"
                  autoFocus
                  value={tempNote}
                  onChange={(e) => setTempNote(e.target.value)}
                  onKeyDown={(e) => {
                    if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                      e.preventDefault();
                      handleNoteSave();
                      e.currentTarget.blur();
                    } else if (e.key === 'Escape') {
                      setTempNote(itemNote);
                      setIsEditingNote(false);
                    }
                  }}
                  placeholder={t('Vd: Sơ chế sẵn, thái lát mỏng...')}
                  className="min-w-0 flex-1 text-xs py-1 px-2.5 bg-[#f8faf7] border border-[#0f7a4f]/40 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0f7a4f] focus:bg-white text-[#17231d]"
                />
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleNoteSave}
                  className="shrink-0 p-1.5 text-[#0f7a4f] bg-[#0f7a4f]/10 rounded-lg hover:bg-[#0f7a4f]/20 cursor-pointer"
                  title={t('Lưu ghi chú')}
                  aria-label={t('Lưu ghi chú')}
                >
                  <Check size={14} />
                </button>
              </div>
            ) : itemNote ? (
              <div className="w-full flex items-center justify-between gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-[#0f7a4f]/25 bg-[#eaf6ef]/70 px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] text-[#0b4f34]">
                <span className="truncate font-medium" title={itemNote}>📝 {itemNote}</span>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setIsEditingNote(true)}
                  className="shrink-0 font-bold text-[#0f7a4f] hover:underline cursor-pointer"
                >
                  {t('Sửa')}
                </button>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setIsEditingNote(true)}
                className="text-[11px] text-[#17231d]/85 hover:text-[#0f7a4f] flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer font-semibold"
              >
                <MessageSquarePlus size={13} className="shrink-0" />
                <span>+ {t('Thêm quy cách / ghi chú')}</span>
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* PHẦN DƯỚI: Nút Thêm hoặc Stepper */}
      <div className="p-2 sm:p-3 pt-2 sm:pt-3 border-t border-[#17231d]/5 mt-auto bg-white/60">
        {!isInCart ? (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 sm:gap-2 min-w-0">
            <div className="flex h-9 sm:h-11 min-w-0 flex-1 items-center justify-between gap-0.5 rounded-xl border border-[#17231d]/12 bg-[#f8faf7] px-0.5 sm:px-1 overflow-hidden">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setDraftQuantity((value) => String(Math.max(0.1, (Number(value) || 1) - 1)))}
                aria-label={t('Giảm số lượng')}
                className="flex size-6.5 sm:size-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg text-[#466253] hover:bg-white cursor-pointer"
              >
                <Minus size={14} strokeWidth={2.5} />
              </button>
              <input
                type="number"
                min="0.1"
                step="any"
                value={draftQuantity}
                onChange={(e) => setDraftQuantity(e.target.value)}
                aria-label={`Số lượng ${product.name}`}
                className={`w-full min-w-0 flex-1 bg-transparent text-center text-xs sm:text-sm font-extrabold text-[#17382a] outline-none ${NO_SPINNER}`}
              />
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setDraftQuantity((value) => String((Number(value) || 0) + 1))}
                aria-label={t('Tăng số lượng')}
                className="flex size-6.5 sm:size-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg text-[#0f7a4f] hover:bg-white cursor-pointer"
              >
                <Plus size={14} strokeWidth={2.5} />
              </button>
            </div>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onAdd(product, Math.max(0.1, Number(draftQuantity) || 1))}
              aria-label={t('Thêm vào đơn')}
              className="flex h-9 w-9 sm:h-11 sm:w-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-[#0f7a4f] text-white shadow-sm hover:bg-[#0b4f34] cursor-pointer"
            >
              <Plus size={18} className="sm:size-5" />
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="flex h-9 sm:h-10 w-full min-w-0 items-center justify-between gap-1 rounded-xl border border-[#0f7a4f]/25 bg-[#f3f8f5] p-1 overflow-hidden">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onUpdateQty(product.id, Math.max(0, Number((quantityInCart - 1).toFixed(2))))}
              className="flex size-7 sm:size-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-white border border-[#17231d]/10 text-[#17231d] hover:bg-rose-50 hover:text-rose-600 active:scale-95 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              aria-label={t('Giảm số lượng')}
            >
              <Minus size={13} strokeWidth={2.5} />
            </button>

            <input
              type="number"
              min={0}
              step="any"
              value={quantityInCart}
              onChange={(e) => {
                const val = parseFloat(e.target.value);
                onUpdateQty(product.id, isNaN(val) ? 0 : Math.max(0, val));
              }}
              className={`w-full min-w-0 flex-1 text-center font-extrabold text-xs sm:text-base text-[#0b4f34] bg-transparent focus:outline-none ${NO_SPINNER}`}
              aria-label={`Số lượng ${product.name}`}
            />

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onUpdateQty(product.id, Number((quantityInCart + 1).toFixed(2)))}
              className="flex size-7 sm:size-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-[#0f7a4f] text-white hover:bg-[#0b4f34] active:scale-95 transition-colors cursor-pointer shadow-xs"
              aria-label={t('Tăng số lượng')}
            >
              <Plus size={13} strokeWidth={2.5} />
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

📄 2. FULL SOURCE CODE CHUẨN PRODUCTION 100% (100 dòng) — Bản hoàn chỉnh của order-webapp/src/components/products/ProductGrid.tsx 
import { Search } from 'lucide-react';
import type { Product } from '../../lib/api';
import ProductCard from './ProductCard';
import type { OrderItem } from './OrderTabsBar';
import { useLanguage } from '../../contexts/LanguageContext';

interface ProductGridProps {
  products: Product[];
  itemsInCart: OrderItem[];
  favoriteIds: string[];
  onToggleFavorite: (productId: string) => void;
  onAddProduct: (product: Product, qty: number) => void;
  onUpdateQty: (productId: string, qty: number) => void;
  onUpdateNote: (productId: string, note: string) => void;
  searchQuery?: string;
  loading?: boolean;
}

export default function ProductGrid({
  products,
  itemsInCart,
  favoriteIds,
  onToggleFavorite,
  onAddProduct,
  onUpdateQty,
  onUpdateNote,
  searchQuery = '',
  loading = false,
}: ProductGridProps) {
  const { t } = useLanguage();
  // Tạo lookup map cho các mặt hàng đã có trong giỏ của tab hiện tại
  const cartMap = new Map<string, { quantity: number; note?: string }>();
  itemsInCart.forEach((item) => {
    cartMap.set(item.product.id, { quantity: item.quantity, note: item.note });
  });

  if (loading && products.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3 2xl:grid-cols-4 gap-2 sm:gap-3.5" aria-label="Đang tải hàng hóa">
        {Array.from({ length: 8 }).map((_, index) => (
          <div key={index} className="h-48 rounded-2xl border border-[#17231d]/10 bg-white p-4 animate-pulse">
            <div className="flex gap-3">
              <div className="h-16 w-16 rounded-xl bg-[#eaece8]" />
              <div className="flex-1 space-y-2">
                <div className="h-3 w-1/3 rounded bg-[#eaece8]" />
                <div className="h-4 w-4/5 rounded bg-[#eaece8]" />
                <div className="h-3 w-1/2 rounded bg-[#eaece8]" />
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="mt-5 h-10 rounded-xl bg-[#eaece8]" />
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (products.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-[#17231d]/10 p-12 text-center text-[#59665f] space-y-3">
        <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl bg-[#f5f7f3] flex items-center justify-center mx-auto text-[#59665f]/50">
          <Search size={26} />
        </div>
        <div>
          <p className="font-bold text-base text-[#17231d]">{t('Không tìm thấy mặt hàng phù hợp')}</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-[#59665f] mt-1 max-w-sm mx-auto">
            {searchQuery
              ? `Không có kết quả nào cho "${searchQuery}". Hãy thử tìm bằng từ khóa đơn giản hơn hoặc mã SKU.`
              : t('Hãy chọn một nhóm hàng hoặc gõ từ khóa tìm kiếm để xem các sản phẩm có sẵn.')}
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3 2xl:grid-cols-4 gap-2 sm:gap-3.5">
      {products.map((product) => {
        const inCartInfo = cartMap.get(product.id);
        const qty = inCartInfo?.quantity || 0;
        const note = inCartInfo?.note || '';
        const isFav = favoriteIds.includes(product.id);

        return (
          <ProductCard
            key={product.id}
            product={product}
            quantityInCart={qty}
            itemNote={note}
            isFavorite={isFav}
            onToggleFavorite={onToggleFavorite}
            onAdd={onAddProduct}
            onUpdateQty={onUpdateQty}
            onUpdateNote={onUpdateNote}
          />
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

📄 2. FULL SOURCE CODE CHUẨN PRODUCTION 100% (239 dòng) — Bản hoàn chỉnh của order-webapp/src/components/products/QuickOrderSections.tsx 
import type { RefObject } from 'react';
import { Repeat2, Heart, FolderOpen, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react';
import type { Product, Order } from '../../lib/api';
import ProductThumbnail from './ProductThumbnail';
import { useLanguage } from '../../contexts/LanguageContext';
import { useHorizontalScroll } from '../../hooks/useHorizontalScroll';

function money(v: number) {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat('vi-VN').format(Number(v) || 0) + 'đ';
}

function scrollByAmount(ref: RefObject<HTMLDivElement | null>, direction: 'left' | 'right') {
  const el = ref.current;
  if (!el) return;
  const step = Math.max(160, Math.round(el.getBoundingClientRect().width * 0.7));
  el.scrollBy({ left: direction === 'left' ? -step : step, behavior: 'smooth' });
}

function RailArrows({ targetRef }: { targetRef: RefObject<HTMLDivElement | null> }) {
  const base =
    'flex size-7 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-[#17231d]/12 bg-white text-[#59665f] hover:border-[#0f7a4f] hover:text-[#0f7a4f] transition cursor-pointer';
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">
      <button type="button" onClick={() => scrollByAmount(targetRef, 'left')} className={base} aria-label="Cuộn sang trái">
        <ChevronLeft size={15} />
      </button>
      <button type="button" onClick={() => scrollByAmount(targetRef, 'right')} className={base} aria-label="Cuộn sang phải">
        <ChevronRight size={15} />
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

interface QuickOrderSectionsProps {
  latestOrder?: Order;
  loadingLatestOrder: boolean;
  onReorderLatest: () => void;
  frequentProducts: Product[];
  favoriteProducts: Product[];
  favoriteIds: string[];
  onToggleFavorite: (productId: string) => void;
  categories: string[];
  selectedCategory: string;
  onSelectCategory: (category: string) => void;
  onAddProduct: (product: Product, qty?: number) => void;
}

export default function QuickOrderSections({
  latestOrder,
  loadingLatestOrder,
  onReorderLatest,
  frequentProducts,
  favoriteProducts,
  favoriteIds,
  onToggleFavorite,
  categories,
  selectedCategory,
  onSelectCategory,
  onAddProduct,
}: QuickOrderSectionsProps) {
  const { t, productName, productUnit } = useLanguage();
  const frequentRail = useHorizontalScroll<HTMLDivElement>();
  const favoriteRail = useHorizontalScroll<HTMLDivElement>();
  const categoryRail = useHorizontalScroll<HTMLDivElement>();
  const hasAnyQuickSection =
    Boolean(latestOrder) ||
    frequentProducts.length > 0 ||
    favoriteProducts.length > 0 ||
    categories.length > 0;

  if (!hasAnyQuickSection) return null;

  return (
    <section className="space-y-4 min-w-0 w-full max-w-full overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-[#17231d]/10 bg-white p-3 shadow-sm sm:p-4">
      {latestOrder && <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onReorderLatest}
          disabled={loadingLatestOrder}
          className="ml-auto flex min-h-9 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg px-2 text-xs font-bold text-[#0f7a4f] hover:bg-[#f1f7f3] disabled:opacity-60"
        >
          <Repeat2 size={15} className={loadingLatestOrder ? 'animate-spin' : ''} />
          <span>{t(loadingLatestOrder ? 'Đang nạp' : 'Đặt lại đơn gần nhất')}</span>
        </button>}

      {/* 1. Mặt hàng thường mua */}
      {frequentProducts.length > 0 && (
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1.5">
            <p className="text-[11px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#17231d] flex items-center gap-1">
              <span>{t('Hàng thường mua')}</span>
              <span className="px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full bg-[#0f7a4f]/10 text-[#0f7a4f] text-[10px] font-mono">
                {frequentProducts.length}
              </span>
            </p>
            <RailArrows targetRef={frequentRail} />
          </div>
          <div ref={frequentRail} className="flex gap-2.5 overflow-x-auto no-scrollbar pb-1 overscroll-x-contain select-none cursor-grab active:cursor-grabbing">
            {frequentProducts.map((product) => {
              const isFavorite = favoriteIds.includes(product.id);
              return (
                <div
                  key={product.id}
                  className="min-w-57.5 max-w-65 flex items-center gap-2 p-2 rounded-xl border border-[#17231d]/10 bg-[#f8faf7] hover:border-[#0f7a4f]/30 hover:bg-[#f3f7f4] transition-colors"
                >
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => onAddProduct(product, 1)}
                    className="min-w-0 flex-1 flex items-center gap-2 text-left cursor-pointer group"
                    title={`Thêm ${product.name} vào đơn`}
                  >
                    <ProductThumbnail product={product} size="sm" />
                    <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                      <span className="block text-xs font-semibold truncate text-[#17231d] group-hover:text-[#0f7a4f] transition-colors">
                        {productName(product.name)}
                      </span>
                      {product.sku && <span className="block text-[10px] text-[#2c3a33] font-semibold">{product.sku}</span>}
                      <span className="block text-[11px] text-[#2c3a33] font-semibold mt-0.5">
                        {productUnit(product.unit || 'Kg')} •{' '}
                        <strong className="text-[#0f7a4f]">
                          {product.priceOnRequest ? t('Chờ sale báo giá') : money(product.price)}
                        </strong>
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => onToggleFavorite(product.id)}
                    className="p-2 rounded-lg text-[#59665f]/60 hover:text-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-50 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                    aria-label={isFavorite ? `Bỏ yêu thích ${product.name}` : `Yêu thích ${product.name}`}
                  >
                    <Heart
                      size={15}
                      className={isFavorite ? 'fill-rose-500 text-rose-500' : ''}
                    />
                  </button>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* 2. Sản phẩm yêu thích */}
      {favoriteProducts.length > 0 && (
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <div className="mb-1.5 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
            <p className="text-[11px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#17231d] flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <Heart size={13} className="fill-rose-500 text-rose-500" />
              <span>{t('Món yêu thích của bếp')}</span>
              <span className="px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 text-[10px] font-mono border border-rose-200">
                {favoriteProducts.length}
              </span>
            </p>
            <RailArrows targetRef={favoriteRail} />
          </div>
          <div ref={favoriteRail} className="flex gap-2.5 overflow-x-auto no-scrollbar pb-1 overscroll-x-contain select-none cursor-grab active:cursor-grabbing">
            {favoriteProducts.map((product) => (
              <div
                key={product.id}
                className="min-w-57.5 max-w-65 flex items-center gap-2 p-2 rounded-xl border border-rose-100 bg-rose-50/40 hover:bg-rose-50/80 transition-colors"
              >
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => onAddProduct(product, 1)}
                  className="min-w-0 flex-1 flex items-center gap-2 text-left cursor-pointer group"
                >
                  <ProductThumbnail product={product} size="sm" />
                  <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                    <span className="block text-xs font-semibold truncate text-[#17231d] group-hover:text-rose-700">
                      {productName(product.name)}
                    </span>
                    {product.sku && <span className="block text-[10px] text-[#2c3a33] font-semibold">{product.sku}</span>}
                    <span className="block text-[11px] text-[#0f7a4f] font-bold mt-0.5">
                      {product.priceOnRequest ? t('Chờ sale báo giá') : money(product.price)}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => onToggleFavorite(product.id)}
                  className="p-2 rounded-lg text-rose-500 hover:bg-rose-100 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                  title="Bỏ yêu thích"
                  aria-label={`Bỏ yêu thích ${product.name}`}
                >
                  <Heart size={15} className="fill-rose-500 text-rose-500" />
                </button>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* 3. Danh mục sản phẩm (Category chips) */}
      {categories.length > 0 && (
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <div className="mb-1.5 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
            <p className="text-[11px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#17231d] flex items-center gap-1">
              <FolderOpen size={13} className="text-[#0f7a4f]" />
              <span>{t('Duyệt theo nhóm hàng')}</span>
            </p>
            <RailArrows targetRef={categoryRail} />
          </div>
          <div ref={categoryRail} className="flex gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto no-scrollbar pb-1 overscroll-x-contain select-none cursor-grab active:cursor-grabbing">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onSelectCategory('')}
              className={`shrink-0 px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs transition-all cursor-pointer touch-target ${
                !selectedCategory
                  ? 'bg-[#0f7a4f] text-white border-[#0f7a4f] shadow-sm shadow-[#0f7a4f]/20 font-bold'
                  : 'bg-white text-[#17231d] font-bold border border-[#17231d]/20 shadow-2xs hover:border-[#0f7a4f] hover:text-[#0f7a4f] hover:bg-[#eaf6ef]/40'
              }`}
            >
              {t('Tất cả nhóm')}
            </button>
            {categories.map((cat) => {
              const isSelected = selectedCategory === cat;
              return (
                <button
                  key={cat}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => onSelectCategory(cat)}
                  className={`shrink-0 px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs transition-all cursor-pointer touch-target ${
                    isSelected
                      ? 'bg-[#0f7a4f] text-white border-[#0f7a4f] shadow-sm shadow-[#0f7a4f]/20 font-bold'
                      : 'bg-white text-[#17231d] font-bold border border-[#17231d]/20 shadow-2xs hover:border-[#0f7a4f] hover:text-[#0f7a4f] hover:bg-[#eaf6ef]/40'
                  }`}
                >
                  {productName(cat)}
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </div>

        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

import { useEffect, useRef, useState, type MouseEvent } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ArrowRight, BadgeCheck } from 'lucide-react';
import type { StoreBanner } from '../../lib/api';
import { useAuth } from '../../contexts/AuthContext';
import { useLanguage } from '../../contexts/LanguageContext';
import { useHorizontalScroll } from '../../hooks/useHorizontalScroll';

function isInternal(url: string) {
  return url.startsWith('/');
}

/** Khung thương hiệu khi admin chưa thêm banner: gọn trên điện thoại để gian hàng hiện sớm. */
function BrandHero() {
  const { session } = useAuth();
  const { t } = useLanguage();

  const handleStartShopping = (e: MouseEvent) => {
    if (!session) {
      const el = document.getElementById('store-home-catalog');
      if (el && el.offsetParent !== null) {
        e.preventDefault();
        el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
      }
    }
  };

  return (
    <section className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-2xl bg-[#0b4f34] text-white sm:rounded-3xl">
      <img src={`${import.meta.env.BASE_URL}images/store-hero.webp`} alt="" className="absolute inset-0 h-full w-full object-cover opacity-60" />
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-linear-to-r from-[#062b1c] via-[#0b4f34]/90 to-transparent" />
      <div className="relative max-w-160 px-4 py-4 sm:px-7 sm:py-6">
        <p className="hidden items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-white/12 px-3 py-1 text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-emerald-100 ring-1 ring-white/20 sm:inline-flex">
          <BadgeCheck size={14} /> {t('Đạt chuẩn ISO 22000 & HACCP')}
        </p>
        <h1 className="text-balance text-[18px] sm:text-[22px] lg:text-[26px] font-black leading-[1.28] sm:mt-2.5">
          <span>{t('Nguồn thực phẩm sạch cho')}</span> <span className="inline-block">{t('bếp ăn,')}</span>{' '}
          <span>{t('giao')}</span> <span className="inline-block">{t('tận nơi')}</span> <span>{t('theo nhu cầu của bếp.')}</span>
        </h1>
        <p className="mt-2 hidden max-w-130 text-pretty text-[13.5px] leading-relaxed text-emerald-50/85 sm:block">
          {t('Hơn 5.000 mặt hàng, từ rau củ, thịt cá đến gia vị và đồ khô, để bếp của quý khách chỉ cần đặt hàng')} <span className="inline-block">{t('một nơi.')}</span>
        </p>
        <div className="mt-2.5 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-3 gap-y-1 text-[11px] sm:text-[12px] font-medium text-emerald-100/90">
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1">✓ {t('Chuẩn ISO 22000 & HACCP')}</span>
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1">✓ {t('Hơn 5.000 mặt hàng sẵn có')}</span>
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1">✓ {t('Giao đúng hẹn 99,8% ca sáng')}</span>
        </div>
        <Link
          to={session ? '/dat-hang' : '/danh-muc'}
          onClick={handleStartShopping}
          className="mt-3 inline-flex min-h-10 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl bg-[#e0742f] px-5 text-[13px] font-bold text-white whitespace-nowrap shadow-lg shadow-black/20 hover:bg-[#c4601f] sm:mt-4 sm:text-sm"
        >
          <span>{t('Bắt đầu chọn hàng')}</span> <ArrowRight size={16} />
        </Link>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

/**
 * Banner dạng slide do admin bán hàng quản lý (bảng store_banners): vuốt
 * ngang trên điện thoại (hé slide kế tiếp), tự chuyển sau vài giây.
 */
export default function BannerCarousel({ banners }: { banners: StoreBanner[] }) {
  const { t } = useLanguage();
  const row = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  useHorizontalScroll(row);
  const [index, setIndex] = useState(0);
  const paused = useRef(false);

  const goTo = (i: number) => {
    const el = row.current;
    const slide = el?.children[i] as HTMLElement | undefined;
    if (el && slide) el.scrollTo({ left: slide.offsetLeft - el.offsetLeft, behavior: 'smooth' });
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (banners.length < 2) return;
    const timer = window.setInterval(() => {
      if (!paused.current) goTo((index + 1) % banners.length);
    }, 5000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(timer);
  }, [banners.length, index]);

  if (banners.length === 0) return <BrandHero />;

  const onScroll = () => {
    const el = row.current;
    if (!el) return;
    const width = (el.children[0] as HTMLElement | undefined)?.offsetWidth || el.clientWidth;
    setIndex(Math.min(banners.length - 1, Math.round(el.scrollLeft / width)));
  };

  return (
    <section
      className="relative"
      aria-roledescription="carousel"
      onPointerEnter={() => { paused.current = true; }}
      onPointerLeave={() => { paused.current = false; }}
    >
      <div ref={row} onScroll={onScroll} className="tps1-snap-row -mx-1 flex snap-x snap-mandatory gap-2.5 overflow-x-auto px-1 lg:mx-0 lg:gap-0 lg:px-0 overscroll-contain select-none cursor-grab">
        {banners.map((banner) => {
          const image = (
            <picture className="block aspect-[2/1] w-full overflow-hidden rounded-2xl bg-[#eaf6ef] lg:aspect-[3/1] lg:rounded-2xl">
              <source media="(max-width: 1023px)" srcSet={banner.imageMobileUrl} />
              <img src={banner.imageUrl} alt={banner.title} className="h-full w-full object-cover" draggable={false} />
            </picture>
          );
          return (
            <div key={banner.id} className={`shrink-0 snap-start ${banners.length > 1 ? 'w-[88%] sm:w-[70%]' : 'w-full'} lg:w-full`}>
              {banner.linkUrl ? (
                isInternal(banner.linkUrl)
                  ? <Link to={banner.linkUrl} aria-label={banner.title}>{image}</Link>
                  : <a href={banner.linkUrl} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label={banner.title}>{image}</a>
              ) : image}
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
      {banners.length > 1 && (
        <div className="mt-2 flex justify-center gap-1.5 lg:absolute lg:inset-x-0 lg:bottom-3 lg:mt-0">
          {banners.map((banner, i) => (
            <button
              key={banner.id}
              type="button"
              aria-label={`${t('Banner')} ${i + 1}`}
              onClick={() => goTo(i)}
              className={`h-1.5 rounded-full transition-all ${i === index ? 'w-5 bg-[#0f7a4f] lg:bg-white' : 'w-1.5 bg-[#0f7a4f]/30 lg:bg-white/60'}`}
            />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

import { useState } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { PackageOpen } from 'lucide-react';
import type { StoreCategory } from '../../lib/api';
import { useLanguage } from '../../contexts/LanguageContext';
import { useHorizontalScroll } from '../../hooks/useHorizontalScroll';

function CategoryImage({ category }: { category: StoreCategory }) {
  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);
  return category.imageUrl && !failed ? (
    <img src={category.imageUrl} alt="" loading="lazy" onError={() => setFailed(true)} className="h-full w-full object-cover transition duration-300 group-hover:scale-105" />
  ) : (
    <PackageOpen size={26} className="text-[#0f7a4f]/50" />
  );
}

/** Danh mục có ảnh: hàng trượt ngang trên mobile, lưới trên desktop. */
export default function CategoryRail({ categories, layout = 'rail' }: { categories: StoreCategory[]; layout?: 'rail' | 'grid' }) {
  const { t } = useLanguage();
  const railRef = useHorizontalScroll<HTMLDivElement>();
  if (categories.length === 0) return null;
  const wrapper = layout === 'grid'
    ? 'grid grid-cols-3 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-5'
    : 'no-scrollbar -mx-1 flex gap-2.5 sm:gap-3 overflow-x-auto px-1 pb-1 lg:grid lg:grid-cols-10 lg:overflow-visible overscroll-contain select-none cursor-grab';

  return (
    <div ref={layout === 'rail' ? railRef : undefined} className={wrapper}>
      {categories.map((category) => (
        <Link
          key={category.slug}
          to={`/danh-muc/${category.slug}`}
          className={`group flex flex-col items-center gap-2 text-center ${layout === 'rail' ? 'w-21 shrink-0 sm:w-23 lg:w-auto' : ''}`}
        >
          <span className={`flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-2xl bg-[#eaf6ef] ring-1 ring-[#0f7a4f]/12 shadow-xs transition group-hover:-translate-y-0.5 group-hover:ring-[#0f7a4f]/35 ${layout === 'grid' ? 'aspect-square w-full' : 'size-17 sm:size-19 lg:size-20'}`}>
            <CategoryImage category={category} />
          </span>
          <span className="line-clamp-2 text-[12px] font-semibold leading-tight text-[#17231d] group-hover:text-[#0b4f34] sm:text-[13px]">
            {t(category.name)}
          </span>
        </Link>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

import { useEffect, useRef, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ChevronLeft, ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react';
import type { Product } from '../../lib/api';
import { useLanguage } from '../../contexts/LanguageContext';
import { useHorizontalScroll } from '../../hooks/useHorizontalScroll';
import StoreProductCard from './StoreProductCard';

/** Khối sản phẩm trượt ngang (bộ sưu tập, gợi ý, hàng hay đặt). */
export default function ProductRail({
  title,
  subtitle,
  products,
  moreTo,
  icon,
  autoScroll = true,
}: {
  title: string;
  subtitle?: string | null;
  products: Product[];
  moreTo?: string;
  icon?: ReactNode;
  autoScroll?: boolean;
}) {
  const { t } = useLanguage();
  const scroller = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  useHorizontalScroll(scroller);

  const posRef = useRef(0);
  const dirRef = useRef<1 | -1>(1);
  const pausedRef = useRef(false);
  const resumeTimerRef = useRef<number | undefined>(undefined);

  const pauseAutoScroll = () => {
    pausedRef.current = true;
    window.clearTimeout(resumeTimerRef.current);
    const el = scroller.current;
    if (el) posRef.current = el.scrollLeft;
  };

  const resumeAutoScroll = (delay = 1200) => {
    window.clearTimeout(resumeTimerRef.current);
    resumeTimerRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => {
      const el = scroller.current;
      if (el) posRef.current = el.scrollLeft;
      pausedRef.current = false;
    }, delay);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!autoScroll || products.length === 0) return;
    const el = scroller.current;
    if (!el) return;

    posRef.current = el.scrollLeft;
    let lastTime: number | null = null;
    let rafId: number;
    const SPEED = 26; // Tốc độ trôi êm ái: ~26px / giây

    const tick = (now: number) => {
      if (lastTime === null) lastTime = now;
      const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
      lastTime = now;

      if (!pausedRef.current && el) {
        const width = el.getBoundingClientRect().width;
        const maxScrollLeft = el.scrollWidth - width;

        if (maxScrollLeft > 5) {
          posRef.current += dirRef.current * SPEED * dt;

          if (posRef.current >= maxScrollLeft - 1) {
            posRef.current = maxScrollLeft - 1;
            dirRef.current = -1;
          } else if (posRef.current <= 1) {
            posRef.current = 1;
            dirRef.current = 1;
          }

          el.scrollLeft = Math.round(posRef.current);
        }
      }

      rafId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    };

    rafId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);

    return () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(rafId);
      window.clearTimeout(resumeTimerRef.current);
    };
  }, [autoScroll, products.length]);

  if (products.length === 0) return null;

  const scrollBy = (direction: 1 | -1) => {
    pauseAutoScroll();
    const el = scroller.current;
    if (el) el.scrollBy({ left: direction * el.clientWidth * 0.85, behavior: 'smooth' });
    resumeAutoScroll(2000);
  };

  return (
    <section className="rounded-2xl bg-white p-3 ring-1 ring-[#17231d]/6 sm:p-4">
      <div className="mb-2.5 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <h2 className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-base font-black text-[#17231d] sm:text-lg">
            {icon}
            <span className="truncate">{t(title)}</span>
          </h2>
          {subtitle && <p className="line-clamp-1 hidden text-xs text-[#59665f] sm:block">{t(subtitle)}</p>}
        </div>
        <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2">
          {moreTo && (
            <Link to={moreTo} className="whitespace-nowrap text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-[#0f7a4f] hover:underline">
              {t('Xem tất cả')}
            </Link>
          )}
          <div className="hidden gap-1.5 md:flex">
            <button type="button" aria-label={t('Trước')} onClick={() => scrollBy(-1)} className="flex size-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-[#17231d]/10 bg-white text-[#17231d] hover:bg-[#f5f7f3]">
              <ChevronLeft size={18} />
            </button>
            <button type="button" aria-label={t('Sau')} onClick={() => scrollBy(1)} className="flex size-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-[#17231d]/10 bg-white text-[#17231d] hover:bg-[#f5f7f3]">
              <ChevronRight size={18} />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div
        ref={scroller}
        onMouseEnter={pauseAutoScroll}
        onMouseLeave={() => resumeAutoScroll(1200)}
        onPointerDown={pauseAutoScroll}
        onPointerUp={() => resumeAutoScroll(1200)}
        onTouchStart={pauseAutoScroll}
        onTouchEnd={() => resumeAutoScroll(1200)}
        onWheel={pauseAutoScroll}
        className="no-scrollbar -mx-1 flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto px-1 pb-1 sm:gap-2.5 overscroll-contain select-none cursor-grab"
      >
        {products.map((product) => (
          <div key={product.id} className="w-[42%] shrink-0 sm:w-[23%] md:w-[18.5%] lg:w-[15.5%] xl:w-[13.4%]">
            <StoreProductCard product={product} />
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

📄 2. FULL SOURCE CODE CHUẨN PRODUCTION 100% (199 dòng) — Bản hoàn chỉnh của order-webapp/src/components/store/StoreProductCard.tsx 
import { useState, type MouseEvent } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Heart, Minus, PackageOpen, Plus, ShoppingCart } from 'lucide-react';
import type { Product } from '../../lib/api';
import { useCart } from '../../contexts/CartContext';
import { useAuth } from '../../contexts/AuthContext';
import { useFavorites } from '../../contexts/FavoritesContext';
import { useLanguage } from '../../contexts/LanguageContext';
import { productImage } from '../products/ProductThumbnail';

export function money(value: number) {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat('vi-VN').format(Math.round(Number(value) || 0)) + 'đ';
}

/** Bước tăng giảm theo quy cách đặt hàng của sản phẩm. */
export function stepOf(product: Product) {
  const step = Number(product.orderStep) || 1;
  return step > 0 ? step : 1;
}

export function firstQuantityOf(product: Product) {
  return Math.max(Number(product.minOrderQty) || 0, stepOf(product));
}

/** Giá + nhãn "Giá tham khảo" cho khách chưa đăng nhập. */
export function PriceTag({ product, large = false }: { product: Product; large?: boolean }) {
  const { session } = useAuth();
  const { t, productUnit } = useLanguage();
  if (product.priceOnRequest || !(product.price > 0)) {
    return <p className={`${large ? 'text-lg' : 'text-sm'} font-bold text-[#c4601f]`}>{t('Liên hệ báo giá')}</p>;
  }
  return (
    <div className="min-w-0">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-baseline gap-x-1.5 leading-tight">
        <span className={`${large ? 'text-2xl' : 'text-[15px]'} font-black text-[#0b4f34]`}>
          {money(product.price)}
        </span>
        <span className={`${large ? 'text-sm' : 'text-[11.5px]'} font-semibold text-[#59665f] whitespace-nowrap`}>
          / {productUnit(product.unit || 'Kg')}
        </span>
      </div>
      {!session && <p className="mt-0.5 text-[10.5px] font-medium text-[#8a9690]">{t('Giá tham khảo')}</p>}
    </div>
  );
}

/** Nút "Thêm" chuyển thành bộ tăng/giảm khi đã có trong giỏ. */
export function QuantityStepper({ product, size = 'md' }: { product: Product; size?: 'md' | 'lg' }) {
  const { quantityOf, addProduct, updateQty, tab } = useCart();
  const { t } = useLanguage();
  const quantity = quantityOf(product.id);
  const step = stepOf(product);
  const plainLine = tab.items.find((item) => item.product.id === product.id && !item.note?.trim());
  const height = size === 'lg' ? 'h-12' : 'h-10';

  if (quantity <= 0) {
    return (
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => addProduct(product, firstQuantityOf(product))}
        className={`${height} inline-flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl bg-[#0f7a4f] px-3 text-sm font-bold text-white shadow-sm shadow-[#0f7a4f]/20 transition hover:bg-[#0b4f34] active:scale-[0.98]`}
      >
        <ShoppingCart size={size === 'lg' ? 18 : 16} />
        <span>{t('Thêm vào giỏ')}</span>
      </button>
    );
  }

  const change = (delta: number) => {
    if (!plainLine) { addProduct(product, Math.max(step, delta)); return; }
    const next = Number((plainLine.quantity + delta).toFixed(3));
    updateQty(plainLine.lineKey || plainLine.product.id, next < (Number(product.minOrderQty) || 0) ? 0 : next);
  };

  return (
    <div className={`${height} flex w-full items-center justify-between rounded-xl border border-[#0f7a4f]/25 bg-[#eaf6ef] p-1`}>
      <button type="button" aria-label={t('Giảm')} onClick={() => change(-step)} className="flex h-full aspect-square items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-white text-[#0f7a4f] shadow-sm active:scale-95">
        <Minus size={16} />
      </button>
      <span className="min-w-0 px-1 text-center text-sm font-black text-[#0b4f34]">{Number(quantity.toFixed(3))}</span>
      <button type="button" aria-label={t('Tăng')} onClick={() => change(step)} className="flex h-full aspect-square items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-[#0f7a4f] text-white shadow-sm active:scale-95">
        <Plus size={16} />
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

/** Nút tim: thêm/bỏ khỏi danh sách hàng yêu thích. */
export function FavoriteButton({ productId, className = '' }: { productId: string; className?: string }) {
  const { isFavorite, toggle } = useFavorites();
  const { t } = useLanguage();
  const active = isFavorite(productId);
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      aria-pressed={active}
      aria-label={active ? t('Bỏ khỏi yêu thích') : t('Thêm vào yêu thích')}
      onClick={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); toggle(productId); }}
      className={`flex ${/(^| )size-/.test(className) ? '' : 'size-9'} items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-white/95 shadow-sm ring-1 ring-black/5 transition active:scale-90 ${active ? 'text-rose-500' : 'text-[#2c3a33] hover:text-rose-500'} ${className}`}
    >
      <Heart size={16} className={active ? 'fill-rose-500 text-rose-500' : 'fill-none text-[#2c3a33] hover:text-rose-500'} strokeWidth={active ? 2 : 2.2} />
    </button>
  );
}

export function ProductImage({ product, className = '' }: { product: Product; className?: string }) {
  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);
  const { t } = useLanguage();
  const src = productImage(product);
  return (
    <div className={`relative flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden bg-[#f3f6f2] ${className}`}>
      {src && !failed ? (
        <img src={src} alt={product.name} loading="lazy" decoding="async" onError={() => setFailed(true)} className="h-full w-full object-cover" />
      ) : (
        <PackageOpen size={36} strokeWidth={1.5} className="text-[#59665f]/35" />
      )}
      {src && !failed && product.imageIsIllustration && (
        <span className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-1.5 left-1.5 rounded-md bg-black/45 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[9.5px] font-semibold text-white backdrop-blur-sm">
          {t('Ảnh minh hoạ')}
        </span>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/**
 * Nút thêm gọn đặt trên ảnh (kiểu sàn bán sỉ): "+" tròn khi chưa có trong
 * giỏ, chuyển thành "− số lượng +" khi đã chọn — thẻ sản phẩm thấp hơn,
 * mỗi hàng hiện được nhiều món hơn.
 */
export function CompactAdd({ product, className = '' }: { product: Product; className?: string }) {
  const { quantityOf, addProduct, updateQty, tab } = useCart();
  const { t } = useLanguage();
  const quantity = quantityOf(product.id);
  const step = stepOf(product);
  const plainLine = tab.items.find((item) => item.product.id === product.id && !item.note?.trim());
  const stop = (e: MouseEvent) => { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); };

  if (quantity <= 0) {
    return (
      <button
        type="button"
        aria-label={t('Thêm vào giỏ')}
        onClick={(e) => { stop(e); addProduct(product, firstQuantityOf(product)); }}
        className={`flex size-8 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[#0f7a4f] text-white sm:size-9 shadow-md shadow-black/15 transition hover:bg-[#0b4f34] active:scale-90 ${className}`}
      >
        <Plus size={18} strokeWidth={2.6} />
      </button>
    );
  }

  const change = (e: MouseEvent, delta: number) => {
    stop(e);
    if (!plainLine) { addProduct(product, Math.max(step, delta)); return; }
    const next = Number((plainLine.quantity + delta).toFixed(3));
    updateQty(plainLine.lineKey || plainLine.product.id, next < (Number(product.minOrderQty) || 0) ? 0 : next);
  };

  return (
    <div className={`flex h-8 items-center rounded-full bg-[#0f7a4f] sm:h-9 text-white shadow-md shadow-black/15 ${className}`}>
      <button type="button" aria-label={t('Giảm')} onClick={(e) => change(e, -step)} className="flex size-8 items-center justify-center rounded-full active:scale-90 sm:size-9"><Minus size={16} strokeWidth={2.6} /></button>
      <span className="min-w-5 text-center text-[12.5px] font-black">{Number(quantity.toFixed(3))}</span>
      <button type="button" aria-label={t('Tăng')} onClick={(e) => change(e, step)} className="flex size-8 items-center justify-center rounded-full active:scale-90 sm:size-9"><Plus size={16} strokeWidth={2.6} /></button>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function StoreProductCard({ product }: { product: Product }) {
  const { session } = useAuth();
  const { t, productName, productUnit } = useLanguage();
  const name = productName(product.name);
  const priced = !product.priceOnRequest && product.price > 0;
  return (
    <article className="group flex h-full flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-xl bg-white ring-1 ring-[#17231d]/7 transition hover:shadow-[0_8px_22px_rgba(16,40,30,0.10)]">
      <div className="relative">
        <Link to={`/san-pham/${product.id}`} className="block">
          <ProductImage product={product} className="aspect-square w-full" />
        </Link>
        <FavoriteButton productId={product.id} className="absolute right-1 top-1 size-7 sm:right-1.5 sm:top-1.5 sm:size-8" />
        <CompactAdd product={product} className="absolute bottom-1.5 right-1.5" />
      </div>
      <Link to={`/san-pham/${product.id}`} className="flex flex-1 flex-col px-2 pb-2 pt-1.5 sm:px-2.5">
        {priced ? (
          <p className="text-[13.5px] font-black leading-tight sm:text-[14.5px] text-[#0b4f34]">
            {money(product.price)}
            {!session && <span className="ml-1 text-[10px] font-semibold text-[#8a9690] whitespace-nowrap">{t('Giá tham khảo')}</span>}
          </p>
        ) : (
          <p className="truncate text-[12px] font-bold leading-[1.45] text-[#c4601f]">{t('Liên hệ báo giá')}</p>
        )}
        <h3 className="mt-0.5 line-clamp-2 min-h-[2.4em] text-[12px] font-medium leading-[1.2] sm:text-[12.5px] text-[#17231d] group-hover:text-[#0b4f34]">{name}</h3>
        <p className="mt-0.5 line-clamp-1 text-[11px] text-[#8a9690]">
          {product.packagingNote || productUnit(product.unit || 'Kg')}
        </p>
      </Link>
    </article>
  );
}

📄 2. FULL SOURCE CODE CHUẨN PRODUCTION 100% (58 dòng) — Bản hoàn chỉnh của order-webapp/src/components/store/StoreProductGrid.tsx 
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import type { Product } from '../../lib/api';
import StoreProductCard from './StoreProductCard';

/** Lưới sản phẩm dùng chung: 2 cột điện thoại → 7 cột màn hình lớn. */
export const STORE_GRID = 'grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-4 sm:gap-2.5 md:grid-cols-5 lg:grid-cols-6 xl:grid-cols-7';

// Khớp các mốc màn hình của Tailwind trong STORE_GRID.
export function columnsFor(width: number): number {
  if (width >= 1280) return 7;
  if (width >= 1024) return 6;
  if (width >= 768) return 5;
  if (width >= 640) return 4;
  return 2;
}

export function useGridColumns() {
  const [columns, setColumns] = useState(() => (typeof window === 'undefined' ? 2 : columnsFor(window.innerWidth)));
  useEffect(() => {
    const onResize = () => setColumns(columnsFor(window.innerWidth));
    window.addEventListener('resize', onResize);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('resize', onResize);
  }, []);
  return columns;
}

/**
 * Khi còn hàng để "Xem thêm", chỉ hiện đủ số hàng chẵn cột để hàng cuối không bị lẻ;
 * những món dư sẽ hiện ở lần tải kế tiếp.
 */
export default function StoreProductGrid({ products, hasMore = false }: { products: Product[]; hasMore?: boolean }) {
  const columns = useGridColumns();
  const full = Math.floor(products.length / columns) * columns;
  const visible = hasMore && full > 0 ? products.slice(0, full) : products;
  return (
    <div className={STORE_GRID}>
      {visible.map((product) => <StoreProductCard key={product.id} product={product} />)}
    </div>
  );
}

export function StoreGridSkeleton({ count = 14 }: { count?: number }) {
  return (
    <div className={STORE_GRID} aria-hidden="true">
      {Array.from({ length: count }).map((_, i) => (
        <div key={i} className="overflow-hidden rounded-xl bg-white ring-1 ring-[#17231d]/6">
          <div className="aspect-square animate-pulse bg-[#eef2ee]" />
          <div className="space-y-1.5 p-2">
            <div className="h-4 w-2/3 animate-pulse rounded bg-[#eef2ee]" />
            <div className="h-3 animate-pulse rounded bg-[#eef2ee]" />
            <div className="h-3 w-1/3 animate-pulse rounded bg-[#eef2ee]" />
          </div>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}
