type LanguageContextValue = { language: Language; setLanguage: (value: Language) => void; t: (value: string) => string; productName: (value: string) => string; productUnit: (value: string) => string; errorText: (error: { code?: string; message?: string }) => string };
const Context = createContext<LanguageContextValue | null>(null);

export function LanguageProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [language, setLanguage] = useState<Language>(() => {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    return saved === 'en' || saved === 'zh-CN' ? saved : 'vi';
  });
  const [catalogNames, setCatalogNames] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  useEffect(() => { localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, language); document.documentElement.lang = language; }, [language]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (language === 'vi') { setCatalogNames({}); return; }
    let active = true;
    setCatalogNames({});
    fetch(`${import.meta.env.BASE_URL}catalog-names-${language}.json`)
      .then(response => { if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Catalog translations unavailable'); return response.json(); })
      .then(data => { if (active) setCatalogNames(data); })
      .catch(() => { if (active) setCatalogNames({}); });
    return () => { active = false; };
  }, [language]);
  const t = useCallback((value: string) => language === 'vi' ? value : translations[value]?.[language === 'en' ? 0 : 1] || value, [language]);
  const productName = useCallback((value: string) => language === 'vi' ? value : categoryTranslations[value]?.[language === 'en' ? 0 : 1] || catalogNames[value] || value, [language, catalogNames]);
  const productUnit = useCallback((value: string) => language === 'vi' ? value : unitTranslations[value]?.[language === 'en' ? 0 : 1] || value, [language]);
  const errorText = useCallback((error: { code?: string; message?: string }) => t((error.code && errorTranslations[error.code]) || error.message || 'Có lỗi xảy ra, vui lòng thử lại sau.'), [t]);
  return <Context.Provider value={{ language, setLanguage, t, productName, productUnit, errorText }}>{children}</Context.Provider>;
}

export function useLanguage() {
  const value = useContext(Context);
  if (!value) throw new Error('LanguageProvider is missing');
  return value;
}

export function LanguageSwitcher({ dark = false }: { dark?: boolean }) {
  const { language, setLanguage } = useLanguage();
  return <label className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs ${dark ? 'text-white' : 'text-[#17231d]'}`}>
    <span className="sr-only">Language</span>
    <select aria-label="Language / Ngôn ngữ / 语言" value={language} onChange={event => setLanguage(event.target.value as Language)}
      className={`min-h-11 rounded-xl border px-2 font-semibold ${dark ? 'border-white/30 bg-[#0b4f34] text-white' : 'border-[#17231d]/15 bg-white text-[#17231d]'}`}>
      <option value="vi">Tiếng Việt</option><option value="en">English</option><option value="zh-CN">简体中文</option>
    </select>
  </label>;
}

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Link, NavLink, Outlet, useLocation, useNavigate, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import {
  ChevronDown,
  ChevronLeft,
  ChevronRight,
  ClipboardList,
  FileSpreadsheet,
  Flame,
  Heart,
  Home,
  LayoutGrid,
  LogOut,
  PackageOpen,
  Phone,
  ShoppingCart,
  Truck,
  UserCircle2,
  X,
  Zap,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { useAuth } from '../contexts/AuthContext';
import { useCart } from '../contexts/CartContext';
import { useFavorites } from '../contexts/FavoritesContext';
import { LanguageSwitcher, useLanguage } from '../contexts/LanguageContext';
import { useStoreHome } from '../hooks/useStoreHome';
import NotificationBell from '../components/NotificationBell';
import TrustMarquee from '../components/store/TrustMarquee';
import SmartSearchBox from '../components/store/SmartSearchBox';
import InstallBanner from '../components/store/InstallBanner';

const HOTLINE = '089 890 2222';

const SUB_CATEGORY_META: Record<string, { bgClass: string; imageUrl?: string }> = {
  'Rau củ quả': {
    bgClass: 'bg-[#e8f8f0]',
    imageUrl: 'https://yntgxollwjemyidizhnn.supabase.co/storage/v1/object/public/product-images/store/category/rau-cu-qua.webp',
  },
  'Trái cây': {
    bgClass: 'bg-[#fff4e6]',
    imageUrl: 'https://yntgxollwjemyidizhnn.supabase.co/storage/v1/object/public/product-images/thumbs/224fa28a-ac49-4338-b4ab-1c899d8e9571.webp',
  },
  'Chay, chả giò': {
    bgClass: 'bg-[#e8f8f0]',
    imageUrl: 'https://yntgxollwjemyidizhnn.supabase.co/storage/v1/object/public/product-images/thumbs/1b3bbc6c-5cd3-4168-bb20-ead5baf5d39b.webp',
  },
  'Đậu hũ': {
    bgClass: 'bg-[#fef9c3]',
    imageUrl: 'https://yntgxollwjemyidizhnn.supabase.co/storage/v1/object/public/product-images/thumbs/a555a572-0a8e-438f-a16c-2bddd2aaca82.webp',
  },
};

function CartButton({ compact = false }: { compact?: boolean }) {
  const { count } = useCart();
  const { t } = useLanguage();
  return (
    <Link to="/gio-hang" aria-label={`${t('Giỏ hàng')} (${count})`} className={`relative inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl ${compact ? 'size-10 justify-center' : 'h-10 px-3.5'} bg-[#0f7a4f] text-white hover:bg-[#0b4f34]`}>
      <ShoppingCart size={18} />
      {!compact && <span className="text-sm font-bold">{t('Giỏ hàng')}</span>}
      {count > 0 && (
        <span className="absolute -right-1.5 -top-1.5 flex h-5 min-w-5 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[#e0742f] px-1 text-[11px] font-black text-white ring-2 ring-white">
          {count > 99 ? '99+' : count}
        </span>
      )}
    </Link>
  );
}

export default function StoreLayout() {
  const { session, logout } = useAuth();
  const { count } = useCart();
  const { ids: favoriteIds } = useFavorites();
  const { t } = useLanguage();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { data: home } = useStoreHome();
  const location = useLocation();
  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const [categoryDrawerOpen, setCategoryDrawerOpen] = useState(false);
  const [activeCategorySlug, setActiveCategorySlug] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [mobileShowSub, setMobileShowSub] = useState(false);
  const loginTarget = `/dang-nhap?quay-lai=${encodeURIComponent(location.pathname + location.search)}`;

  const activeCategory = (home?.categories || []).find((c) => c.slug === activeCategorySlug) || null;
  const hasActiveSub = Boolean(activeCategory && activeCategory.productCategories && activeCategory.productCategories.length > 1);
  const subCategories = hasActiveSub ? activeCategory!.productCategories : [];

  useEffect(() => { window.scrollTo({ top: 0 }); }, [location.pathname]);
  useEffect(() => {
    setCategoryDrawerOpen(false);
    setMobileShowSub(false);
  }, [location.pathname]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (categoryDrawerOpen) {
      document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    } else {
      document.body.style.overflow = '';
      setMobileShowSub(false);
    }
    return () => { document.body.style.overflow = ''; };
  }, [categoryDrawerOpen]);

  const bottomTabs = [
    { to: '/', label: 'Trang chủ', icon: Home, end: true },
    { to: '/yeu-thich', label: 'Yêu thích', icon: Heart, badge: favoriteIds.length },
    { to: '/don-hang', label: 'Đơn hàng', icon: ClipboardList },
    { to: '/gio-hang', label: 'Giỏ hàng', icon: ShoppingCart, badge: count },
    { to: session ? '/tai-khoan' : loginTarget, label: session ? 'Tài khoản' : 'Đăng nhập', icon: UserCircle2 },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen w-full max-w-full overflow-x-clip flex-col bg-[#f5f7f3] selection:bg-[#0f7a4f] selection:text-white">
      <header className="sticky top-0 z-30 w-full max-w-full border-b border-[#17231d]/8 bg-white/95 pt-[env(safe-area-inset-top,0px)] backdrop-blur-md">
        <TrustMarquee />

        {/* TẦNG 1: Cùng hàng với Logo */}
        <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-330 items-center justify-between gap-3 sm:gap-4 px-3 py-2.5 sm:px-5 sm:py-3">
          {/* Cụm trái: Logo */}
          <Link to="/" className="shrink-0" aria-label="Thực Phẩm Số Một">
            <img src={`${import.meta.env.BASE_URL}images/tps1-logo-horizontal.png`} alt="Thực Phẩm Số Một" className="h-9 sm:h-10 w-auto object-contain shrink-0" />
          </Link>

          {/* Cụm giữa: 3 thẻ thông tin vận hành B2B chia đều không gian */}
          <div className="hidden lg:grid lg:grid-cols-3 flex-1 max-w-170 items-center gap-2.5 px-2">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-[#17231d]/8 bg-[#f8faf7] px-3 py-1.5 min-w-0">
              <Truck size={15} className="text-[#0f7a4f] shrink-0" />
              <div className="min-w-0 leading-tight">
                <span className="block truncate text-[10px] font-semibold text-[#59665f]">{t('Khung giờ giao bếp')}</span>
                <span className="block truncate text-xs font-bold text-[#17231d]">{t('Ca sớm: 05:00 – 07:00')}</span>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-[#17231d]/8 bg-[#f8faf7] px-3 py-1.5 min-w-0">
              <PackageOpen size={15} className="text-[#0f7a4f] shrink-0" />
              <div className="min-w-0 leading-tight">
                <span className="block truncate text-[10px] font-semibold text-[#59665f]">{t('Điểm giao mặc định')}</span>
                <span className="block truncate text-xs font-bold text-[#0b4f34]">
                  {session?.defaultShippingAddress?.address ? (session.defaultShippingAddress.alias || t('Bếp chính')) : t('TP.HCM · Bình Dương · Đồng Nai')}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
            <a
              href={'tel:' + HOTLINE.replace(/\s/g, '')}
              className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-[#e0742f]/20 bg-[#fef5ee]/70 px-3 py-1.5 min-w-0 hover:bg-[#fef5ee] transition"
              title={t('Tổng đài đặt hàng')}
            >
              <Phone size={15} className="text-[#e0742f] shrink-0" />
              <div className="min-w-0 leading-tight">
                <span className="block truncate text-[10px] font-semibold text-[#8a5729]">{t('Tổng đài B2B (24/7)')}</span>
                <span className="block truncate text-xs font-black text-[#c4601f]">{HOTLINE}</span>

          {/* Cụm phải: Ngôn ngữ, Thông báo, Yêu thích, Tài khoản, Giỏ hàng */}

                favoriteIds.length > 0

                  <span className="hidden max-w-32.5 truncate text-sm font-semibold text-[#17231d] md:inline">{session.company || session.name}</span>
                </Link>

              </div>
            )}

            <div className="hidden sm:block"><CartButton /></div>

          </div>

          <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-330 items-center gap-2.5 px-3 py-2 sm:px-5">
            {/* Nút Tất cả danh mục */}

                setActiveCategorySlug(null);
                setMobileShowSub(false);

              <span className="hidden xs:inline sm:inline">{t('Tất cả danh mục')}</span>
              <span className="xs:hidden sm:hidden">{t('Danh mục')}</span>
              <ChevronDown size={15} />
            </button>

            {/* Thanh tìm kiếm thông minh ở giữa - Smart Search Popover kiểu Kamereo */}
            <SmartSearchBox className="flex-1 min-w-0" />

            {/* Nhóm nút tác vụ nhanh bên phải */}

                <NavLink

                  }`}

                  <ClipboardList size={15} />
                  <span>{t('Đơn hàng')}</span>
                </NavLink>
              </div>
            )}

        </div>
      </header>

      {/* SIDEBAR / DRAWER DANH MỤC 2 CỘT KIỂU KAMEREO */}

            className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/35 backdrop-blur-[1px] transition-opacity"
            onClick={() => setCategoryDrawerOpen(false)}
            aria-hidden="true"
          />
          <aside
            className="fixed inset-y-0 left-0 z-50 flex flex-row bg-white shadow-2xl transition-transform animate-in slide-in-from-left duration-200 max-h-screen"

                mobileShowSub ? 'hidden sm:flex' : 'flex'
              }`}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-[#17231d]/8 bg-[#f8faf7] px-4 py-3 sm:px-5 sm:py-3.5">

                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setCategoryDrawerOpen(false)}

                >
                  <X size={18} />
                </button>
              </div>

              <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto py-2">
                <Link
                  to={session ? '/dat-hang' : '/'}

                  <ChevronRight size={16} className="text-[#8a9690] transition-transform group-hover:translate-x-0.5 group-hover:text-[#0f7a4f]" />

                {(home?.categories || []).map((cat) => {
                  const isActive = activeCategorySlug === cat.slug;
                  const hasSub = Boolean(cat.productCategories && cat.productCategories.length > 1);
                  return (
                    <Link
                      key={cat.slug}
                      to={`/danh-muc/${cat.slug}`}
                      onClick={(e) => {
                        if (window.innerWidth < 640 && hasSub) {
                          e.preventDefault();
                          setActiveCategorySlug(cat.slug);
                          setMobileShowSub(true);
                        } else {
                          setCategoryDrawerOpen(false);
                        }
                      }}
                      onMouseEnter={() => {
                        if (window.innerWidth >= 640) {
                          if (hasSub) {
                            setActiveCategorySlug(cat.slug);
                          } else {
                            setActiveCategorySlug(null);
                          }
                        }
                      }}
                      className={`group flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2 sm:px-4 sm:py-2.5 transition rounded-xl mx-2 my-0.5 ${
                        isActive
                          ? 'bg-[#eaf6ef] text-[#0f7a4f] font-bold'
                          : 'hover:bg-[#eaf6ef]/60 text-[#17231d]'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <span className="flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-[#f3f6f2] ring-1 ring-black/5">
                        {cat.imageUrl ? (
                          <img src={cat.imageUrl} alt="" className="h-full w-full object-cover" />
                        ) : (
                          <PackageOpen size={18} className="text-[#0f7a4f]/50" />
                        )}
                      </span>
                      <span className="flex-1 text-[13.5px] sm:text-[14px] font-semibold group-hover:text-[#0f7a4f]">
                        {t(cat.name)}
                      </span>
                      {hasSub ? (
                        <ChevronRight
                          size={16}
                          className={`transition-transform group-hover:translate-x-0.5 ${
                            isActive ? 'text-[#0f7a4f]' : 'text-[#8a9690] group-hover:text-[#0f7a4f]'
                          }`}
                        />
                      ) : (
                        <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#8a9690]/60 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity">→</span>
                      )}
                    </Link>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* CỘT 2: Danh mục con (Chỉ hiện khi danh mục đang chọn có nhóm nhỏ thực tế) */}
            {hasActiveSub && activeCategory && (
              <div
                className={`w-full sm:w-80 md:w-90 flex flex-col bg-white shrink-0 ${
                  mobileShowSub ? 'flex' : 'hidden sm:flex'
                }`}
              >
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-[#17231d]/8 bg-[#f8faf7] px-4 py-3 sm:px-5 sm:py-3.5">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setMobileShowSub(false)}
                      className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-bold text-[#59665f] hover:text-[#0f7a4f] sm:hidden"
                    >
                      <ChevronLeft size={16} />
                      <span>{t('Quay lại')}</span>
                    </button>
                    <h3 className="truncate text-sm sm:text-base font-bold text-[#17231d]">
                      {t(activeCategory.name)}
                    </h3>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 shrink-0">
                    <Link
                      to={`/danh-muc/${activeCategory.slug}`}
                      onClick={() => setCategoryDrawerOpen(false)}
                      className="text-xs sm:text-[13px] font-bold text-[#0f7a4f] hover:underline whitespace-nowrap"
                    >
                      {t('Xem tất cả')} →
                    </Link>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setCategoryDrawerOpen(false)}
                      aria-label={t('Đóng')}
                      className="flex size-8 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-[#59665f] hover:bg-black/5 hover:text-[#17231d]"
                    >
                      <X size={18} />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 sm:p-5">
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-x-4 gap-y-5 sm:gap-x-5 sm:gap-y-6">
                    {/* Ô Tất cả danh mục này */}
                    <Link
                      to={`/danh-muc/${activeCategory.slug}`}
                      onClick={() => setCategoryDrawerOpen(false)}
                      className="group flex flex-col items-center text-center"
                    >
                      <div className="flex size-20 sm:size-24 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-full p-2.5 sm:p-3 transition duration-200 group-hover:scale-105 shadow-xs ring-1 ring-black/5 bg-[#f3f6f2]">
                        {activeCategory.imageUrl ? (
                          <img src={activeCategory.imageUrl} alt={activeCategory.name} className="h-full w-full rounded-full object-cover" />
                        ) : (
                          <PackageOpen size={28} className="text-[#0f7a4f]/50" />
                        )}
                      </div>
                      <span className="mt-2 text-[12.5px] sm:text-[13.5px] font-bold leading-snug text-[#17231d] group-hover:text-[#0f7a4f]">
                        {t(`Tất cả ${activeCategory.name}`)}
                      </span>
                    </Link>

                    {/* Các nhóm nhỏ thực tế từ API */}
                    {subCategories.map((subName) => {
                      const meta = SUB_CATEGORY_META[subName];
                      const imageUrl = meta?.imageUrl || activeCategory.imageUrl;
                      const bgClass = meta?.bgClass || 'bg-[#f3f6f2]';
                      return (
                        <Link
                          key={subName}
                          to={`/danh-muc/${activeCategory.slug}?nhom=${encodeURIComponent(subName)}`}
                          onClick={() => setCategoryDrawerOpen(false)}
                          className="group flex flex-col items-center text-center"
                        >
                          <div className={`flex size-20 sm:size-24 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-full p-2.5 sm:p-3 transition duration-200 group-hover:scale-105 shadow-xs ring-1 ring-black/5 ${bgClass}`}>
                            {imageUrl ? (
                              <img src={imageUrl} alt={subName} className="h-full w-full rounded-full object-cover" />
                            ) : (
                              <PackageOpen size={28} className="text-[#0f7a4f]/50" />
                            )}
                          </div>
                          <span className="mt-2 text-[12.5px] sm:text-[13.5px] font-bold leading-snug text-[#17231d] group-hover:text-[#0f7a4f]">
                            {t(subName)}
                          </span>
                        </Link>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </aside>
        </div>
      )}

      <main className="mx-auto w-full max-w-330 min-w-0 flex-1 px-3 py-4 pb-28 sm:px-5 md:pb-8">
        {session?.verificationStatus === 'pending' && (
          <div role="status" className="mb-3 rounded-xl bg-amber-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-amber-900 ring-1 ring-amber-200">
            {t('Tài khoản của quý khách đang chờ TPS1 xác minh. Quý khách vẫn đặt hàng bình thường, giá sẽ được xác nhận khi chốt đơn.')}
          </div>
        )}
        <Outlet />
      </main>

      <footer className="hidden border-t border-[#17231d]/8 bg-white md:block">
        <div className="mx-auto grid max-w-330 gap-6 px-5 py-8 text-sm text-[#59665f] md:grid-cols-3">
          <div>
            <img src={`${import.meta.env.BASE_URL}images/tps1-logo-horizontal.png`} alt="Thực Phẩm Số Một" className="h-9 w-auto" />
            <p className="mt-3">{t('Đồng hành cùng nhà hàng, bếp ăn công ty và trường học tại Đồng Nai, TP.HCM và các tỉnh lân cận, đưa thực phẩm tươi đến tận bếp.')}</p>
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="font-bold text-[#17231d]">{t('Liên hệ')}</p>
            <p className="mt-2">{t('Tổng đài đặt hàng')}: <a href={`tel:${HOTLINE.replace(/\s/g, '')}`} className="font-bold text-[#0f7a4f]">{HOTLINE}</a></p>
            <p>Email: <a href="mailto:contact@thucphamsomot.vn" className="text-[#0f7a4f]">contact@thucphamsomot.vn</a></p>
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="font-bold text-[#17231d]">{t('Về Thực Phẩm Số Một')}</p>
            <p className="mt-2"><a href="https://thucphamsomot.vn/gioi-thieu" className="hover:text-[#0f7a4f]">{t('Giới thiệu công ty')}</a></p>
            <p><a href="https://thucphamsomot.vn/quy-trinh" className="hover:text-[#0f7a4f]">{t('Quy trình giao nhận')}</a></p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </footer>

      <nav className="fixed inset-x-0 bottom-0 z-30 border-t border-[#17231d]/10 bg-white/95 shadow-[0_-8px_24px_rgba(0,0,0,0.06)] backdrop-blur-lg md:hidden" style={{ paddingBottom: 'env(safe-area-inset-bottom)' }} aria-label={t('Điều hướng chính')}>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-5">
          {bottomTabs.map(({ to, label, icon: Icon, end, badge }) => (
            <NavLink key={label} to={to} end={end} className={({ isActive }) => `flex h-15 flex-col items-center justify-center gap-0.5 text-[10.5px] ${isActive ? 'font-bold text-[#0f7a4f]' : 'font-medium text-[#59665f]'}`}>
              {({ isActive }) => {
                const isFavTab = label === 'Yêu thích';
                const hasFavs = favoriteIds.length > 0;
                return (
                  <>
                    <span className={`relative flex h-7 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-full ${isActive ? 'bg-[#0f7a4f]/12' : ''}`}>
                      <Icon
                        size={19}
                        strokeWidth={isActive ? 2.5 : 2}
                        className={isFavTab && hasFavs ? 'fill-rose-500 text-rose-500' : ''}
                      />
                      {Boolean(badge) && (
                        <span className={`absolute -right-1 -top-1 flex h-4 min-w-4 items-center justify-center rounded-full ${isFavTab ? 'bg-rose-500' : 'bg-[#e0742f]'} px-1 text-[10px] font-black text-white ring-2 ring-white`}>
                          {badge! > 99 ? '99+' : badge}
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </span>
                    <span className="truncate whitespace-nowrap px-0.5">{t(label)}</span>
                  </>
                );
              }}
            </NavLink>
          ))}
        </div>
      </nav>

      <InstallBanner />
    </div>
  );
}

📄 2. FULL SOURCE CODE CHUẨN PRODUCTION 100% (81 dòng) — Bản hoàn chỉnh của order-webapp/src/pages/AccountPage.tsx 
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Building2, KeyRound, MapPin, Phone, Mail, LogOut, ChevronRight, ShieldCheck, Plus, Trash2, Pencil, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { api, ApiError, type AccountProfile } from '../lib/api';
import { resizeImageFile } from '../lib/resizeImage';
import { useAuth } from '../contexts/AuthContext';
import { useLanguage } from '../contexts/LanguageContext';

export default function AccountPage() {
  const { session, logout, setSession } = useAuth();
  const { t, errorText } = useLanguage();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [profile, setProfile] = useState<AccountProfile | null>(null);
  const [profileForm, setProfileForm] = useState({ name: session?.name || '', company: session?.company || '', taxCode: session?.taxCode || '', email: session?.email || '', address: session?.address || '' });
  const [editingProfile, setEditingProfile] = useState(false);
  const [profileBusy, setProfileBusy] = useState(false);
  const [profileError, setProfileError] = useState('');
  const [addresses, setAddresses] = useState<Awaited<ReturnType<typeof api.addresses>>['addresses']>([]);
  const [showAddressForm, setShowAddressForm] = useState(false);
  const [editingAddressId, setEditingAddressId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [addressForm, setAddressForm] = useState({ label: '', address: '', contactName: session?.name || '', contactPhone: session?.phone || '', isDefault: false });
  const [addressError, setAddressError] = useState('');
  const [addressBusy, setAddressBusy] = useState(false);
  const addressFailure = (cause: unknown, fallback: string) => cause instanceof ApiError ? errorText(cause) : t(fallback);
  const reloadAddresses = () => api.addresses().then(result => { setAddresses(result.addresses); setAddressError(''); }).catch(cause => setAddressError(addressFailure(cause, 'Không tải được sổ địa chỉ')));
  useEffect(() => { let live = true; api.account().then(({ account }) => { if (live) { setProfile(account); setProfileForm({ name: account.name, company: account.company, taxCode: account.taxCode, email: account.email, address: account.address }); } }).catch(() => undefined); return () => { live = false; }; }, []);
  useEffect(() => { let live = true; api.addresses().then(r => { if (live) setAddresses(r.addresses); }).catch(cause => { if (live) setAddressError(addressFailure(cause, 'Không tải được sổ địa chỉ')); }); return () => { live = false; }; }, []);
  const rows = [
    { icon: Building2, label: t('Công ty / cửa hàng'), value: profile?.company || session?.company || '—' },
    { icon: Phone, label: t('Số điện thoại'), value: session?.phone || '—' },
    { icon: Mail, label: t('Email'), value: session?.email || '—' },
    { icon: MapPin, label: t('Địa chỉ công ty / xuất hóa đơn'), value: profile?.address || session?.address || '—' },
    { icon: MapPin, label: t('Địa chỉ giao hàng mặc định'), value: addresses.find(address => address.isDefault)?.address || session?.defaultShippingAddress?.address || session?.address || '—' },
    { icon: Building2, label: t('Mã số thuế'), value: profile?.taxCode || session?.taxCode || '—' },
  ];
  return <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl space-y-5 pb-6">
    <header className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3"><div><h1 className="text-2xl font-black text-[#17382a]">{t('Tài khoản của tôi')}</h1></div><button onClick={() => { logout(); navigate('/dang-nhap'); }} className="flex min-h-10 shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-rose-200 bg-white px-3 text-sm font-bold text-rose-700"><LogOut size={16}/>{t('Đăng xuất')}</button></header>
    <section className="overflow-hidden rounded-3xl bg-linear-to-br from-[#0b4f34] to-[#13875b] p-5 text-white shadow-lg sm:p-7">
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3"><label className="relative flex size-14 cursor-pointer items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-2xl bg-white/15 text-xl font-black">{profile?.avatarUrl ? <img src={profile.avatarUrl} alt={t('Ảnh đại diện')} className="size-full object-cover" /> : (profile?.name || session?.name || 'K').slice(0,1).toUpperCase()}{profileBusy && <span className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/35"><Loader2 size={20} className="animate-spin" /></span>}<input type="file" accept="image/*" className="sr-only" disabled={profileBusy} onChange={async event => {
          const input = event.target;
          const file = input.files?.[0];
          if (!file) return;
          setProfileBusy(true); setProfileError('');
          try {
            // Ảnh chụp điện thoại thường vài MB, vượt giới hạn 2 MB → thu nhỏ trên máy trước khi gửi.
            const small = await resizeImageFile(file, 1024);
            const result = await api.uploadAvatar(small);
            setProfile(result.account);
          } catch (cause) {
            setProfileError(cause instanceof ApiError ? errorText(cause) : t('Không lưu được ảnh đại diện'));
          } finally { setProfileBusy(false); input.value = ''; }
        }} /></label>
        <span className="rounded-full bg-white/15 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold">{session?.verificationStatus === 'pending' ? t('Chưa xác minh') : session?.verificationStatus === 'rejected' ? t('Đã từ chối') : t('Đã xác minh')}</span></div>
      <h2 className="mt-4 text-xl font-extrabold">{profile?.name || session?.name || session?.company}</h2><p className="mt-1 text-sm text-white/75">{t('Mã khách hàng')}: <strong className="font-mono text-white">{session?.code}</strong> · {t('Chạm ảnh để đổi')}</p>
    </section>
    <section className="rounded-2xl border border-[#17231d]/10 bg-white p-4 shadow-sm"><div className="flex items-center justify-between"><h3 className="font-bold text-[#17382a]">{t('Hồ sơ')}</h3><button type="button" onClick={() => setEditingProfile(value => !value)} className="text-sm font-bold text-[#0f7a4f]">{t(editingProfile ? 'Đóng' : 'Chỉnh sửa')}</button></div>
      {editingProfile && <form className="mt-3 grid gap-3" onSubmit={async event => { event.preventDefault(); setProfileBusy(true); setProfileError(''); try { const result = await api.saveAccount(profileForm); setProfile(result.account); if (session) setSession({ ...session, name: result.account.name, email: result.account.email, address: result.account.address }); setEditingProfile(false); } catch { setProfileError(t('Không lưu được hồ sơ')); } finally { setProfileBusy(false); } }}>{([['name','Tên người liên hệ'],['email','Email liên hệ'],['company','Tên công ty / cửa hàng'],['taxCode','Mã số thuế'],['address','Địa chỉ công ty / xuất hóa đơn']] as const).map(([key,label]) => <label key={key} className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#53695c]">{t(label)}<input type={key === 'email' ? 'email' : 'text'} required={key === 'name' || key === 'company'} value={profileForm[key]} onChange={event => setProfileForm(form => ({ ...form, [key]: event.target.value }))} className="mt-1 h-11 w-full rounded-lg border border-[#d7e3d9] px-3 text-sm" /></label>)}<p className="text-xs text-[#718579]">{t('Tên công ty/MST cần TPS1 duyệt')}</p><button disabled={profileBusy} className="min-h-11 rounded-lg bg-[#0f7a4f] font-bold text-white disabled:opacity-50">{t('Lưu hồ sơ')}</button></form>}
      {profile?.request?.status === 'pending' && <p className="mt-3 rounded-lg bg-amber-50 p-2 text-xs text-amber-900">{t('Tên công ty/MST đang chờ duyệt')}: {profile.request.company} · {profile.request.tax_code || '—'}</p>}
      {profile?.request?.status === 'rejected' && <p className="mt-3 text-xs text-rose-700">{t('Yêu cầu thay đổi đã bị từ chối')}</p>}
      {profileError && <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-rose-700">{profileError}</p>}
    </section>
    <section className="rounded-2xl border border-[#17231d]/10 bg-white p-4 shadow-sm">
      <h3 className="font-bold text-[#17382a]">{t('Tên đăng nhập')}</h3>
      <p className="mt-1 text-sm font-semibold text-[#263e31]">{profile?.username || '—'}</p>
    </section>
    <section className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-[#17231d]/10 bg-white shadow-sm"><h3 className="border-b border-[#17231d]/8 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-[#17382a]">{t('Thông tin khách hàng')}</h3>
      {rows.map(({icon: Icon,label,value}) => <div key={label} className="flex gap-3 border-b border-[#17231d]/5 px-4 py-3.5 last:border-0"><span className="mt-0.5 text-[#0f7a4f]"><Icon size={18}/></span><div className="min-w-0"><p className="text-xs text-[#75857b]">{label}</p><p className="mt-0.5 break-words text-sm font-semibold text-[#263e31]">{value}</p></div></div>)}
    </section>
    <section className="space-y-3 rounded-2xl border border-[#17231d]/10 bg-white p-4 shadow-sm">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between"><h3 className="font-bold text-[#17382a]">{t('Sổ địa chỉ')}</h3><button onClick={()=>{setEditingAddressId(null);setAddressForm({label:'',address:'',contactName:profile?.name||session?.name||'',contactPhone:session?.phone||'',isDefault:false});setShowAddressForm(true);}} className="flex min-h-10 items-center gap-1 rounded-lg bg-[#eaf6ef] px-3 text-sm font-bold text-[#0f7a4f]"><Plus size={16}/>{t('Thêm địa chỉ')}</button></div>
      {addresses.map(a=><div key={a.id} className="rounded-xl border border-[#e2eae4] p-3"><div className="flex items-start gap-2"><MapPin size={17} className="mt-0.5 text-[#0f7a4f]"/><div className="min-w-0 flex-1"><p className="font-bold text-[#263e31]">{a.label} {a.isDefault&&<span className="ml-1 rounded-full bg-emerald-50 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] text-emerald-700">{t('Mặc định')}</span>}</p><p className="mt-1 text-sm text-[#53695c]">{a.address}</p><p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[#718579]">{a.contactName} · {a.contactPhone}</p></div><button aria-label={t('Sửa địa chỉ')} onClick={()=>{setEditingAddressId(a.id);setAddressForm({label:a.label,address:a.address,contactName:a.contactName,contactPhone:a.contactPhone,isDefault:a.isDefault});setShowAddressForm(true);}} className="p-2 text-[#0f7a4f]"><Pencil size={17}/></button><button aria-label={t('Xóa địa chỉ')} onClick={()=>api.deleteAddress(a.id).then(reloadAddresses)} className="p-2 text-rose-600"><Trash2 size={17}/></button></div>{!a.isDefault&&<button onClick={()=>api.setDefaultAddress(a.id).then(reloadAddresses)} className="mt-2 text-xs font-bold text-[#0f7a4f]">{t('Đặt làm mặc định')}</button>}</div>)}
      {showAddressForm&&<form className="grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2" onSubmit={async e=>{e.preventDefault();if(addressBusy)return;setAddressBusy(true);setAddressError('');try{if(editingAddressId){await api.updateAddress(editingAddressId,addressForm);}else{await api.addAddress(addressForm);}setEditingAddressId(null);setShowAddressForm(false);await reloadAddresses();}catch(cause){setAddressError(addressFailure(cause,'Không lưu được địa chỉ'));}finally{setAddressBusy(false);}}}>{([['label','Tên địa chỉ',2],['address','Địa chỉ giao hàng',5],['contactName','Người nhận',2],['contactPhone','Số điện thoại',8]] as const).map(([key,label,minLength])=><label key={key} className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#53695c]">{t(label)}<input required minLength={minLength} value={addressForm[key]} onChange={e=>setAddressForm(v=>({...v,[key]:e.target.value}))} className="mt-1 h-11 w-full rounded-lg border border-[#d7e3d9] px-3 text-sm"/></label>)}{!editingAddressId&&<label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm"><input type="checkbox" checked={addressForm.isDefault} onChange={e=>setAddressForm(v=>({...v,isDefault:e.target.checked}))}/>{t('Đặt làm mặc định')}</label>}<button disabled={addressBusy} className="min-h-11 rounded-lg bg-[#0f7a4f] font-bold text-white disabled:opacity-60">{t('Lưu địa chỉ')}</button><button type="button" onClick={()=>{setShowAddressForm(false);setEditingAddressId(null);}} className="min-h-11 rounded-lg border border-[#d7e3d9] text-sm font-semibold">{t('Hủy')}</button></form>}
      {addressError&&<p className="text-sm text-rose-700">{addressError}</p>}
    </section>
    <section className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-[#17231d]/10 bg-white shadow-sm">
      <Link to="/doi-mat-khau" className="flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-4 text-sm font-semibold text-[#263e31]"><KeyRound size={18} className="text-[#0f7a4f]"/>{t('Đổi mật khẩu')}<ChevronRight size={17} className="ml-auto text-[#829087]"/></Link>
    </section>
    <p className="flex items-center gap-2 px-1 text-xs text-[#718579]"><ShieldCheck size={16} className="text-[#0f7a4f]"/>{t('Thông tin tài khoản được bảo mật')}</p>
  </div>;
}

📄 2. FULL SOURCE CODE CHUẨN PRODUCTION 100% (282 dòng) — Bản hoàn chỉnh của order-webapp/src/pages/CartPage.tsx 
import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { Link, useNavigate, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ArrowRight, Check, CheckCircle2, Info, Minus, Plus, ShoppingBag, ShoppingCart, Trash2, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { useCart, lineKeyOf } from '../contexts/CartContext';
import { useAuth } from '../contexts/AuthContext';
import { useLanguage } from '../contexts/LanguageContext';
import { useOrderSubmit } from '../hooks/useOrderSubmit';
import CartSummarySidebar from '../components/products/CartSummarySidebar';
import PushOptIn from '../components/store/PushOptIn';
import UnlistedItemEntry from '../components/products/UnlistedItemEntry';
import ExcelOrderSection from '../components/cart/ExcelOrderSection';
import { ProductImage, money, stepOf } from '../components/store/StoreProductCard';
import type { OrderItem } from '../components/products/OrderTabsBar';

function CartLineRow({ item }: { item: OrderItem }) {
  const { updateQty, updateNote, removeItem } = useCart();
  const { t, productName, productUnit } = useLanguage();
  const key = lineKeyOf(item);
  const step = stepOf(item.product);
  const priced = !item.product.isUnlisted && !item.product.priceOnRequest && item.product.price > 0;

  const [isEditingNote, setIsEditingNote] = useState(false);
  const [draftNote, setDraftNote] = useState(item.note || '');

  useEffect(() => {
    setDraftNote(item.note || '');
  }, [item.note]);

  const commitNote = () => {
    const trimmed = draftNote.trim();
    updateNote(key, trimmed);
    setIsEditingNote(false);
  };

  const cancelEditNote = () => {
    setDraftNote(item.note || '');
    setIsEditingNote(false);
  };

  const content = (
    <>
      <p className="line-clamp-2 text-sm font-semibold text-[#17231d]">{productName(item.product.name)}</p>
      <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-[#59665f]">
        {productUnit(item.product.unit || 'Kg')} ·{' '}
        {item.product.isUnlisted
          ? <span className="text-amber-700">{t('Hàng ngoài danh mục · chờ báo giá')}</span>
          : priced ? money(item.product.price) : <span className="text-[#c4601f]">{t('Chờ báo giá')}</span>}
      </p>
    </>
  );

  return (
    <li className="flex gap-3 p-3 sm:p-4">
      {item.product.isUnlisted
        ? <ProductImage product={item.product} className="size-16 shrink-0 rounded-xl sm:size-20" />
        : <Link to={`/san-pham/${item.product.id}`} className="shrink-0"><ProductImage product={item.product} className="size-16 rounded-xl sm:size-20" /></Link>}
      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1 space-y-2">
        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
          <div className="min-w-0">
            {item.product.isUnlisted ? content : <Link to={`/san-pham/${item.product.id}`} className="block hover:text-[#0b4f34]">{content}</Link>}
          </div>
          <button type="button" onClick={() => removeItem(key)} aria-label={t('Xoá')} className="flex size-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg text-[#8a9690] hover:bg-red-50 hover:text-red-600">
            <Trash2 size={16} />
          </button>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <div className="flex h-10 items-center rounded-xl border border-[#17231d]/12 bg-[#f8faf7]">
            <button type="button" aria-label={t('Giảm')} onClick={() => updateQty(key, Number((item.quantity - step).toFixed(3)))} className="flex size-10 items-center justify-center text-[#0f7a4f]"><Minus size={15} /></button>
            <input
              type="number"
              inputMode="decimal"
              step="any"
              min={0}
              value={item.quantity}
              onChange={(e) => { const v = parseFloat(e.target.value); updateQty(key, Number.isNaN(v) ? 0 : v); }}
              className="w-14 bg-transparent text-center text-sm font-bold focus:outline-none"
            />
            <button type="button" aria-label={t('Tăng')} onClick={() => updateQty(key, Number((item.quantity + step).toFixed(3)))} className="flex size-10 items-center justify-center text-[#0f7a4f]"><Plus size={15} /></button>
          </div>
          <p className="text-base font-black text-[#0b4f34]">{priced ? money(item.quantity * item.product.price) : '—'}</p>
        </div>

        {/* Khóa ghi chú khi bấm Enter hoặc click ✓ */}
        {isEditingNote ? (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 pt-0.5">
            <input
              type="text"
              autoFocus
              value={draftNote}
              onChange={(e) => setDraftNote(e.target.value)}
              onKeyDown={(e) => {
                if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                  e.preventDefault();
                  commitNote();
                } else if (e.key === 'Escape') {
                  cancelEditNote();
                }
              }}
              onBlur={commitNote}
              placeholder={t('Ghi chú cho mặt hàng (cách sơ chế, quy cách...)')}
              className="h-8 min-w-0 flex-1 rounded-lg border border-[#0f7a4f]/50 bg-white px-2.5 text-xs text-[#17231d] shadow-2xs focus:border-[#0f7a4f] focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#0f7a4f]"
            />
            <button
              type="button"
              onMouseDown={(e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                commitNote();
              }}
              aria-label={t('Lưu ghi chú')}
              title={t('Lưu')}
              className="flex size-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-[#0f7a4f] text-white hover:bg-[#0b4f34] transition"
            >
              <Check size={14} />
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onMouseDown={(e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                cancelEditNote();
              }}
              aria-label={t('Hủy')}
              title={t('Hủy')}
              className="flex size-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-[#17231d]/8 text-[#59665f] hover:bg-[#17231d]/15 transition"
            >
              <X size={14} />
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : item.note ? (
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-lg bg-emerald-50/80 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs text-[#0b4f34] ring-1 ring-emerald-200/60">
            <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 break-words font-medium">
              📝 <strong className="font-semibold">{t('Ghi chú')}:</strong> {item.note}
            </span>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1 shrink-0">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setDraftNote(item.note || '');
                  setIsEditingNote(true);
                }}
                className="text-[11px] font-bold text-[#0f7a4f] hover:underline px-1 py-0.5 rounded hover:bg-emerald-100/50"
              >
                {t('Sửa')}
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => updateNote(key, '')}
                aria-label={t('Xóa ghi chú')}
                title={t('Xóa')}
                className="text-[#59665f] hover:text-red-600 p-0.5 rounded"
              >
                <X size={13} />
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              setDraftNote('');
              setIsEditingNote(true);
            }}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[11.5px] font-medium text-[#59665f] hover:text-[#0f7a4f] hover:underline pt-0.5"
          >
            <span>+</span> <span>{t('Ghi chú cho mặt hàng (cách sơ chế, quy cách...)')}</span>
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
    </li>
  );
}

export default function CartPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const isExcel = searchParams.get('excel') === '1';
  const excelSectionRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  const { session } = useAuth();
  const { t } = useLanguage();
  const { tab, updateTab, updateQty, removeItem, addUnlisted, repricedCount, dismissRepriced } = useCart();
  const { submit, submitting, errorMessage, successOrder, clearSuccess, cutoffInfo } = useOrderSubmit();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isExcel && excelSectionRef.current) {
      excelSectionRef.current.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
    }
  }, [isExcel]);

  if (successOrder) {
    return (
      <div className="flex min-h-[60vh] items-center justify-center p-2">
        <div className="w-full max-w-md space-y-4 rounded-3xl bg-white p-8 text-center shadow-xl shadow-black/5 ring-1 ring-[#17231d]/8">
          <div className="mx-auto flex size-20 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-emerald-50 text-[#0f7a4f] ring-1 ring-emerald-200"><CheckCircle2 size={42} /></div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-black text-[#17231d]">{t('Cảm ơn quý khách, TPS1 đã nhận được đơn!')}</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-[#59665f]">
            {t('Mã đơn hàng:')} <strong className="rounded-lg bg-[#0f7a4f]/10 px-2 py-0.5 font-mono text-base text-[#0f7a4f]">{successOrder.code}</strong>. {t('Đội ngũ TPS1 sẽ liên hệ xác nhận giá và lịch giao trong thời gian sớm nhất.')}
          </p>
          {session?.verificationStatus === 'pending' && <p className="rounded-xl bg-amber-50 p-3 text-sm text-amber-900">{t('Tài khoản của quý khách đang chờ xác minh. TPS1 sẽ xác minh tài khoản và xác nhận đơn cùng lúc.')}</p>}
          {successOrder.pendingPriceCount > 0 && <p className="rounded-xl bg-amber-50 p-3 text-sm text-amber-900">{t('Đơn có mặt hàng chưa có giá. Nhân viên kinh doanh sẽ báo giá, hoặc bỏ món tạm hết hàng, trước khi xác nhận đơn với quý khách.')}</p>}
          <PushOptIn />
          <div className="flex flex-col justify-center gap-3 pt-2 sm:flex-row">
            <button type="button" onClick={() => { clearSuccess(); navigate('/'); }} className="flex h-11 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-[#17231d]/15 px-5 text-sm font-bold text-[#17231d] hover:bg-[#f5f7f3]"><ShoppingBag size={16} /> {t('Tiếp tục mua hàng')}</button>
            <button type="button" onClick={() => { clearSuccess(); navigate('/don-hang'); }} className="flex h-11 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl bg-[#0f7a4f] px-5 text-sm font-bold text-white hover:bg-[#0b4f34]">{t('Đơn hàng của tôi')} <ArrowRight size={16} /></button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (tab.items.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto my-4 max-w-4xl space-y-5">
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-md space-y-4 rounded-3xl bg-white px-6 py-10 text-center ring-1 ring-[#17231d]/8 shadow-xs">
          <div className="mx-auto flex size-18 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-[#f5f7f3] text-[#59665f]/40"><ShoppingCart size={32} /></div>
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-lg font-bold text-[#17231d]">{t('Giỏ hàng đang trống')}</h1>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-[#59665f]">{t('Quý khách chọn hàng từ cửa hàng, hoặc tải file Excel lên để lên đơn nhanh chóng.')}</p>
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-2">
            <Link to="/" className="inline-flex h-10 items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-[#0f7a4f] px-5 text-sm font-bold text-white hover:bg-[#0b4f34]"><ShoppingBag size={16} /> {t('Xem cửa hàng')}</Link>
          </div>
        </div>

        {session && <ExcelOrderSection ref={excelSectionRef} autoFocusHighlight={isExcel} />}
        {session && <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white p-3 ring-1 ring-[#17231d]/6"><UnlistedItemEntry onAdd={addUnlisted} /></div>}
      </div>
    );
  }

  const handleSubmit = () => {
    if (!session) { navigate(`/dang-nhap?quay-lai=${encodeURIComponent('/gio-hang')}`); return; }
    void submit();
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-black text-[#17231d]">{t('Giỏ hàng')} <span className="text-base font-semibold text-[#59665f]">({tab.items.length} {t('mặt hàng')})</span></h1>

      {repricedCount > 0 && (
        <div className="flex items-start gap-2 rounded-2xl bg-[#eaf6ef] p-3 text-sm text-[#0b4f34] ring-1 ring-[#0f7a4f]/15">
          <Info size={17} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
          <p className="flex-1">{t('Đã cập nhật giá theo hợp đồng của quý khách cho')} {repricedCount} {t('mặt hàng trong giỏ.')}</p>
          <button type="button" onClick={dismissRepriced} aria-label={t('Đóng')} className="text-[#0b4f34]/70"><X size={16} /></button>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="grid items-start gap-4 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_380px]">
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {session && <ExcelOrderSection ref={excelSectionRef} autoFocusHighlight={isExcel} />}

          <ul className="divide-y divide-[#17231d]/6 overflow-hidden rounded-3xl bg-white ring-1 ring-[#17231d]/6">
            {tab.items.map((item) => <CartLineRow key={lineKeyOf(item)} item={item} />)}
          </ul>
          {session && <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white p-3 ring-1 ring-[#17231d]/6"><UnlistedItemEntry onAdd={addUnlisted} /></div>}
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-5 gap-y-2">
            <Link to="/" className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm font-bold text-[#0f7a4f] hover:underline"><ShoppingBag size={15} /> {t('Tiếp tục chọn hàng')}</Link>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="lg:sticky lg:top-37.5">
          <CartSummarySidebar
            session={session}
            activeTab={tab}
            onUpdateTab={updateTab}
            onUpdateQty={updateQty}
            onRemoveItem={removeItem}
            onAddUnlisted={addUnlisted}
            onSubmitOrder={handleSubmit}
            submitting={submitting}
            errorMessage={errorMessage}
            earliestDate={cutoffInfo?.earliestDate}
            cutoffTimeStr={cutoffInfo?.cutoffTimeStr}
            guest={!session}
            sticky={false}
            showItems={false}
          />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

📄 2. FULL SOURCE CODE CHUẨN PRODUCTION 100% (322 dòng) — Bản hoàn chỉnh của order-webapp/src/pages/ExcelOrderPage.tsx 
import { useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { AlertTriangle, CheckCircle2, Download, FileSpreadsheet, Search, ShoppingCart, Upload, XCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { api, ApiError, requestFile, type ExcelMapping, type ExcelMatchResult, type ExcelPreviewResponse, type ExcelProduct, type Product } from '../lib/api';
import { useCart } from '../contexts/CartContext';
import { useAuth } from '../contexts/AuthContext';
import { useLanguage } from '../contexts/LanguageContext';
import { Capacitor } from '@capacitor/core';
import { saveDocument } from '../lib/android-document';

const columns = ['sku', 'name', 'quantity', 'unit', 'note'] as const;
type RowEdit = { name: string; quantity: string; unit: string; note: string };
const keyFor = (row: ExcelMatchResult) => `${row.sourceSheet}:${row.row}`;
const money = (value: number) => new Intl.NumberFormat('vi-VN').format(Number(value) || 0) + 'đ';
const displayNumber = (value: number | null) => value == null ? '' : String(value);
const parseQuantity = (value: string) => Number(value.trim().replace(',', '.'));
const normalizeSearch = (value: string) => value.normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '')
  .replace(/đ/g, 'd').replace(/Đ/g, 'D').toLocaleLowerCase('vi-VN')
  .replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N}]+/gu, ' ').trim();

export default function ExcelOrderPage() {
  const { addItem } = useCart();
  const { session } = useAuth();
  const { t, errorText, productName } = useLanguage();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const fileRef = useRef<File | null>(null);
  const [preview, setPreview] = useState<ExcelPreviewResponse | null>(null);
  const [mapping, setMapping] = useState<ExcelMapping | null>(null);
  const [mappingOpen, setMappingOpen] = useState(false);
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const [edits, setEdits] = useState<Record<string, RowEdit>>({});
  const [chosen, setChosen] = useState<Record<string, ExcelProduct | Product>>({});
  const [unlisted, setUnlisted] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const [searchText, setSearchText] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [searchResults, setSearchResults] = useState<Record<string, Product[]>>({});
  const [rowErrors, setRowErrors] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [visibleCount, setVisibleCount] = useState(30);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [adding, setAdding] = useState(false);
  const [searching, setSearching] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [downloadMessage, setDownloadMessage] = useState('');
  const [downloading, setDownloading] = useState(false);
  const [allowRepeat, setAllowRepeat] = useState(false);

  const duplicateKey = preview && session ? `tps1_excel_added_${session.id}_${preview.fileHash}` : '';
  const duplicate = Boolean(duplicateKey && localStorage.getItem(duplicateKey) === '1');

  const downloadTemplate = async () => {
    if (downloading) return;
    setDownloading(true); setError(''); setDownloadMessage('');
    try {
      const blob = await requestFile('/api/customer/order/import-excel');
      if (blob.size < 100) throw new Error('Invalid Excel file');
      if (Capacitor.getPlatform() === 'android') {
        const data = await new Promise<string>((resolve, reject) => {
          const reader = new FileReader();
          reader.onload = () => resolve(String(reader.result).split(',')[1]);
          reader.onerror = () => reject(reader.error);
          reader.readAsDataURL(blob);
        });
        const result = await saveDocument.save({ name: 'TPS1-mau-dat-hang.xlsx', data });
        if (result.saved) setDownloadMessage(t('File mẫu đã được lưu trên điện thoại'));
      } else {
        const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        const anchor = document.createElement('a');
        anchor.href = url; anchor.download = 'mau-dat-hang.xlsx'; anchor.style.display = 'none';
        document.body.appendChild(anchor); anchor.click(); anchor.remove();
        window.setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1500);
        setDownloadMessage(t('File mẫu đã được tải xuống'));
      }
    } catch (cause) {
      setError(cause instanceof ApiError ? errorText(cause) : t('Không tải được file mẫu, vui lòng thử lại'));
    } finally { setDownloading(false); }
  };

  const loadFile = async (file: File, customMapping?: ExcelMapping) => {
    setLoading(true); setError(''); setRowErrors({}); setAllowRepeat(false);
    try {
      if (file.size > 4 * 1024 * 1024) throw new Error(t('Chỉ nhận file Excel tối đa 4 MB'));
      const response = await api.importExcel(file, customMapping);
      setPreview(response);
      const nextMapping = response.mapping || response.sheets[0]?.detectedMapping || {
        sheet: response.sheets[0]?.name || '', headerRow: 0, columns: {},
      };
      setMapping(nextMapping);
      setMappingOpen(response.needsMapping);
      setVisibleCount(30);
      setChosen({}); setUnlisted(new Set()); setSearchText({}); setSearchResults({});
      setSelected(new Set(response.results.filter((row) => row.status === 'matched').map(keyFor)));
      setEdits(Object.fromEntries(response.results.map((row) => [keyFor(row), {
        name: row.name || row.sku, quantity: displayNumber(row.requestedQuantity), unit: row.unit || row.product?.unit || '', note: row.note,
      }])));
    } catch (cause) {
      setError(cause instanceof ApiError ? errorText(cause) : cause instanceof Error && cause.message === t('Chỉ nhận file Excel tối đa 4 MB') ? cause.message : t('Không đọc được file, vui lòng thử lại'));
    } finally { setLoading(false); }
  };

  const editRow = (key: string, change: Partial<RowEdit>) => {
    setEdits((current) => ({ ...current, [key]: { ...current[key], ...change } }));
    setRowErrors((current) => { const next = { ...current }; delete next[key]; return next; });
  };
  const chooseProduct = (row: ExcelMatchResult, product: ExcelProduct | Product) => {
    const key = keyFor(row);
    setChosen((current) => ({ ...current, [key]: product }));
    setUnlisted((current) => { const next = new Set(current); next.delete(key); return next; });
    setSelected((current) => new Set(current).add(key));
    setSearchResults((current) => ({ ...current, [key]: [] }));
  };
  const chooseUnlisted = (row: ExcelMatchResult) => {
    const key = keyFor(row);
    setUnlisted((current) => new Set(current).add(key));
    setChosen((current) => { const next = { ...current }; delete next[key]; return next; });
    setSelected((current) => new Set(current).add(key));
    setRowErrors((current) => { const next = { ...current }; delete next[key]; return next; });
  };
  const searchProducts = async (row: ExcelMatchResult) => {
    const key = keyFor(row);
    const query = (searchText[key] || row.name || row.sku).trim();
    if (!query) return;
    setSearching(key);
    try {
      const response = await api.products({ search: query, page: 0 });
      let products = response.products || [];
      if (!products.length) {
        const catalog = await api.productCatalog();
        const words = normalizeSearch(query).split(' ').filter(Boolean);
        products = catalog.items.filter(([, sku, name]) => words.every((word) =>
          normalizeSearch(`${sku} ${name} ${productName(name)}`).includes(word)))
          .slice(0, 30).map(([id, sku, name, category, unit, price, priceOnRequest]) => ({
            id, sku, name, category, unit, price, priceOnRequest, imageUrl: null, available: true,
          }));
      }
      setSearchResults((current) => ({ ...current, [key]: products }));
    } catch (cause) { setError(cause instanceof ApiError ? errorText(cause) : t('Không tìm được sản phẩm')); }
    finally { setSearching(''); }
  };

  const addSelected = async () => {
    if (!preview || adding || (duplicate && !allowRepeat)) return;
    const source = preview.results.filter((row) => selected.has(keyFor(row)));
    if (!source.length) { setError(t('Chọn ít nhất một mặt hàng')); return; }
    const invalid: Record<string, string> = {};
    const items = source.flatMap((row) => {
      const key = keyFor(row);
      const product = unlisted.has(key) ? null : chosen[key] || row.product;
      const edit = edits[key];
      const quantity = parseQuantity(edit?.quantity || '');
      if (!product && !unlisted.has(key)) { invalid[key] = t('Chọn sản phẩm TPS1'); return []; }
      if (!Number.isFinite(quantity) || quantity <= 0) { invalid[key] = t('Số lượng phải lớn hơn 0'); return []; }
      if (unlisted.has(key) && (!edit?.name.trim() || !edit?.unit.trim())) {
        invalid[key] = t('Nhập tên và đơn vị hàng ngoài danh mục'); return [];
      }
      return [{ sourceSheet: row.sourceSheet, row: row.row, productId: product?.id || '', quantity,
        unit: (edit?.unit || product?.unit || '').trim(), note: edit?.note || '',
        unlisted: unlisted.has(key), name: unlisted.has(key) ? edit.name.trim() : undefined }];
    });
    if (Object.keys(invalid).length) { setRowErrors(invalid); setError(t('Kiểm tra lại các dòng được đánh dấu')); return; }
    setAdding(true); setError(''); setRowErrors({});
    try {
      const response = await api.resolveExcel(items);
      const grouped = new Map<string, { item: typeof response.items[number]; quantity: number; notes: string[] }>();
      for (const item of response.items) {
        const existing = item.isUnlisted ? undefined : grouped.get(item.id);
        if (existing) { existing.quantity += item.quantity; if (item.note && !existing.notes.includes(item.note)) existing.notes.push(item.note); }
        else grouped.set(item.id, { item, quantity: item.quantity, notes: item.note ? [item.note] : [] });
      }
      for (const { item, quantity, notes } of grouped.values()) {
        addItem({ id: item.id, sku: item.sku, name: item.name, category: null, unit: item.unit,
          imageUrl: item.isUnlisted ? '/product-unlisted.svg' : null, price: item.price, priceOnRequest: item.priceOnRequest, isUnlisted: item.isUnlisted, available: true,
          minOrderQty: item.minOrderQty, orderStep: item.orderStep, enforceOrderStep: item.enforceOrderStep },
          quantity, notes.join('; ').slice(0, 500));
      }
      if (duplicateKey) localStorage.setItem(duplicateKey, '1');
      navigate('/gio-hang');
    } catch (cause) {
      if (cause instanceof ApiError && Array.isArray(cause.details)) {
        setRowErrors(Object.fromEntries(cause.details.map((issue: { sourceSheet: string; row: number; message: string }) =>
          [`${issue.sourceSheet}:${issue.row}`, issue.message])));
      }
      setError(cause instanceof ApiError ? errorText(cause) : t('Không thêm được hàng, vui lòng thử lại'));
    } finally { setAdding(false); }
  };

  const selectedCount = selected.size;
  const skippedCount = (preview?.results.length || 0) - selectedCount;
  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl space-y-5 pb-8">
      <header>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-[#14231c]">{t('Đặt hàng bằng file Excel')}</h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-[#59665f]">{t('Tải file lên → kiểm tra sản phẩm → thêm vào giỏ')}</p>
      </header>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
        <button onClick={downloadTemplate} disabled={downloading} className="flex min-h-11 items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-[#14231c]/15 px-4 font-medium text-[#14231c] disabled:opacity-60">
          <Download size={18}/>{t(downloading ? 'Đang tải file mẫu...' : 'Tải file mẫu')}
        </button>
        <button onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()} disabled={loading} className="flex min-h-11 items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-[#0f7a4f] px-4 font-medium text-white disabled:opacity-50">
          <Upload size={18}/>{t(loading ? 'Đang xử lý...' : 'Chọn file Excel')}
        </button>
        <input ref={fileInputRef} type="file" accept=".xlsx,.xls" className="hidden" onChange={(event) => {
          const file = event.target.files?.[0];
          if (file) { fileRef.current = file; void loadFile(file); }
          event.target.value = '';
        }}/>
      </div>
      {fileRef.current && <p className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-[#59665f]"><FileSpreadsheet size={16}/>{fileRef.current.name}</p>}
      {error && <div role="alert" className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-3 text-sm text-red-700">{error}</div>}
      {downloadMessage && <div role="status" className="rounded-xl border border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 p-3 text-sm text-emerald-800">{downloadMessage}</div>}

      {preview && mapping && (mappingOpen || preview.sheets.length > 1) && (
        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-[#14231c]/10 bg-white p-4 space-y-4">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
            <h2 className="font-bold text-[#14231c]">{t('Chọn bảng và cột hàng')}</h2>
            {!preview.needsMapping && <button className="text-sm font-semibold text-[#0f7a4f]" onClick={() => setMappingOpen((open) => !open)}>{t(mappingOpen ? 'Thu gọn' : 'Đổi sheet hoặc cột')}</button>}
          </div>
          {mappingOpen && <>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
              <label className="text-sm font-semibold">{t('Sheet')}
                <select value={mapping.sheet} onChange={(event) => {
                  const sheet = preview.sheets.find((entry) => entry.name === event.target.value);
                  setMapping(sheet?.detectedMapping || { sheet: event.target.value, headerRow: 0, columns: {} });
                }} className="mt-1 block min-h-10 w-full rounded-lg border px-2">
                  {preview.sheets.map((sheet) => <option key={sheet.name} value={sheet.name}>{sheet.name}</option>)}
                </select>
              </label>
              <label className="text-sm font-semibold">{t('Dòng tiêu đề')}
                <select value={mapping.headerRow} onChange={(event) => setMapping({ ...mapping, headerRow: Number(event.target.value) })} className="mt-1 block min-h-10 w-full rounded-lg border px-2">
                  <option value={0}>{t('Không có tiêu đề')}</option>
                  {(preview.sheets.find((sheet) => sheet.name === mapping.sheet)?.rows || []).map((entry) => <option key={entry.row} value={entry.row}>{t('Dòng')} {entry.row}: {entry.cells.filter(Boolean).slice(0, 3).join(' · ').slice(0, 70)}</option>)}
                </select>
              </label>
              {columns.map((column) => <label key={column} className="text-sm font-semibold">{t(({ sku: 'Mã hàng', name: 'Tên hàng', quantity: 'Số lượng', unit: 'Đơn vị', note: 'Ghi chú' } as const)[column])}
                <select value={mapping.columns[column] ?? -1} onChange={(event) => setMapping({ ...mapping, columns: { ...mapping.columns, [column]: Number(event.target.value) < 0 ? undefined : Number(event.target.value) } })} className="mt-1 block min-h-10 w-full rounded-lg border px-2">
                  <option value={-1}>{t('Không dùng')}</option>
                  {Array.from({ length: 20 }, (_, index) => <option key={index} value={index}>{String.fromCharCode(65 + index)}</option>)}
                </select>
              </label>)}
            </div>
            <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-xl border text-xs">
              <table className="min-w-full whitespace-nowrap"><tbody>
                {(preview.sheets.find((sheet) => sheet.name === mapping.sheet)?.rows || []).slice(0, 18).map((entry) => <tr key={entry.row} className={entry.row === mapping.headerRow ? 'bg-emerald-50 font-bold' : 'border-t'}>
                  <th className="sticky left-0 bg-white px-2 py-1.5 text-left">{entry.row}</th>
                  {entry.cells.slice(0, 12).map((cell, index) => <td key={index} className="max-w-44 truncate px-2 py-1.5">{cell}</td>)}
                </tr>)}
              </tbody></table>
            </div>
            <button onClick={() => fileRef.current && void loadFile(fileRef.current, mapping)} disabled={loading} className="min-h-11 rounded-xl bg-[#0f7a4f] px-5 font-bold text-white disabled:opacity-50">{t('Đọc lại theo cột đã chọn')}</button>
          </>}
        </section>
      )}

      {preview?.summary && <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-4">
        <SummaryTile label={t('Đã khớp')} value={preview.summary.matched || 0} icon={<CheckCircle2 size={17}/>} color="text-emerald-700"/>
        <SummaryTile label={t('Cần chọn')} value={preview.summary.ambiguous || 0} icon={<AlertTriangle size={17}/>} color="text-amber-700"/>
        <SummaryTile label={t('Chưa tìm thấy')} value={preview.summary.notFound || 0} icon={<XCircle size={17}/>} color="text-red-700"/>

      </div>}

        {preview.results.slice(0, visibleCount).map((row) => {
          const key = keyFor(row);
          const product = unlisted.has(key) ? null : chosen[key] || row.product;
          const edit = edits[key];
          const isSelected = selected.has(key);
          return <article key={key} className={`rounded-2xl border bg-white p-3.5 sm:p-4 ${rowErrors[key] ? 'border-red-300' : isSelected ? 'border-emerald-200' : 'border-[#14231c]/10'}`}>
            <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
              <input type="checkbox" aria-label={`${t('Chọn dòng')} ${row.row}`} checked={isSelected} onChange={(event) => setSelected((current) => {
                const next = new Set(current); if (event.target.checked) next.add(key); else next.delete(key); return next;
              })} className="mt-1 size-5 shrink-0 accent-[#0f7a4f]"/>
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-2">
                  <div><p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#59665f]">{row.sourceSheet} · {t('Dòng')} {row.row}</p>
                    <p className="font-semibold text-[#14231c] break-words">{row.name || row.sku}</p>
                    {row.sku && row.name !== row.sku && <p className="text-xs text-[#59665f]">{row.sku}</p>}
                  </div><StatusBadge status={row.status} selected={Boolean(chosen[key])} unlisted={unlisted.has(key)} t={t}/>
                </div>
                {product && <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-[#0f7a4f]">→ {productName(product.name)} ({product.sku}) · {chosen[key] && row.suggestions?.some((candidate) => candidate.id === product.id) ? t('Giá kiểm tra khi thêm vào giỏ') : product.priceOnRequest ? t('Chờ sale báo giá') : money(product.price)}</p>}
                {row.warning && <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-amber-800">{t(row.warning)}</p>}

                <div className="mt-3 grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-[110px_110px_1fr]">
                  <label className="text-xs text-[#59665f]">{t('Số lượng')}<input inputMode="decimal" value={edit?.quantity ?? ''} onChange={(event) => editRow(key, { quantity: event.target.value })} className="mt-1 min-h-10 w-full rounded-lg border px-2 text-sm text-[#14231c]"/></label>
                  <label className="text-xs text-[#59665f]">{t('Đơn vị')}<input value={edit?.unit ?? ''} onChange={(event) => editRow(key, { unit: event.target.value })} className="mt-1 min-h-10 w-full rounded-lg border px-2 text-sm text-[#14231c]"/></label>
                  <label className="col-span-2 text-xs text-[#59665f] sm:col-span-1">{t('Ghi chú')}<input value={edit?.note ?? ''} onChange={(event) => editRow(key, { note: event.target.value })} className="mt-1 min-h-10 w-full rounded-lg border px-2 text-sm text-[#14231c]"/></label>
                </div>
                {(row.suggestions?.length || row.status !== 'matched' || unlisted.has(key)) ? <div className="mt-3 space-y-2">
                  {unlisted.has(key) && row.product && <button onClick={() => chooseProduct(row, row.product!)} className="mr-2 rounded-lg border border-[#14231c]/15 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-left text-xs">{t('Dùng sản phẩm đã khớp')} · {productName(row.product.name)} · {row.product.sku}</button>}
                  {Boolean(row.suggestions?.length) && <div className="max-h-40 space-y-1.5 overflow-y-auto rounded-lg border border-[#14231c]/10 p-1.5">{row.suggestions?.map((candidate) => <button key={candidate.id} onClick={() => chooseProduct(row, candidate)} className={`block min-h-9 w-full rounded-lg border px-2.5 py-1.5 text-left text-xs ${product?.id === candidate.id ? 'border-emerald-600 bg-emerald-50' : 'border-[#14231c]/15'}`}>{productName(candidate.name)} · {candidate.sku} · {candidate.unit}</button>)}</div>}
                  <div className="flex gap-2"><input value={searchText[key] ?? ''} onChange={(event) => setSearchText((current) => ({ ...current, [key]: event.target.value }))} onKeyDown={(event) => { if (event.key === 'Enter') { event.preventDefault(); void searchProducts(row); } }} placeholder={t('Tìm sản phẩm TPS1')} className="min-w-0 min-h-10 flex-1 rounded-lg border px-3 text-sm"/>
                    <button onClick={() => void searchProducts(row)} disabled={searching === key} className="min-h-10 rounded-lg border px-3 text-sm font-semibold disabled:opacity-50"><Search size={17}/></button></div>
                  {searchResults[key]?.length > 0 && <div className="max-h-44 space-y-1 overflow-y-auto rounded-lg border p-2">{searchResults[key].map((candidate) => <button key={candidate.id} onClick={() => chooseProduct(row, candidate)} className="block w-full rounded-lg px-2 py-1.5 text-left text-sm hover:bg-emerald-50">{productName(candidate.name)} · {candidate.sku} · {candidate.unit}</button>)}</div>}
                </div> : null}
                <button type="button" onClick={() => chooseUnlisted(row)} className={`mt-3 min-h-10 rounded-lg border px-3 text-left text-xs font-semibold ${unlisted.has(key) ? 'border-amber-600 bg-amber-50 text-amber-900' : 'border-[#14231c]/15 text-[#59665f]'}`}>
                  {t('Không có trong danh mục · 0đ')}
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </article>;
        })}
        {preview.results.length > visibleCount && <button onClick={() => setVisibleCount((count) => count + 30)} className="w-full rounded-xl border bg-white py-3 font-semibold text-[#0f7a4f]">{t('Hiện thêm mặt hàng')}</button>}
        <div className="sticky bottom-16 z-10 rounded-2xl border border-emerald-200 bg-white/95 p-3 shadow-lg backdrop-blur-sm md:bottom-3">
          <p className="mb-2 text-xs text-[#59665f]">{t('Đã chọn')} {selectedCount}. {skippedCount > 0 && <>{skippedCount} {t('dòng không chọn sẽ bỏ qua')}.</>}</p>
          {duplicate && <label className="mb-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-amber-800"><input type="checkbox" checked={allowRepeat} onChange={(event) => setAllowRepeat(event.target.checked)}/>{t('File này đã thêm vào giỏ. Tôi muốn thêm lại.')}</label>}
          <button onClick={() => void addSelected()} disabled={adding || !selectedCount || (duplicate && !allowRepeat)} className="flex min-h-12 w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-[#0f7a4f] font-bold text-white disabled:opacity-50"><ShoppingCart size={18}/>{t(adding ? 'Đang kiểm tra...' : 'Thêm mặt hàng đã chọn vào giỏ')} ({selectedCount})</button>
        </div>
      </section>}
    </div>
  );
}

function SummaryTile({ label, value, icon, color }: { label: string; value: number; icon: React.ReactNode; color: string }) {
  return <div className="rounded-xl border border-[#14231c]/10 bg-white p-3 text-center"><div className={`flex items-center justify-center gap-1 font-bold ${color}`}>{icon}{value}</div><p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[#59665f]">{label}</p></div>;
}
function StatusBadge({ status, selected, unlisted, t }: { status: ExcelMatchResult['status']; selected: boolean; unlisted: boolean; t: (key: string) => string }) {
  const label = unlisted ? 'Ngoài danh mục' : selected || status === 'matched' ? 'Đã khớp' : status === 'ambiguous' ? 'Cần chọn' : status === 'not_found' ? 'Chưa tìm thấy' : 'Sai số lượng/đơn vị';
  const color = unlisted ? 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-800' : selected || status === 'matched' ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700' : status === 'ambiguous' ? 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700' : 'bg-red-50 text-red-700';
  return <span className={`shrink-0 rounded-full px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-bold ${color}`}>{t(label)}</span>;
}
