📄 2. FULL SOURCE CODE CHUẨN PRODUCTION 100% (126 dòng) — Bản hoàn chỉnh của order-webapp/src/pages/FavoritesPage.tsx 
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Heart, ShoppingBag } from 'lucide-react';
import { api, ApiError, type Product } from '../lib/api';
import { useAuth } from '../contexts/AuthContext';
import { useFavorites } from '../contexts/FavoritesContext';
import { useLanguage } from '../contexts/LanguageContext';
import StoreProductGrid, { StoreGridSkeleton } from '../components/store/StoreProductGrid';

// Cache sản phẩm yêu thích ở cấp module (sống xuyên suốt qua các lần đổi trang)
// Lưu cả null cho các ID không tồn tại trên server để không bao giờ hỏi lại
const favoriteProductCache = new Map<string, Product | null>();
let cachedOwnerKey = '';

/** Danh sách hàng yêu thích; giá lấy mới mỗi lần mở (theo hợp đồng nếu đã đăng nhập). */
export default function FavoritesPage() {
  const { ids } = useFavorites();
  const { session, logout } = useAuth();
  const { t } = useLanguage();

  const ownerKey = session?.id || 'guest';
  if (cachedOwnerKey !== ownerKey) {
    favoriteProductCache.clear();
    cachedOwnerKey = ownerKey;
  }

  const [tick, setTick] = useState(0);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(
    () => ids.some((id) => !favoriteProductCache.has(id))
  );
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  // Tính danh sách sản phẩm hiển thị ĐỒNG BỘ ngay trong lúc render
  // Khi người dùng bấm bỏ yêu thích 1 món, món đó biến mất tức thì (0ms) trên cùng frame
  const visibleProducts = useMemo(
    () =>
      ids
        .map((id) => favoriteProductCache.get(id))
        .filter((p): p is Product => Boolean(p)),
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
    [ids.join(','), tick]
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (ids.length === 0) {
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    const missingIds = ids.filter((id) => !favoriteProductCache.has(id));

    // Toàn bộ ID đã có trong cache (bao gồm khi khách vừa bỏ yêu thích 1 món):
    // Tuyệt đối không gọi API, không bật loading
    if (missingIds.length === 0) {
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    let alive = true;
    // Chỉ bật loading skeleton nếu trong cache chưa có bất kỳ sản phẩm nào thuộc ids hiện tại
    if (!ids.some((id) => Boolean(favoriteProductCache.get(id)))) {
      setLoading(true);
    }
    setError('');

    const chunks: string[][] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < missingIds.length; i += 24) chunks.push(missingIds.slice(i, i + 24));

    Promise.all(chunks.map((chunk) => api.productsByIds(chunk)))
      .then((responses) => {
        if (!alive) return;
        // Đánh dấu null trước cho tất cả missingIds để id không tồn tại không bị query lại
        for (const id of missingIds) {
          if (!favoriteProductCache.has(id)) {
            favoriteProductCache.set(id, null);
          }
        }
        for (const res of responses) {
          for (const p of res.products || []) {
            favoriteProductCache.set(p.id, p);
          }
        }
        setTick((t) => t + 1);
      })
      .catch((err) => {
        if (!alive) return;
        if (err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 401) { logout(); return; }
        setError(t('Không tải được danh sách yêu thích, vui lòng thử lại'));
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (alive) setLoading(false);
      });

    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [ids.join(','), ownerKey]);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div>
        <h1 className="flex items-center gap-2 text-2xl font-black text-[#17231d]">
          <Heart size={22} className="fill-rose-500 text-rose-500" /> {t('Hàng yêu thích')}
        </h1>
        {ids.length > 0 && <p className="text-sm text-[#59665f]">{ids.length} {t('mặt hàng')}</p>}
      </div>

      {error && <p className="rounded-2xl bg-red-50 p-4 text-sm text-red-700">{error}</p>}

      {loading ? (
        <StoreGridSkeleton count={Math.min(ids.length || 6, 14)} />
      ) : visibleProducts.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-md space-y-3 rounded-3xl bg-white px-6 py-12 text-center ring-1 ring-[#17231d]/8">
          <div className="mx-auto flex size-16 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-[#fef5ee] text-[#e0742f]"><Heart size={30} /></div>
          <p className="font-bold text-[#17231d]">{t('Chưa có mặt hàng yêu thích')}</p>
          <p className="text-sm text-[#59665f]">{t('Bấm vào hình trái tim trên mỗi mặt hàng để lưu lại, lần sau đặt chỉ cần mở trang này.')}</p>
          <Link to="/" className="inline-flex h-11 items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-[#0f7a4f] px-6 text-sm font-bold text-white hover:bg-[#0b4f34]"><ShoppingBag size={16} /> {t('Xem cửa hàng')}</Link>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <StoreProductGrid products={visibleProducts} />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

const REMEMBER_KEY = 'tps1_remembered_customer_code';

export default function LoginPage() {
  const [code, setCode] = useState(() => localStorage.getItem(REMEMBER_KEY) || '');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [rememberCode, setRememberCode] = useState(() => Boolean(localStorage.getItem(REMEMBER_KEY)));
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [showHelp, setShowHelp] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  const { t, errorText } = useLanguage();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [params] = useSearchParams();
  const returnTo = safeReturnTo(params.get('quay-lai'));

  useEffect(() => {
    if (rememberCode && code.trim()) localStorage.setItem(REMEMBER_KEY, code.trim());
    else if (!rememberCode) localStorage.removeItem(REMEMBER_KEY);
  }, [rememberCode, code]);

  const handleSubmit = async (event: React.FormEvent) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setError('');
    const cleanCode = code.trim();
    if (!cleanCode) { setError(t('Vui lòng nhập SĐT hoặc tên đăng nhập')); return; }
    if (!password) { setError(t('Vui lòng nhập mật khẩu')); return; }
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      if (rememberCode) localStorage.setItem(REMEMBER_KEY, cleanCode);
      else localStorage.removeItem(REMEMBER_KEY);
      const session = await login(cleanCode, password);
      navigate(session.mustChangePassword ? '/doi-mat-khau' : returnTo, { replace: true });
    } catch (cause) {
      setError(errorText(cause instanceof ApiError ? cause : { message: 'Có lỗi xảy ra, vui lòng thử lại sau.' }));
    } finally { setLoading(false); }

import { Link, useParams, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';

import { api, ApiError, type Product } from '../lib/api';
import { useAuth } from '../contexts/AuthContext';
import { useLanguage } from '../contexts/LanguageContext';

import { searchProducts } from '../lib/productSearch';
import StoreProductGrid, { StoreGridSkeleton, useGridColumns } from '../components/store/StoreProductGrid';
import CategoryRail from '../components/store/CategoryRail';

type Sort = 'default' | 'price_asc' | 'price_desc' | 'name';

function sortProducts(products: Product[], sort: Sort) {
  if (sort === 'default') return products;
  const copy = [...products];
  const price = (p: Product) => (p.priceOnRequest || !(p.price > 0) ? Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY : p.price);
  if (sort === 'price_asc') copy.sort((a, b) => price(a) - price(b));
  if (sort === 'price_desc') copy.sort((a, b) => (price(b) === Infinity ? -1 : price(b)) - (price(a) === Infinity ? -1 : price(a)));
  if (sort === 'name') copy.sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name, 'vi'));
  return copy;
}

function getPageNumbers(current: number, total: number): (number | string)[] {
  if (total <= 7) return Array.from({ length: total }, (_, i) => i + 1);
  if (current <= 4) return [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, '...', total];
  if (current >= total - 3) return [1, '...', total - 4, total - 3, total - 2, total - 1, total];
  return [1, '...', current - 1, current, current + 1, '...', total];
}

const ProductGridSkeleton = () => <StoreGridSkeleton />;

/**
 * /danh-muc            → tất cả danh mục (lưới ảnh)
 * /danh-muc/:slug      → sản phẩm của danh mục (phân trang chuẩn số cột)
 * /tim-kiem?q=…        → kết quả tìm kiếm (phân trang chuẩn số cột)
 */
export default function ProductListingPage({ mode }: { mode: 'categories' | 'category' | 'search' }) {
  const { slug = '' } = useParams();
  const [params, setParams] = useSearchParams();
  const query = (params.get('q') || '').trim();
  const sub = params.get('nhom') || '';
  const currentPage = Math.max(1, Number(params.get('trang')) || 1);

  const { t } = useLanguage();
  const { logout } = useAuth();
  const { data: home } = useStoreHome();
  const category = home?.categories.find((c) => c.slug === slug) || null;
  const subRailRef = useHorizontalScroll<HTMLDivElement>();
  const searchRailRef = useHorizontalScroll<HTMLDivElement>();

  const columns = useGridColumns();
  // Số sản phẩm/trang luôn chia hết cho số cột (đủ 4 hàng kín mít, ví dụ 7 cột x 4 hàng = 28 món)
  const displayPageSize = columns * 4;

  const [products, setProducts] = useState<Product[]>([]);
  const [allMatchedProducts, setAllMatchedProducts] = useState<Product[]>([]);
  const [total, setTotal] = useState(0);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(mode !== 'categories');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [sort, setSort] = useState<Sort>('default');
  const [selectedSearchCategory, setSelectedSearchCategory] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    setSelectedSearchCategory('');
  }, [query]);

  // Đếm số lượng sản phẩm khớp theo từng category (chỉ giữ lại category có count > 0)
  const searchCategoryCounts = useMemo(() => {
    if (mode !== 'search') return [];
    const map = new Map<string, number>();
    for (const p of allMatchedProducts) {
      const cat = (p.category || '').trim();
      if (cat) {
        map.set(cat, (map.get(cat) || 0) + 1);
      }
    }
    return Array.from(map.entries())
      .map(([cat, count]) => ({ cat, count }))
      .sort((a, b) => b.count - a.count || a.cat.localeCompare(b.cat, 'vi'));
  }, [mode, allMatchedProducts]);

  const filteredSearchProducts = useMemo(() => {
    if (mode !== 'search') return [];
    if (!selectedSearchCategory) return allMatchedProducts;
    return allMatchedProducts.filter((p) => (p.category || '').trim() === selectedSearchCategory);
  }, [mode, allMatchedProducts, selectedSearchCategory]);

  const availableSubCategories = useMemo(() => {
    if (!category) return [];
    return category.productCategories || [];
  }, [category]);

  const filterCategories = useMemo(() => {
    if (mode !== 'category' || !category) return null;
    if (sub && availableSubCategories.includes(sub)) {
      return [sub];
    }
    return availableSubCategories;
  }, [mode, category, sub, availableSubCategories]);

  // Chế độ tìm kiếm: Tải toàn bộ sản phẩm khớp query để đếm danh mục và lọc tại chỗ
  useEffect(() => {
    if (mode !== 'search') return;
    if (!query) {
      setAllMatchedProducts([]);
      setTotal(0);
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    const controller = new AbortController();
    setLoading(true);
    setError('');

    api.products({ search: query, page: 0 }, controller.signal)
      .then(async (firstRes) => {
        const firstProducts = firstRes.products || [];
        const totalCount = firstRes.total || firstProducts.length;
        const pageSize = firstRes.pageSize || 24;
        const totalPages = Math.ceil(totalCount / pageSize);
        let all = [...firstProducts];

        if (totalPages > 1) {
          const extraPages = Array.from({ length: Math.min(totalPages - 1, 15) }, (_, i) => i + 1);
          const extraResponses = await Promise.all(
            extraPages.map((p) => api.products({ search: query, page: p }, controller.signal))
          );
          for (const res of extraResponses) {
            all.push(...(res.products || []));
          }
        }

        const seen = new Set<string>();
        all = all.filter((p) => {
          if (seen.has(p.id)) return false;
          seen.add(p.id);
          return true;
        });

        // Lọc lại chuẩn theo nguyên chữ/cụm từ bằng searchProducts (tránh danh mục thừa)
        const strictMatched = searchProducts(
          all,
          query,
          (p) => ({ text: `${p.sku || ''} ${p.name} ${t(p.name)} ${p.category || ''}`, name: t(p.name) }),
          (p) => Boolean(p.thumbUrl || p.imageUrl),
          500
        );
        const finalMatched = strictMatched.length > 0 ? strictMatched : all;

        setAllMatchedProducts(finalMatched);
        setTotal(finalMatched.length);
        setLoading(false);
      })
      .catch((err) => {
        if (controller.signal.aborted) return;
        if (err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 401) { logout(); return; }
        setError(t('Không tải được kết quả tìm kiếm, vui lòng thử lại'));
        setLoading(false);
      });

    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [mode, query, logout, t]);

  // Chế độ xem danh mục: Tải theo trang
  useEffect(() => {
    if (mode !== 'category' || !filterCategories) return;

    const controller = new AbortController();
    setLoading(true);
    setError('');

    const BACKEND_PAGE_SIZE = 24;
    const startIdx = (currentPage - 1) * displayPageSize;
    const endIdx = startIdx + displayPageSize;
    const firstBackendPage = Math.floor(startIdx / BACKEND_PAGE_SIZE);
    const lastBackendPage = Math.floor((endIdx - 1) / BACKEND_PAGE_SIZE);

    const pagesToFetch = [firstBackendPage];
    if (lastBackendPage !== firstBackendPage) {
      pagesToFetch.push(lastBackendPage);
    }

    Promise.all(
      pagesToFetch.map((p) =>
        api.products({ categories: filterCategories, page: p }, controller.signal)
      )
    )
      .then((responses) => {
        const combined: Product[] = [];
        for (const res of responses) {
          combined.push(...(res.products || []));
        }
        const offset = startIdx - firstBackendPage * BACKEND_PAGE_SIZE;
        const sliced = combined.slice(offset, offset + displayPageSize);
        setProducts(sliced);
        setTotal(responses[0]?.total || 0);
      })
      .catch((err) => {
        if (controller.signal.aborted) return;
        if (err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 401) { logout(); return; }
        setError(t('Không tải được sản phẩm, vui lòng thử lại'));
      })
      .finally(() => { if (!controller.signal.aborted) setLoading(false); });

    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [mode, filterCategories, currentPage, displayPageSize, logout, t]);

  if (mode === 'categories') {
    return (
      <div className="space-y-4">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-black text-[#17231d]">{t('Danh mục hàng hóa')}</h1>
        <div className="rounded-3xl bg-white p-4 ring-1 ring-[#17231d]/6 sm:p-6">
          {home ? <CategoryRail categories={home.categories} layout="grid" /> : <ProductGridSkeleton />}
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const title = mode === 'search' ? `${t('Kết quả cho')} “${query}”` : category ? t(category.name) : t('Danh mục');

  const totalCount = mode === 'search' ? filteredSearchProducts.length : total;
  const sorted = sortProducts(mode === 'search' ? filteredSearchProducts : products, sort);
  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(totalCount / displayPageSize));
  const displayedProducts = mode === 'search'
    ? sorted.slice((currentPage - 1) * displayPageSize, currentPage * displayPageSize)
    : sorted;

  const goToPage = (p: number) => {
    const next = new URLSearchParams(params);
    if (p <= 1) next.delete('trang');
    else next.set('trang', String(p));
    setParams(next);
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
  };

  const fromItem = totalCount === 0 ? 0 : (currentPage - 1) * displayPageSize + 1;
  const toItem = Math.min(totalCount, (currentPage - 1) * displayPageSize + displayedProducts.length);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      <nav className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-[#59665f]" aria-label="breadcrumb">
        <Link to="/" className="hover:text-[#0f7a4f]">{t('Trang chủ')}</Link>
        <ChevronRight size={13} />
        {mode === 'category' ? <Link to="/danh-muc" className="hover:text-[#0f7a4f]">{t('Danh mục')}</Link> : <span>{t('Tìm kiếm')}</span>}
        {mode === 'category' && category && (<><ChevronRight size={13} /><span className="font-semibold text-[#17231d]">{t(category.name)}</span></>)}
      </nav>

      {mode === 'search' ? (
        <div className="rounded-3xl bg-white p-4 sm:p-6 ring-1 ring-[#17231d]/8 shadow-xs space-y-4">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-3">
            <div>
              <h1 className="text-xl font-black text-[#17231d] sm:text-2xl">{title}</h1>
              {!loading && (
                <p className="text-sm text-[#59665f]">
                  {selectedSearchCategory ? `${filteredSearchProducts.length} / ` : ''}{allMatchedProducts.length} {t('mặt hàng')}
                </p>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Thanh công cụ Sort & Filter nhóm hàng chuẩn Kamereo */}
          <div className="rounded-2xl bg-[#f8faf8] px-3.5 py-2.5 ring-1 ring-[#17231d]/8 shadow-2xs flex flex-wrap sm:flex-nowrap items-center gap-3">
            <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold text-[#17231d] shrink-0">
              <span className="hidden sm:inline">{t('Sắp xếp theo')}:</span>
              <select
                value={sort}
                onChange={(e) => setSort(e.target.value as Sort)}
                className="h-9 rounded-full border border-[#17231d]/15 bg-white px-3.5 text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-[#17231d] focus:border-[#0f7a4f] focus:outline-none cursor-pointer"
              >
                <option value="default">{t('Phổ biến')}</option>
                <option value="price_asc">{t('Giá thấp đến cao')}</option>
                <option value="price_desc">{t('Giá cao đến thấp')}</option>
                <option value="name">{t('Tên A–Z')}</option>
              </select>
            </label>

            {!loading && searchCategoryCounts.length > 0 && (
              <>
                <div className="hidden sm:block h-5 w-px bg-[#17231d]/12 shrink-0" />

                <div ref={searchRailRef} className="no-scrollbar flex-1 flex items-center gap-2 overflow-x-auto overscroll-contain select-none cursor-grab">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      setSelectedSearchCategory('');
                      goToPage(1);
                    }}
                    className={`h-8 shrink-0 rounded-full px-3.5 text-xs font-bold ring-1 transition cursor-pointer ${
                      !selectedSearchCategory
                        ? 'bg-[#eaf6ef] text-[#0f7a4f] ring-1.5 ring-[#0f7a4f]'
                        : 'bg-white text-[#17231d] ring-1 ring-[#17231d]/15 hover:ring-[#0f7a4f]'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {t('Tất cả')} ({allMatchedProducts.length})
                  </button>
                  {searchCategoryCounts.map(({ cat, count }) => {
                    const isActive = selectedSearchCategory === cat;
                    return (
                      <button
                        key={cat}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => {
                          setSelectedSearchCategory(isActive ? '' : cat);
                          goToPage(1);
                        }}
                        className={`h-8 shrink-0 rounded-full px-3.5 text-xs font-bold ring-1 transition cursor-pointer ${
                          isActive
                            ? 'bg-[#eaf6ef] text-[#0f7a4f] ring-1.5 ring-[#0f7a4f]'
                            : 'bg-white text-[#17231d] ring-1 ring-[#17231d]/15 hover:ring-[#0f7a4f]'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {t(cat)} ({count})
                      </button>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              </>
            )}
          </div>

          {error && <p className="rounded-2xl bg-red-50 p-4 text-sm text-red-700">{error}</p>}

          {loading ? (
            <ProductGridSkeleton />
          ) : displayedProducts.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3 rounded-2xl bg-[#f8faf8] px-6 py-14 text-center ring-1 ring-[#17231d]/6">
              <PackageSearch size={40} className="text-[#59665f]/40" />
              <p className="font-bold text-[#17231d]">{t('Chưa tìm thấy mặt hàng phù hợp')}</p>
              <p className="max-w-sm text-sm text-[#59665f]">{t('Chưa thấy mặt hàng cần tìm? Quý khách có thể ghi tên hàng trong giỏ hàng, TPS1 sẽ báo giá riêng.')}</p>
              <Link to="/gio-hang" className="mt-1 text-sm font-bold text-[#0f7a4f] hover:underline">{t('Mở giỏ hàng')}</Link>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <StoreProductGrid products={displayedProducts} hasMore={false} />
          )}

          {/* Phân trang (Pagination) */}
          {totalPages > 1 && !loading && (
            <div className="mt-6 flex flex-col items-center gap-3 border-t border-[#17231d]/8 pt-4 sm:flex-row sm:justify-between">
              <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-medium text-[#59665f]">
                {t('Hiển thị')} <span className="font-bold text-[#17231d]">{fromItem}–{toItem}</span> / <span className="font-bold text-[#17231d]">{totalCount}</span> {t('mặt hàng')} · {t('Trang')} <span className="font-bold text-[#0f7a4f]">{currentPage}</span>/{totalPages}
              </p>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1 sm:gap-1.5">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  disabled={currentPage <= 1}
                  onClick={() => goToPage(currentPage - 1)}
                  className="flex h-9 items-center gap-1 rounded-lg border border-[#17231d]/12 bg-white px-2.5 text-xs font-bold text-[#17231d] transition hover:bg-[#f5f7f3] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
                >
                  <ChevronLeft size={15} />
                  <span className="hidden sm:inline">{t('Trước')}</span>
                </button>

                {getPageNumbers(currentPage, totalPages).map((p, idx) =>
                  p === '...' ? (
                    <span key={`ellipsis-${idx}`} className="flex h-9 w-7 items-center justify-center text-xs font-bold text-[#8a9690]">
                      …
                    </span>
                  ) : (
                    <button
                      key={p}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => goToPage(Number(p))}
                      className={`flex h-9 min-w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-lg px-2 text-xs font-bold transition ${
                        p === currentPage
                          ? 'bg-[#0f7a4f] text-white shadow-xs'
                          : 'border border-[#17231d]/12 bg-white text-[#17231d] hover:bg-[#f5f7f3]'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {p}
                    </button>
                  )
                )}

                <button
                  type="button"
                  disabled={currentPage >= totalPages}
                  onClick={() => goToPage(currentPage + 1)}
                  className="flex h-9 items-center gap-1 rounded-lg border border-[#17231d]/12 bg-white px-2.5 text-xs font-bold text-[#17231d] transition hover:bg-[#f5f7f3] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
                >
                  <span className="hidden sm:inline">{t('Sau')}</span>
                  <ChevronRight size={15} />
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-3">
            <div>
              <h1 className="text-xl font-black text-[#17231d] sm:text-2xl">{title}</h1>
              {!loading && <p className="text-sm text-[#59665f]">{total} {t('mặt hàng')}</p>}
            </div>
            <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-[#59665f]">
              <span className="hidden sm:inline">{t('Sắp xếp')}</span>
              <select value={sort} onChange={(e) => setSort(e.target.value as Sort)} className="h-10 rounded-xl border border-[#17231d]/12 bg-white px-3 text-sm font-semibold text-[#17231d] focus:border-[#0f7a4f] focus:outline-none">
                <option value="default">{t('Phổ biến')}</option>
                <option value="price_asc">{t('Giá thấp đến cao')}</option>
                <option value="price_desc">{t('Giá cao đến thấp')}</option>
                <option value="name">{t('Tên A–Z')}</option>
              </select>
            </label>
          </div>

          {mode === 'category' && category && availableSubCategories.length > 1 && (
            <div ref={subRailRef} className="no-scrollbar flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-1 overscroll-contain select-none cursor-grab">
              {['', ...availableSubCategories].map((value) => (
                <button
                  key={value || 'all'}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    const next = new URLSearchParams(params);
                    if (value) next.set('nhom', value);
                    else next.delete('nhom');
                    next.delete('trang'); // Tự động về trang 1 khi đổi nhóm
                    setParams(next, { replace: true });
                  }}
                  className={`h-9 shrink-0 rounded-full px-4 text-sm font-semibold ring-1 transition ${sub === value ? 'bg-[#0f7a4f] text-white ring-[#0f7a4f]' : 'bg-white text-[#17231d] ring-[#17231d]/10 hover:ring-[#0f7a4f]/40'}`}
                >
                  {value ? t(value) : t('Tất cả')}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}

          {mode === 'category' && home && !category && (
            <p className="rounded-2xl bg-amber-50 p-4 text-sm text-amber-900">{t('Danh mục này không còn hiển thị.')} <Link to="/danh-muc" className="font-bold underline">{t('Xem danh mục khác')}</Link></p>
          )}
          {error && <p className="rounded-2xl bg-red-50 p-4 text-sm text-red-700">{error}</p>}

          {loading ? (
            <ProductGridSkeleton />
          ) : sorted.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3 rounded-3xl bg-white px-6 py-14 text-center ring-1 ring-[#17231d]/6">
              <PackageSearch size={40} className="text-[#59665f]/40" />
              <p className="font-bold text-[#17231d]">{t('Nhóm hàng này đang được bổ sung')}</p>
              <p className="max-w-sm text-sm text-[#59665f]">{t('Chưa thấy mặt hàng cần tìm? Quý khách có thể ghi tên hàng trong giỏ hàng, TPS1 sẽ báo giá riêng.')}</p>
              <Link to="/gio-hang" className="mt-1 text-sm font-bold text-[#0f7a4f] hover:underline">{t('Mở giỏ hàng')}</Link>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <StoreProductGrid products={sorted} hasMore={false} />
          )}

          {/* Phân trang (Pagination) */}
          {totalPages > 1 && !loading && (
            <div className="mt-6 flex flex-col items-center gap-3 border-t border-[#17231d]/8 pt-4 sm:flex-row sm:justify-between">
              <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-medium text-[#59665f]">
                {t('Hiển thị')} <span className="font-bold text-[#17231d]">{fromItem}–{toItem}</span> / <span className="font-bold text-[#17231d]">{total}</span> {t('mặt hàng')} · {t('Trang')} <span className="font-bold text-[#0f7a4f]">{currentPage}</span>/{totalPages}
              </p>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1 sm:gap-1.5">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  disabled={currentPage <= 1}
                  onClick={() => goToPage(currentPage - 1)}
                  className="flex h-9 items-center gap-1 rounded-lg border border-[#17231d]/12 bg-white px-2.5 text-xs font-bold text-[#17231d] transition hover:bg-[#f5f7f3] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
                >
                  <ChevronLeft size={15} />
                  <span className="hidden sm:inline">{t('Trước')}</span>
                </button>

                {getPageNumbers(currentPage, totalPages).map((p, idx) =>
                  p === '...' ? (
                    <span key={`ellipsis-${idx}`} className="flex h-9 w-7 items-center justify-center text-xs font-bold text-[#8a9690]">
                      …
                    </span>
                  ) : (
                    <button
                      key={p}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => goToPage(Number(p))}
                      className={`flex h-9 min-w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-lg px-2 text-xs font-bold transition ${
                        p === currentPage
                          ? 'bg-[#0f7a4f] text-white shadow-xs'
                          : 'border border-[#17231d]/12 bg-white text-[#17231d] hover:bg-[#f5f7f3]'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {p}
                    </button>
                  )
                )}

                <button
                  type="button"
                  disabled={currentPage >= totalPages}
                  onClick={() => goToPage(currentPage + 1)}
                  className="flex h-9 items-center gap-1 rounded-lg border border-[#17231d]/12 bg-white px-2.5 text-xs font-bold text-[#17231d] transition hover:bg-[#f5f7f3] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
                >
                  <span className="hidden sm:inline">{t('Sau')}</span>
                  <ChevronRight size={15} />
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
#27 order-webapp/src/pages/ProductsPage.tsx Chỉnh sửa (M) +208 / -34 · Tổng 680 dòng

Đặc tả kỹ thuật cho AI: Trang Đặt hàng nhanh (/dat-hang): bỏ thanh tìm kiếm trùng lặp bên dưới; thay nút Xem thêm bằng Phân trang chẵn cột (12/9/8 món/trang); đưa tiêu đề TẤT CẢ SẢN PHẨM lên trên khung 2 cột và dùng ResizeObserver đồng bộ chiều cao giữa lưới sản phẩm và CartSummarySidebar (chuẩn OCD); tăng pb-24 trên mobile để CartFloatingBar không che mất nút Phân trang; thêm min-w-0 w-full max-w-full chống giãn khung khi bật F12.

import { useState, useEffect, useRef, useCallback, useMemo } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Clock } from 'lucide-react';
import { searchProducts } from '../lib/productSearch';
import {
  api,
  type Product,
  type ProductCatalogResponse,
  type Order,
  type FrequentItem,
  ApiError,
} from '../lib/api';
import { useAuth } from '../contexts/AuthContext';
import { useCart } from '../contexts/CartContext';
import { useOrderSubmit } from '../hooks/useOrderSubmit';
import { useFavorites } from '../contexts/FavoritesContext';
import { useLanguage } from '../contexts/LanguageContext';

import QuickOrderSections from '../components/products/QuickOrderSections';
import ProductGrid from '../components/products/ProductGrid';
import CartSummarySidebar from '../components/products/CartSummarySidebar';
import CartFloatingBar from '../components/products/CartFloatingBar';
import CartCheckoutDrawer from '../components/products/CartCheckoutDrawer';
import OrderSuccessModal from '../components/products/OrderSuccessModal';

const PRODUCT_CACHE_TTL_MS = 2 * 60 * 1000;
const productSearchCache = new Map<string, { expiresAt: number; products: Product[] }>();
const PRODUCT_CATALOG_TTL_MS = 5 * 60 * 1000;
const productCatalogMemoryCache = new Map<string, { expiresAt: number; products: Product[] }>();
function decodeCatalog(catalog: ProductCatalogResponse): Product[] {
  const imageBaseUrl = catalog.imageBaseUrl.replace(/\/$/, '');
  return catalog.items.map(([id, sku, name, category, unit, price, priceOnRequest, hasImage]) => {
    const imageUrl = hasImage && imageBaseUrl ? `${imageBaseUrl}/${id}.webp` : null;
    const product: Product = {
      id,
      sku,
      name,
      category,
      unit,
      price: Number(price) || 0,
      priceOnRequest,
      imageUrl,
      thumbUrl: imageUrl,
      available: true,
    };
    return product;
  });
}

function searchLocalCatalog(products: Product[], rawQuery: string, productName: (name: string) => string): Product[] {
  return searchProducts(
    products,
    rawQuery,
    (product) => ({
      text: `${product.sku || ''} ${product.name} ${productName(product.name)} ${product.category || ''}`,
      name: productName(product.name),
    }),
    (product) => Boolean(product.thumbUrl || product.imageUrl),
  );
}

export default function ProductsPage() {
  const { session, logout } = useAuth();
  const { t, productName } = useLanguage();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  // Giỏ hàng chung của cả app (CartContext) và luồng gửi đơn dùng chung.
  const {
    tab: activeTab,
    updateTab: updateActiveTab,
    addProduct,
    addUnlisted,
    updateQty,
    updateNote,
    removeItem,
  } = useCart();
  const { submit, submitting, errorMessage, setErrorMessage, successOrder, clearSuccess, cutoffInfo } = useOrderSubmit();

  const [searchParams, setSearchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const urlQuery = searchParams.get('q') || '';

  // 2. Search & Catalog state
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState(urlQuery);
  const [searchCategoryFilter, setSearchCategoryFilter] = useState('');
  const [searchResults, setSearchResults] = useState<Product[]>([]);
  const [searchLoading, setSearchLoading] = useState(false);
  const [initialProducts, setInitialProducts] = useState<Product[]>([]);
  const [catalogProducts, setCatalogProducts] = useState<Product[]>([]);
  const [catalogLoading, setCatalogLoading] = useState(true);
  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState(1);
  const [pageSize, setPageSize] = useState(8);
  const gridTopRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const productGridWrapRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [sidebarHeight, setSidebarHeight] = useState<number | undefined>(undefined);
  const [selectedCategory, setSelectedCategory] = useState('');
  const [recentOrders, setRecentOrders] = useState<Order[]>([]);
  const [frequentItems, setFrequentItems] = useState<FrequentItem[]>([]);
  const [loadingLatestOrder, setLoadingLatestOrder] = useState(false);
  const hasSubmittedSearch = Boolean(searchQuery.trim());

  useEffect(() => {
    setSearchQuery(urlQuery);
  }, [urlQuery]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setSearchCategoryFilter('');
  }, [searchQuery]);
  // Hàng yêu thích dùng chung với cửa hàng (FavoritesContext).
  const { ids: favoriteIds, toggle: toggleFavorite } = useFavorites();

  // Mobile Checkout Drawer & Success Modal state
  const [isDrawerOpen, setIsDrawerOpen] = useState(false);

  const searchTimeoutRef = useRef<any>(null);
  const searchAbortRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);
  const searchRequestRef = useRef(0);
  const catalogAbortRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);


  // Tìm kiếm qua Search API khi catalog cục bộ chưa sẵn sàng
  const loadProducts = useCallback(
    async (rawQuery: string) => {
      const query = rawQuery.trim();
      const cacheKey = `${session?.id || 'anonymous'}:${query.toLocaleLowerCase('vi-VN')}`;
      const cached = productSearchCache.get(cacheKey);
      if (cached && cached.expiresAt > Date.now()) {
        setSearchResults(cached.products);
        return;
      }

      searchAbortRef.current?.abort();
      const controller = new AbortController();
      searchAbortRef.current = controller;
      const requestId = ++searchRequestRef.current;
      setSearchLoading(true);
      try {
        const res = await api.products(query ? { search: query } : {}, controller.signal);
        if (requestId !== searchRequestRef.current) return;
        const products = res.products || [];
        productSearchCache.set(cacheKey, {
          expiresAt: Date.now() + PRODUCT_CACHE_TTL_MS,
          products,
        });
        setSearchResults(products);
      } catch (err) {
        if (controller.signal.aborted) return;
        if (err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 401) {
          logout();
          navigate('/dang-nhap');
        }
      } finally {
        if (requestId === searchRequestRef.current) setSearchLoading(false);
      }
    },
    [logout, navigate, session?.id]
  );

  // Tải 20 mặt hàng thường đặt
  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    void api
      .frequentItems(controller.signal)
      .then((res) => setFrequentItems(res.items || []))
      .catch(() => undefined);
    return () => controller.abort();
  }, []);

  // Nạp đơn gần nhất để khách có thể đặt lại ngay ở màn hình mặc định.
  useEffect(() => {
    void api.orders()
      .then((res) => setRecentOrders((res.orders || []).slice(0, 1)))
      .catch(() => undefined);
  }, []);

  // Hiển thị nhóm hàng đầu tiên trước. Không bắt khách chờ toàn bộ catalog
  // 5.000+ mặt hàng; tìm kiếm sẽ gọi API theo từ khóa trong lúc catalog nền
  // chưa sẵn sàng.
  useEffect(() => {
    const cached = productCatalogMemoryCache.get(session?.id || '');
    if (cached && cached.expiresAt > Date.now()) return;
    const controller = new AbortController();
    void api.products({}, controller.signal)
      .then((res) => {
        if (controller.signal.aborted) return;
        setInitialProducts(res.products || []);
        setCatalogLoading(false);
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) setCatalogLoading(false);
      });
    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [session?.id]);

  // Tải toàn bộ Catalog vào bộ nhớ ở nền để tìm kiếm tức thì cho các lần sau.
  // Trì hoãn nhẹ để request đầu tiên và khung thao tác của khách được ưu tiên.
  useEffect(() => {
    const customerId = session?.id;
    if (!customerId) return;
    const now = Date.now();
    const memory = productCatalogMemoryCache.get(customerId);
    if (memory && memory.expiresAt > now) {
      setCatalogProducts(memory.products);
      setCatalogLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    const storageKey = `tps1_product_catalog_v1_${customerId}`;
    try {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem(storageKey);
      if (saved) {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(saved) as { expiresAt: number; catalog: ProductCatalogResponse };
        if ((parsed.expiresAt > now || !navigator.onLine) && Array.isArray(parsed.catalog?.items)) {
          const products = decodeCatalog(parsed.catalog);
          productCatalogMemoryCache.set(customerId, { expiresAt: parsed.expiresAt, products });
          setCatalogProducts(products);
          setCatalogLoading(false);
          return;
        }
        localStorage.removeItem(storageKey);
      }
    } catch {
      localStorage.removeItem(storageKey);
    }

    const controller = new AbortController();
    catalogAbortRef.current = controller;
    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => void api
      .productCatalog(controller.signal)
      .then((catalog) => {
        if (controller.signal.aborted) return;
        const products = decodeCatalog(catalog);
        const expiresAt = Date.now() + PRODUCT_CATALOG_TTL_MS;
        productCatalogMemoryCache.set(customerId, { expiresAt, products });
        setCatalogProducts(products);
        setCatalogLoading(false);
        try {
          localStorage.setItem(storageKey, JSON.stringify({ expiresAt, catalog }));
        } catch {}
      })
      .catch((error) => {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) {
          setCatalogLoading(false);
          console.warn('Không tải được catalog nền:', error);
        }
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) setCatalogLoading(false);
      }), 3000);

    return () => {
      window.clearTimeout(timer);
      controller.abort();
    };
  }, [session?.id]);

  // Phím tắt F3 để focus ô tìm kiếm
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'F3') {
        e.preventDefault();
        const headerInput = document.querySelector('header form[role="search"] input') as HTMLInputElement | null;
        headerInput?.focus();
        headerInput?.select();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
  }, []);

  // Debounced search
  useEffect(() => {
    const q = searchQuery.trim();
    if (!q) {
      setSearchResults([]);
      return;
    }

    if (catalogProducts.length > 0) {
      searchAbortRef.current?.abort();
      setSearchLoading(false);
      setSearchResults(searchLocalCatalog(catalogProducts, q, productName));
      return;
    }

    clearTimeout(searchTimeoutRef.current);
    searchTimeoutRef.current = setTimeout(() => void loadProducts(q), 120);

    return () => clearTimeout(searchTimeoutRef.current);
  }, [searchQuery, loadProducts, catalogProducts, productName]);

  // Thao tác Thêm / Sửa số lượng / Ghi chú dòng
  const handleAddProduct = (
    product: Product,
    quantityToAdd: number = 1,
    options?: { resetSearch?: boolean; keepFocus?: boolean }
  ) => {
    addProduct(product, Math.max(0.1, Number(quantityToAdd) || 1));

    if (options?.resetSearch) { setSearchQuery(''); }
    if (options?.keepFocus) {
      const headerInput = document.querySelector('header form[role="search"] input') as HTMLInputElement | null;
      headerInput?.focus();
    }
  };

  const handleAddUnlisted = addUnlisted;
  const handleUpdateQty = updateQty;
  const handleUpdateItemNote = updateNote;
  const handleRemoveItem = removeItem;

  // Nhận yêu cầu "Đặt lại" từ lịch sử đơn sau khi catalog đã sẵn sàng.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (catalogProducts.length === 0) return;
    const raw = sessionStorage.getItem('tps1_reorder_request_v1');
    if (!raw) return;
    sessionStorage.removeItem('tps1_reorder_request_v1');
    try {
      const requested = JSON.parse(raw) as Array<{ productId: string; quantity: number; note?: string }>;
      const productsById = new Map(catalogProducts.map((product) => [product.id, product]));
      const items = requested
        .map((item) => {
          const product = productsById.get(item.productId);
          return product
            ? { product, quantity: Math.max(0.1, Number(item.quantity) || 1), note: item.note || '' }
            : null;
        })
        .filter((item): item is NonNullable<typeof item> => Boolean(item));
      if (items.length === 0) {
        setErrorMessage('Các mặt hàng trong đơn cũ hiện không còn trong danh mục. Vui lòng tìm và chọn lại.');
        return;
      }
      updateActiveTab((tab) => ({ ...tab, items }));
      if (items.length < requested.length) {
        setErrorMessage('Một số mặt hàng cũ không còn trong danh mục và đã được bỏ qua.');
      }
    } catch {
      setErrorMessage('Không đọc được dữ liệu đơn cũ. Vui lòng thử lại.');
    }
  }, [catalogProducts, updateActiveTab]);

  // Danh sách gợi ý từ catalog và lịch sử
  const frequentProducts = useMemo(
    () =>
      frequentItems.slice(0, 10).map((item) => ({
        id: item.productId,
        sku: item.sku || '',
        name: item.name,
        category: item.category || null,
        unit: item.unit || 'Kg',
        price: Number(item.price) || 0,
        priceOnRequest: Boolean(item.priceOnRequest),
        imageUrl: item.imageUrl || null,
        thumbUrl: item.imageUrl || null,
        available: true,
      } satisfies Product)),
    [frequentItems]
  );

  const favoriteProducts = useMemo(
    () =>
      favoriteIds
        .map((id) => catalogProducts.find((p) => p.id === id))
        .filter((p): p is Product => Boolean(p))
        .slice(0, 10),
    [catalogProducts, favoriteIds]
  );

  const latestOrder = recentOrders[0];

  const displayedProducts = useMemo(() => {
    return hasSubmittedSearch ? searchResults : [];
  }, [hasSubmittedSearch, searchResults]);

  const searchResultCategories = useMemo(() => {
    if (!hasSubmittedSearch) return [];
    return Array.from(new Set(searchResults.map((p) => (p.category || '').trim()).filter(Boolean)));
  }, [hasSubmittedSearch, searchResults]);

  const filteredSearchResults = useMemo(() => {
    if (!searchCategoryFilter) return displayedProducts;
    return displayedProducts.filter((p) => (p.category || '').trim() === searchCategoryFilter);
  }, [displayedProducts, searchCategoryFilter]);

  const categories = useMemo(() => [...new Set(catalogProducts.map(p => String(p.category || '').trim()).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b) => a.localeCompare(b,'vi')), [catalogProducts]);
  const browseProducts = useMemo(
    () => {
      const products = catalogProducts.length ? catalogProducts : initialProducts;
      return selectedCategory ? products.filter(product => product.category === selectedCategory) : products;
    },
    [catalogProducts, initialProducts, selectedCategory]
  );
  const sourceProducts = hasSubmittedSearch ? filteredSearchResults : browseProducts;
  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(sourceProducts.length / pageSize));
  const safePage = Math.min(currentPage, totalPages);
  const visibleProducts = sourceProducts.slice((safePage - 1) * pageSize, safePage * pageSize);

  // Khớp chiều cao CartSummarySidebar trên Desktop với Lưới sản phẩm (chống lệch OCD)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!productGridWrapRef.current) return;
    const el = productGridWrapRef.current;
    const updateHeight = () => {
      if (window.innerWidth >= 1024 && visibleProducts.length >= 5) {
        const h = Math.round(el.getBoundingClientRect().height);
        if (h > 400) {
          setSidebarHeight(h);
          return;
        }
      }
      setSidebarHeight(undefined);
    };
    updateHeight();
    const observer = new ResizeObserver(updateHeight);
    observer.observe(el);
    return () => observer.disconnect();
  }, [visibleProducts.length]);

  // Số sản phẩm/trang theo số cột thực tế của lưới (1/2/3/4 cột)
  useEffect(() => {
    const updatePageSize = () => {
      const w = window.innerWidth;
      if (w >= 1536) setPageSize(12); // 4 cột x 3 hàng
      else if (w >= 1280) setPageSize(9); // 3 cột x 3 hàng
      else setPageSize(8); // 2 cột x 4 hàng / mobile
    };
    updatePageSize();
    window.addEventListener('resize', updatePageSize);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('resize', updatePageSize);
  }, []);

  // Về trang 1 khi đổi nhóm hàng hoặc từ khóa
  useEffect(() => {
    setCurrentPage(1);
  }, [selectedCategory, searchQuery]);

  const goToPage = (page: number) => {
    setCurrentPage(Math.min(Math.max(1, page), totalPages));
    gridTopRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
  };

  const pageNumbers: (number | string)[] = (() => {
    if (totalPages <= 7) return Array.from({ length: totalPages }, (_, i) => i + 1);
    if (safePage <= 4) return [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, '...', totalPages];
    if (safePage >= totalPages - 3) return [1, '...', totalPages - 4, totalPages - 3, totalPages - 2, totalPages - 1, totalPages];
    return [1, '...', safePage - 1, safePage, safePage + 1, '...', totalPages];
  })();


  const addOrderAgain = (order: Order) => {
    const productsById = new Map(catalogProducts.map((p) => [p.id, p]));
    order.items.forEach((item) => {
      const p = productsById.get(item.productId);
      if (p) handleAddProduct(p, Number(item.quantity) || 1);
    });
  };

  const loadLatestOrderAndAdd = async () => {
    if (latestOrder) {
      addOrderAgain(latestOrder);
      return;
    }
    setLoadingLatestOrder(true);
    try {
      const res = await api.orders();
      const order = (res.orders || [])[0];
      if (order) {
        setRecentOrders([order]);
        addOrderAgain(order);
      }
    } catch {
    } finally {
      setLoadingLatestOrder(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSubmitOrder = async () => {
    if (await submit()) setIsDrawerOpen(false);
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col min-w-0 w-full max-w-full gap-3.5 sm:gap-4 pb-24 lg:pb-0">
      {/* 1. Header trang: Lời chào & Giờ chốt đơn */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-1 px-0.5">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-black text-[#17231d] tracking-tight">
            {t('Đặt hàng cho bếp')}
          </h1>
        </div>
        <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 self-start sm:self-auto text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#8a5729]">
          <Clock size={13} className="text-[#e0742f]" />
          <span>{cutoffInfo ? `${t('Giao')}: ${cutoffInfo.earliestDate} · ${t('Chốt')}: ${cutoffInfo.cutoffTimeStr}` : t('Đang tải lịch giao')}</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Thao tác đặt nhanh và danh mục chỉ hiện khi chưa gửi từ khóa. */}
      {!hasSubmittedSearch && <QuickOrderSections latestOrder={latestOrder} loadingLatestOrder={loadingLatestOrder} onReorderLatest={loadLatestOrderAndAdd}
        frequentProducts={frequentProducts} favoriteProducts={favoriteProducts} favoriteIds={favoriteIds} onToggleFavorite={toggleFavorite}
        categories={categories} selectedCategory={selectedCategory} onSelectCategory={(category) => { setSelectedCategory(category); }} onAddProduct={handleAddProduct} />}

      {/* 2. Tiêu đề khu vực sản phẩm & Thanh lọc nhóm hàng khi tìm kiếm */}
      <div ref={gridTopRef} className="space-y-2.5 min-w-0 w-full scroll-mt-44">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-1">
          <h2 className="text-sm font-bold text-[#17231d] uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-2">
            <span>
              {hasSubmittedSearch
                ? `${t('Kết quả tìm kiếm')} ("${searchQuery}")`
                : selectedCategory ? productName(selectedCategory) : t('Tất cả sản phẩm')}
            </span>
            <span className="text-xs font-mono font-normal text-[#59665f]">
              ({hasSubmittedSearch ? (searchCategoryFilter ? filteredSearchResults.length : displayedProducts.length) : browseProducts.length} {t('mặt hàng')})
            </span>
          </h2>
          {hasSubmittedSearch && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setSearchParams((prev) => {
                  const next = new URLSearchParams(prev);
                  next.delete('q');
                  return next;
                }, { replace: true });
                setSearchQuery('');
                setSearchResults([]);
                setSearchCategoryFilter('');
              }}
              className="text-xs font-bold text-[#0f7a4f] hover:underline flex items-center gap-1 transition"
            >
              ✕ {t('Xóa tìm kiếm')}
            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Thanh lọc nhóm hàng nhanh khi tìm kiếm trên trang Đặt hàng */}
        {hasSubmittedSearch && searchResultCategories.length > 0 && (
          <div className="no-scrollbar flex items-center gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-1 overscroll-contain select-none">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setSearchCategoryFilter('')}
              className={`h-8 shrink-0 rounded-full px-3.5 text-xs font-bold ring-1 transition cursor-pointer ${
                !searchCategoryFilter
                  ? 'bg-[#eaf6ef] text-[#0f7a4f] ring-[#0f7a4f]'
                  : 'bg-white text-[#17231d] ring-[#17231d]/15 hover:ring-[#0f7a4f]'
              }`}
            >
              {t('Tất cả')} ({searchResults.length})
            </button>
            {searchResultCategories.map((cat) => {
              const count = searchResults.filter((p) => (p.category || '').trim() === cat).length;
              const isActive = searchCategoryFilter === cat;
              return (
                <button
                  key={cat}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setSearchCategoryFilter(isActive ? '' : cat)}
                  className={`h-8 shrink-0 rounded-full px-3.5 text-xs font-bold ring-1 transition cursor-pointer ${
                    isActive
                      ? 'bg-[#eaf6ef] text-[#0f7a4f] ring-[#0f7a4f]'
                      : 'bg-white text-[#17231d] ring-[#17231d]/15 hover:ring-[#0f7a4f]'
                  }`}
                >
                  {productName(cat)} ({count})
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_380px] gap-4 items-start min-w-0 w-full">
        <div ref={productGridWrapRef} className="space-y-3 min-w-0">
          <ProductGrid
            products={visibleProducts}
            itemsInCart={activeTab.items}
            favoriteIds={favoriteIds}
            onToggleFavorite={toggleFavorite}
            onAddProduct={handleAddProduct}
            onUpdateQty={handleUpdateQty}
            onUpdateNote={handleUpdateItemNote}
            searchQuery={hasSubmittedSearch ? searchQuery : selectedCategory}
            loading={hasSubmittedSearch ? searchLoading : catalogLoading}
          />
          {totalPages > 1 && (
            <nav aria-label="Phân trang" className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2.5 border-t border-[#17231d]/8 pt-4 sm:flex-row sm:justify-between">
              <p className="text-xs font-medium text-[#59665f]">
                {t('Trang')} <span className="font-bold text-[#0f7a4f]">{safePage}</span> / {totalPages} ({sourceProducts.length} {t('mặt hàng')})
              </p>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-1">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => goToPage(safePage - 1)}
                  disabled={safePage <= 1}
                  className="h-9 rounded-lg border border-[#17231d]/12 bg-white px-3 text-xs font-bold text-[#17231d] hover:border-[#0f7a4f] hover:text-[#0f7a4f] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
                >
                  {t('Trước')}
                </button>
                {pageNumbers.map((p, idx) =>
                  typeof p === 'string' ? (
                    <span key={`dots-${idx}`} className="px-1 text-xs text-[#8a9690]">...</span>
                  ) : (
                    <button
                      key={p}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => goToPage(p)}
                      aria-current={p === safePage ? 'page' : undefined}
                      className={`size-9 rounded-lg border text-xs transition ${
                        p === safePage
                          ? 'border-[#0f7a4f] bg-[#0f7a4f] font-bold text-white'
                          : 'border-[#17231d]/12 bg-white font-semibold text-[#17231d] hover:border-[#0f7a4f] hover:text-[#0f7a4f]'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {p}
                    </button>
                  )
                )}
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => goToPage(safePage + 1)}
                  disabled={safePage >= totalPages}
                  className="h-9 rounded-lg border border-[#17231d]/12 bg-white px-3 text-xs font-bold text-[#17231d] hover:border-[#0f7a4f] hover:text-[#0f7a4f] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
                >
                  {t('Sau')}
                </button>
              </div>
            </nav>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* CỘT PHẢI (Desktop): Sidebar Tóm tắt đơn & Gửi đơn */}
        <div className="hidden lg:block min-w-0">
          <CartSummarySidebar
            matchedHeight={sidebarHeight}
            session={session}
            earliestDate={cutoffInfo?.earliestDate}
            cutoffTimeStr={cutoffInfo?.cutoffTimeStr}
            activeTab={activeTab}
            onUpdateTab={updateActiveTab}
            onUpdateQty={handleUpdateQty}
            onUpdateNote={handleUpdateItemNote}
            onRemoveItem={handleRemoveItem}
            onAddUnlisted={handleAddUnlisted}
            onSubmitOrder={handleSubmitOrder}
            submitting={submitting}
            errorMessage={errorMessage}
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 5. Mobile Floating Cart Bar (khi có sản phẩm) */}
      <CartFloatingBar
        activeTab={activeTab}
        onOpenDrawer={() => setIsDrawerOpen(true)}
      />

      {/* 6. Mobile Slide-up Checkout Drawer */}
      <CartCheckoutDrawer
        isOpen={isDrawerOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsDrawerOpen(false)}
        session={session}
        earliestDate={cutoffInfo?.earliestDate}
        cutoffTimeStr={cutoffInfo?.cutoffTimeStr}
        activeTab={activeTab}
        onUpdateTab={updateActiveTab}
        onUpdateQty={handleUpdateQty}
        onUpdateNote={handleUpdateItemNote}
        onRemoveItem={handleRemoveItem}
        onAddUnlisted={handleAddUnlisted}
        onSubmitOrder={handleSubmitOrder}
        submitting={submitting}
        errorMessage={errorMessage}
      />

      {/* 7. Modal thông báo đặt hàng thành công */}
      <OrderSuccessModal
        order={successOrder}
        pendingVerification={session?.verificationStatus === 'pending'}
        onClose={clearSuccess}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

 import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
 import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';

+import { api, type StoreCategory, type Product } from '../lib/api';
 import { useAuth } from '../contexts/AuthContext';
 import { useLanguage } from '../contexts/LanguageContext';
 import { useStoreHome } from '../hooks/useStoreHome';
 import BannerCarousel from '../components/store/BannerCarousel';
 import CategoryRail from '../components/store/CategoryRail';
 import ProductRail from '../components/store/ProductRail';
 import StoreProductGrid from '../components/store/StoreProductGrid';
 
+function CategoryThumbnail({ category }: { category: StoreCategory }) {

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import {
  BadgePercent,
  ChevronRight,
  ClipboardList,
  FileSpreadsheet,
  Flame,
  LayoutGrid,
  Loader2,
  Package,
  RotateCcw,
  Sparkles,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { api, type StoreCategory, type Product } from '../lib/api';
import { useAuth } from '../contexts/AuthContext';
import { useLanguage } from '../contexts/LanguageContext';
import { useStoreHome } from '../hooks/useStoreHome';
import BannerCarousel from '../components/store/BannerCarousel';
import CategoryRail from '../components/store/CategoryRail';
import ProductRail from '../components/store/ProductRail';
import StoreProductGrid from '../components/store/StoreProductGrid';

function CategoryThumbnail({ category }: { category: StoreCategory }) {
  const [error, setError] = useState(false);
  return (
    <span className="flex size-7 shrink-0 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-[#eaf6ef] ring-1 ring-[#0f7a4f]/10">
      {category.imageUrl && !error ? (
        <img
          src={category.imageUrl}
          alt=""
          loading="lazy"
          onError={() => setError(true)}
          className="h-full w-full object-cover"
        />
      ) : (
        <Package size={16} className="text-[#0f7a4f]/60" />
      )}
    </span>
  );
}

function CategorySidebar({ categories }: { categories: StoreCategory[] }) {
  const { t } = useLanguage();
  return (
    <nav
      aria-label={t('Danh mục hàng hóa')}
      className="relative hidden min-h-[340px] rounded-2xl bg-white ring-1 ring-[#17231d]/6 lg:block"
    >
      <div className="no-scrollbar absolute inset-0 flex flex-col overflow-y-auto p-1.5">
        {categories.map((c) => (
          <Link
            key={c.slug}
            to={`/danh-muc/${c.slug}`}
            className="group flex items-center gap-2.5 rounded-xl px-2 py-1 text-[13.5px] font-semibold text-[#17231d] transition hover:bg-[#eaf6ef] hover:text-[#0b4f34]"
          >
            <CategoryThumbnail category={c} />
            <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate">{t(c.name)}</span>
            <ChevronRight
              size={16}
              className="shrink-0 text-[#8a9690] transition group-hover:translate-x-0.5 group-hover:text-[#0f7a4f]"
            />
          </Link>
        ))}
        <Link
          to="/danh-muc"
          className="mt-auto flex items-center gap-2.5 rounded-xl px-2 py-1 text-[13px] font-bold text-[#0f7a4f] hover:bg-[#eaf6ef]"
        >
          <span className="flex size-7 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[#0f7a4f]/10">
            <LayoutGrid size={15} />
          </span>
          {t('Xem tất cả danh mục')}
        </Link>
      </div>
    </nav>
  );
}

/** Danh mục không phải thực phẩm: không đưa vào lưới đề xuất. */
const NON_FOOD_SLUGS = new Set(['dung-cu-bep']);

function RailSkeleton() {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white p-3 ring-1 ring-[#17231d]/6 sm:p-4">
      <div className="mb-3 h-5 w-40 animate-pulse rounded-lg bg-[#eef2ee]" />
      <div className="flex gap-2 overflow-hidden">
        {Array.from({ length: 7 }).map((_, i) => (
          <div key={i} className="w-[42%] shrink-0 sm:w-[23%] md:w-[18.5%] lg:w-[15.5%] xl:w-[13.4%]">
            <div className="aspect-square animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-[#eef2ee]" />
            <div className="mt-2 h-4 animate-pulse rounded bg-[#eef2ee]" />
            <div className="mt-1.5 h-3 w-2/3 animate-pulse rounded bg-[#eef2ee]" />
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function StoreHomePage() {
  const { session } = useAuth();
  const { t } = useLanguage();
  const { data, error, loading } = useStoreHome();
  const [recommended, setRecommended] = useState<Product[]>([]);
  const [extra, setExtra] = useState<Product[]>([]);
  const [loadingMore, setLoadingMore] = useState(false);
  const [exhausted, setExhausted] = useState(false);
  // Lần lượt lấy thêm từng trang của mỗi nhóm thực phẩm, để lưới đề xuất đa dạng.
  const cursor = useRef({ index: 0, pages: new Map<string, number>(), done: new Set<string>() });

  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    setExtra([]);
    setExhausted(false);
    cursor.current = { index: 0, pages: new Map(), done: new Set() };
    api.storeRecommendations(controller.signal)
      .then((res) => setRecommended(res.products || []))
      .catch(() => undefined);
    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [session?.id]);

  // Hàng đã có ở các khối phía trên (khuyến mãi, bán chạy) không lặp lại trong lưới đề xuất.
  const suggestions = useMemo(() => {
    const seen = new Set((data?.collections || []).flatMap((c) => c.products.map((p) => p.id)));
    const out: Product[] = [];
    for (const product of [...recommended, ...extra]) {
      if (seen.has(product.id)) continue;
      seen.add(product.id);
      out.push(product);
    }
    return out;
  }, [data, recommended, extra]);

  const foodGroups = useMemo(
    () => (data?.categories || []).filter((c) => !NON_FOOD_SLUGS.has(c.slug) && c.productCategories?.length),
    [data],
  );

  const loadMore = useCallback(async () => {
    if (loadingMore || foodGroups.length === 0) return;
    setLoadingMore(true);
    try {
      const state = cursor.current;
      const batch: Product[] = [];
      // Mỗi lần bấm lấy trang kế tiếp của vài nhóm liền nhau, chỉ giữ hàng có ảnh.
      for (let tries = 0; tries < foodGroups.length && batch.length < 24; tries += 1) {
        const group = foodGroups[state.index % foodGroups.length];
        state.index += 1;
        if (state.done.has(group.slug)) continue;
        const page = state.pages.get(group.slug) || 0;
        const res = await api.products({ categories: group.productCategories, page });
        const items = res.products || [];
        state.pages.set(group.slug, page + 1);
        if (items.length < 24) state.done.add(group.slug);
        batch.push(...items.filter((p) => p.thumbUrl || p.imageUrl));
      }
      if (state.done.size >= foodGroups.length) setExhausted(true);
      setExtra((current) => [...current, ...batch]);
    } catch {
      // Lỗi mạng: khách bấm lại là được.
    } finally {
      setLoadingMore(false);
    }
  }, [foodGroups, loadingMore]);

  // Tự động tải thêm ở nền nếu số lượng gợi ý chưa đủ 21 món (để chia đều 7 cột x 3 hàng = 21 món)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (suggestions.length > 0 && suggestions.length < 21 && !exhausted && !loadingMore && foodGroups.length > 0) {
      loadMore();
    }
  }, [suggestions.length, exhausted, loadingMore, foodGroups.length, loadMore]);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3 sm:space-y-4">
      {/* Điện thoại: danh mục lên đầu để khách thấy gian hàng ngay; máy tính: banner trước. */}
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
        <div className="order-2 grid gap-3 lg:order-1 lg:grid-cols-[250px_minmax(0,1fr)]">
          {data && data.categories.length > 0 ? (
            <CategorySidebar categories={data.categories} />
          ) : (
            <div className="hidden animate-pulse rounded-2xl bg-white ring-1 ring-[#17231d]/6 lg:block" aria-hidden="true" />
          )}
          <BannerCarousel banners={data?.banners || []} />
        </div>

        {session ? (
          <div className="order-3 flex flex-col gap-2 lg:order-2 lg:flex-row lg:items-center lg:rounded-2xl lg:bg-white lg:p-2 lg:pl-4 lg:ring-1 lg:ring-[#17231d]/6">
            <div className="hidden min-w-0 flex-1 items-center gap-2.5 lg:flex">
              <span className="shrink-0 rounded-md bg-[#eaf6ef] px-2 py-0.5 text-[10.5px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#0f7a4f]">
                {t('BẾP ĐỐI TÁC TPS1')}
              </span>
              <span className="truncate text-sm font-black text-[#17231d]">
                {session.company || session.name}
              </span>
              <span className="hidden truncate text-xs text-[#59665f] xl:inline">
                {'· '}
                {t('Giá hiển thị đã áp dụng theo hợp đồng của bếp.')}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 lg:flex lg:shrink-0">
              {[
                { to: '/dat-hang', icon: RotateCcw, label: 'Đặt nhanh', desktop: 'Đặt nhanh & Đặt lại đơn' },
                { to: '/gio-hang?excel=1', icon: FileSpreadsheet, label: 'Đặt từ Excel', desktop: 'Đặt hàng từ file Excel' },
                { to: '/don-hang', icon: ClipboardList, label: 'Đơn hàng', desktop: 'Đơn hàng' },
              ].map(({ to, icon: Icon, label, desktop }, idx) => (
                <Link
                  key={to}
                  to={to}
                  className={`flex min-h-11 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl px-2 py-2.5 text-center text-[12px] font-bold whitespace-nowrap active:scale-[0.98] sm:text-[13px] lg:min-h-10 lg:px-4 ${
                    idx === 0
                      ? 'bg-white text-[#0b4f34] ring-1 ring-[#17231d]/6 lg:bg-[#0f7a4f] lg:text-white lg:ring-0 lg:hover:bg-[#0b4f34]'
                      : 'bg-white text-[#0b4f34] ring-1 ring-[#17231d]/6 lg:ring-[#0f7a4f]/25 lg:hover:bg-[#eaf6ef]'
                  }`}
                >
                  <Icon
                    size={16}
                    className={`shrink-0 ${idx === 0 ? 'text-[#0f7a4f] lg:text-white' : 'text-[#0f7a4f]'}`}
                  />
                  <span className="lg:hidden">{t(label)}</span>
                  <span className="hidden lg:inline">{t(desktop)}</span>
                </Link>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="order-3 hidden items-center gap-3 rounded-2xl bg-linear-to-r from-[#fef5ee] to-white p-2 pl-4 ring-1 ring-[#e0742f]/15 lg:order-2 lg:flex">
            <p className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-sm text-[#59665f]">
              <span className="font-black text-[#17231d]">{t('Giá riêng cho bếp của quý khách')}</span>
              {' · '}
              {t('Đăng ký tài khoản miễn phí để xem đúng giá hợp đồng, gửi đơn bằng file Excel và biết đơn đang giao tới đâu.')}
            </p>
            <Link
              to="/dang-ky"
              className="flex min-h-10 shrink-0 items-center rounded-xl bg-[#e0742f] px-4 text-sm font-bold text-white hover:bg-[#c4601f]"
            >
              {t('Đăng ký tài khoản')}
            </Link>
            <Link
              to="/dang-nhap"
              className="flex min-h-10 shrink-0 items-center rounded-xl border border-[#17231d]/12 bg-white px-4 text-sm font-bold text-[#17231d] hover:bg-[#f5f7f3]"
            >
              {t('Đăng nhập')}
            </Link>
          </div>
        )}

        {data && data.categories.length > 0 && (
          <section id="store-home-catalog" className="order-1 rounded-2xl bg-white px-3 py-3 ring-1 ring-[#17231d]/6 sm:p-4 lg:hidden">
            <div className="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between">
              <h2 className="text-base font-black text-[#17231d] sm:text-lg">{t('Danh mục hàng hóa')}</h2>
            </div>
            <CategoryRail categories={data.categories} />
          </section>
        )}
        {!data && loading && (
          <div className="order-1 flex gap-3 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl bg-white px-3 py-3 ring-1 ring-[#17231d]/6 lg:hidden" aria-hidden="true">
            {Array.from({ length: 5 }).map((_, i) => (
              <div key={i} className="flex w-19.5 shrink-0 flex-col items-center gap-2">
                <div className="size-18 animate-pulse rounded-2xl bg-[#eef2ee]" />
                <div className="h-3 w-14 animate-pulse rounded bg-[#eef2ee]" />
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {loading && <RailSkeleton />}
      {error && !data && (
        <p className="rounded-2xl bg-amber-50 p-4 text-sm text-amber-900">{t('Chưa tải được cửa hàng. Quý khách vui lòng kiểm tra kết nối mạng và thử lại.')}</p>
      )}

      {data?.collections.map((collection) => (
        <ProductRail
          key={collection.slug}
          title={collection.title}
          subtitle={collection.subtitle}
          products={collection.products}
          icon={collection.source === 'best_sellers'
            ? <Flame size={18} className="text-[#e0742f]" />
            : collection.slug === 'khuyen-mai'
              ? <BadgePercent size={18} className="text-[#d6453d]" />
              : collection.source === 'new_arrivals'
                ? <Sparkles size={18} className="text-[#0f7a4f]" />
                : undefined}
        />
      ))}

      {suggestions.length > 0 && (
        <section className="rounded-2xl bg-white p-3 ring-1 ring-[#17231d]/6 sm:p-4">
          <div className="mb-2.5">
            <h2 className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-base font-black text-[#17231d] sm:text-lg">
              <Sparkles size={18} className="text-[#0f7a4f]" />
              {t(session ? 'Đề xuất cho bếp của quý khách' : 'Đề xuất cho quý khách')}
            </h2>
            <p className="hidden text-xs text-[#59665f] sm:block">
              {t(session ? 'Những món bếp của quý khách thường đặt và các mặt hàng cùng nhóm' : 'Những mặt hàng được nhiều bếp ăn lựa chọn')}
            </p>
          </div>
          <StoreProductGrid products={suggestions} hasMore={!exhausted && foodGroups.length > 0} />
          {!exhausted && foodGroups.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-3 flex justify-center">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={loadMore}
                disabled={loadingMore}
                className="inline-flex h-10 items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-[#0f7a4f]/30 bg-white px-6 text-sm font-bold text-[#0f7a4f] hover:bg-[#eaf6ef] disabled:opacity-60"
              >
                {loadingMore && <Loader2 size={16} className="animate-spin" />}
                {t('Xem thêm sản phẩm')}
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </section>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
